### [1] [Self-calibrating thermal interferometry of vortex parity in a two-dimensional chiral superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19343)
*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出在二维手性超导体（chiral superconductor）内部的可重构畴壁（reconfigurable domain wall）可以同时实现两个功能：开放时作为弹道通道，通过量子化热导计数马约拉纳模（Majorana modes）；闭合时作为Fabry–Pérot谐振腔，通过谱移动探测包围涡旋的宇称变化。作者推导了精确透射率、弹性热全计数统计和一则定理，并给出区分外部涡旋假阳性与真实宇称信号的判定方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性超导体边界上承载的手性马约拉纳模数目由整数决定，但该整数从未与单个物体上的局域宇称观测量（local parity observable）一同被测量过。近邻化一维导线虽能快速读取费米子宇称，却需通过独立协议诊断体拓扑，缺乏单器件同时完成两种功能的方案。

Method: 提出在固有二维手性超导体中利用Chern数相反区域之间的可重构畴壁：开路状态下作为弹道通道，以量子化热导（quantized thermal conductance）计数马约拉纳模；闭路状态下作为Fabry–Pérot谐振腔，其谱被内包涡旋宇称变化移动半个能级间距，从而给出二级热导。推导精确透射、弹性热全计数统计（full counting statistics）及线性响应热散射定理。

Result: 线性响应热散射可分辨涡旋宇称但无法分辨远分离核心的融合道（fusion channel）。外部涡旋与畴壁杂化会产生固有假阳性（false positive），可通过温度、几何构型及有限偏压均值–噪声测试予以区分。菱方石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）参数下，亚微米回路在毫开尔文温度进入可分辨区间，该区域内畴壁重配置与噪声测温均已实现。

Conclusion: 可重构畴壁器件有望在同一物理系统上同时完成马约拉纳模计数与局域宇称探测，为手性超导体的拓扑表征提供统一实验平台，且该方案在现有菱方石墨烯实验条件下具备可行性。

Abstract: A chiral superconductor carries chiral Majorana modes along its boundary, and the integer that counts them fixes everything that follows, yet that integer has never been measured together with a local parity observable on one object. Proximitized one-dimensional wires read fermion parity rapidly but diagnose bulk topology through a separate protocol. Here we show that a reconfigurable domain wall between regions of opposite Chern number in an intrinsic two-dimensional chiral superconductor performs both functions. Opened to its contacts the wall is a ballistic channel whose quantized thermal conductance counts its Majorana modes; closed, the same wall is a Fabry--Pérot resonator whose spectrum shifts by half a level spacing when the parity of the enclosed vortices changes, giving a two-level heat conductance. We derive the exact transmission, the elastic heat full counting statistics, and a theorem showing that linear-response heat scattering of a fixed quadratic problem resolves vortex parity but not the fusion channel of well-separated cores. An outside vortex hybridized with the wall is an intrinsic false positive; temperature, geometry and a finite-bias mean--noise test separates it. Rhombohedral-graphene parameters place submicron loops in the resolved regime at millikelvin temperatures, where chiral-domain reconfiguration and noise thermometry are both established.

</details>


### [2] [Free Energy of Non-uniform Disordered Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19573)
*Matthias Eschrig,Anton B. Vorontsov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Luttinger-Ward自由能泛函，将其应用于含有任意散射机制（包括杂质无序和界面）的无序超导体和超流体，并在自洽t-矩阵近似下处理无序效应，且通过复能量平面中的广义积分统一了推迟/超前传播子与Matsubara格林函数的表述方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Luttinger-Ward自由能泛函未涵盖无序超导体和超流体，特别是同时包含杂质散射与界面散射的一般情形，需要发展统一框架来描述此类系统的热力学性质。

Method: 在自洽t-矩阵近似（self-consistent t-matrix approximation）下将无序纳入自由能泛函，允许任意杂质散射强度。界面与杂质散射的自能仅通过自洽确定的费米子传播子隐式地进入泛函。自由能泛函用复能量平面中的广义积分表述，通过适当选择积分路径可分别退化为推迟/超前传播子形式或Matsubara格林函数（Matsubara Green's function）形式。此外还给出了基于准经典传播子（quasiclassical propagator）的特别有用的表述。

Result: 该泛函可应用于空间非均匀和混合系统、三重态及其他非常规凝聚体、强关联费米液体等广泛场景。基于准经典传播子的表述被进一步应用于外斯Zeeman磁场下的非常规非均匀单重态和三重态超导体及超流体。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩展Luttinger-Ward自由能泛函为无序超导体和超流体提供了统一的、自洽的热力学描述框架，且通过准经典传播子形式可直接用于研究外磁场下的非常规超导与超流系统。

Abstract: We extend the Luttinger-Ward free energy functional to disordered superconductors and superfluids with arbitrary scattering mechanisms, including both impurity disorder and the presence of interfaces. The disorder is taken into account within the self-consistent $t$-matrix approximation, thus allowing for arbitrary impurity scattering strengths. It is shown that both the interface and the impurity scattering self-energy appear in the functional only implicitly, through self-consistently determined fermionic propagators. The free energy functional is formulated in terms of a generalized integral in the complex energy plane, which encompasses formulations both in terms of retarded/advanced propagators and in terms of Matsubara Green's functions by appropriately choosing the integration path. It can be applied, e.g., to spatially non-uniform and hybrid systems, triplet and other unconventional condensates, and strongly-correlated Fermi liquids. A particularly useful formulation in terms of the quasiclassical propagators is applied to unconventional non-uniform singlet and triplet superconductors and superfluids in an external Zeeman magnetic field.

</details>


### [3] [Internal cyclotron probe of the vertical polarizability of a surface-state electron on helium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19712)
*Kirill Shulga*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究了液氦上方表面态电子在倾斜磁场中的行为。倾斜磁场使回旋能级谱（cyclotron ladder）与同一电子的纵向里德伯型子带（Rydberg-like subbands）耦合，形成自耦合朗道子带哈密顿量（self-coupled Landau-subband Hamiltonian）。理论表明回旋共振可直接用于探测纵向梯级的动态极化率（dynamical polarizability）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索液氦上表面态电子在倾斜磁场中的新物理效应，揭示倾斜磁场诱导的回旋梯级与纵向子带之间的耦合机制，并寻找探测该耦合的可行实验途径。

Method: 将倾斜磁场中的表面态电子问题映射为自耦合朗道子带哈密顿量；在远离简并（far-detuned）区间采用标准二阶微扰论（second-order perturbation theory）推导回旋谱线态依赖移位；通过精确对角化（exact diagonalization）验证理论并评估微波窗口内的可观测效应。

Result: 推导出回旋共振态依赖移位的解析表达式，证明该移位可直接反映纵向梯级的动态极化率；精确对角化证实存在较宽的微波窗口，在该窗口内能观测到显著的态依赖回旋移位，同时系统仍保持色散性（dispersive）。

Conclusion: 该理论本质上属于多能级（multilevel）理论，表明液氦表面态电子系统可作为研究回旋梯级与纵向子带耦合的平台，并为量子探测提供新的手段。

Abstract: We study a surface-state electron on liquid helium in a tilted magnetic field. The tilt couples the cyclotron ladder to the vertical Rydberg-like subbands of the same electron and thereby realizes a self-coupled Landau-subband Hamiltonian. In the far-detuned regime, the cyclotron line acquires a state-dependent shift described by standard second-order perturbation theory. The resulting expression identifies the cyclotron resonance as a direct probe of the dynamical polarizability of the vertical ladder. The theory is intrinsically multilevel. Exact diagonalization confirms a broad microwave window in which sizable state-dependent cyclotron shifts are expected while the system remains dispersive.

</details>


### [4] [Josephson transport in YBa${}_{2}$Cu${}_{3}$O${}_{7}$ weak links created by focused-helium-ion-beam irradiation: Analysis based on diffusive-SNS-junction model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20109)
*Tetsuro Misawa,Shigeyuki Ishida,Hiroshi Eisaki,Yukinori Morita,Shinichi Ogawa,Chiharu Urano*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文基于氦离子显微镜（helium ion microscope, HIM）辐照制备的YBCO弱连接（weak links）输运特性进行了系统研究，提出用含扩散金属中间层的SNS结理论统一描述其行为。研究发现临界电流Ic和IcRn乘积的温度依赖符合扩散型SNS结模型，但剂量依赖不能仅用Thouless能量ET解释，还需考虑界面透明度和态密度降低导致有效导电通道减少，该解释与Rn随剂量指数增加的观测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚焦氦离子束辐照制备YBCO弱连接是实现高温超导约瑟夫森结器件的可行途径，但目前仅有经验性的剂量-特性关系，其底层输运机制尚不明确，缺乏统一的理论框架来指导器件设计。

Method: 利用氦离子显微镜（HIM）辐照制备YBCO弱连接，系统测量其在不同辐照剂量下的输运性质，包括临界电流Ic和电阻Rn，并基于含扩散金属中间层的SNS结理论建立统一的现象学描述模型。

Result: 在宽温度范围内，Ic的温度依赖和IcRn乘积均可由扩散型SNS结模型良好描述；但剂量依赖显示仅靠有效Thouless能量ET的变化无法解释实验数据，需引入界面透明度降低和态密度下降导致有效导电通道数减少的机制，该解释与Rn随剂量指数增长的观测一致。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个基于扩散输运（diffusion-based）的框架用于理解氦离子辐照YBCO弱连接中的约瑟夫森输运机制，并为此类器件的结设计与优化提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Fabrication of YBCO weak links by focused helium ion beam irradiation is a promising approach for realizing high-temperature superconducting Josephson junction devices. Although empirical dose-characteristic relationships have been established, the underlying transport mechanisms remain unclear. In this study, we perform a detailed investigation of the transport properties of YBCO weak links fabricated using a helium ion microscope (HIM) and provide a unified phenomenological description of the observed behavior based on the theory of SNS junctions with a diffusive metallic interlayer. We demonstrate that the temperature dependence of the critical current $I_{\mathrm{c}}$ and the $I_{\mathrm{c}}R_{\mathrm{n}}$ product are well described by diffusive SNS junction models over a wide temperature range. Analyses show that the observed dose dependences of $I_{\mathrm{c}}$ and $I_{\mathrm{c}}R_{\mathrm{n}}$ cannot be explained solely by variations in the effective Thouless energy $E_{\mathrm{T}}$. The discrepancy suggests reduced interface transparency and a reduction in the density of states, leading to a decrease in the effective number of conducting channels contributing to transport. This interpretation is also consistent with the observed exponential increase in $R_{\mathrm{n}}$ with irradiation dose. These results provide a diffusion-based framework for understanding Josephson transport and guiding junction design in helium-ion-irradiated YBCO weak links.

</details>


### [5] [Photogalvanic transport of nonreciprocal Cooper-pair fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20166)
*Joaquim Telles de Miranda,Alex Levchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文建立了二维非中心对称超导体在转变温度以上涨落区（fluctuation regime）的非线性光学与输运响应理论，涵盖光电流效应（photogalvanic effect）、二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation）和光伏霍尔效应（photovoltaic Hall effect）。涨落区预形成的库珀对（Cooper pair）通过热力学和动力学Lifshitz不变量（Lifshitz invariants）两种途径引入非互易性，作者推导了适用于任意驱动频率和非互易扰动线性阶的广义超导率（paraconductivity）主公式，并揭示了圆偏振光对Aslamazov-Larkin通道和Maki-Thompson通道的判别性，以及点群对称性对非线性电流矢量结构的支配作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非中心对称超导体中，涨落区预形成的库珀对可强烈增强非线性光学和输运响应，但此前缺乏统一的涨落理论框架来描述这些现象。理解这些响应的微观机制、区分不同通道的贡献，对于实验探测和材料设计具有重要意义。

Method: 基于含时Ginzburg-Landau描述（time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau description），通过热力学Lifshitz不变量（编码不对称对谱）和动力学Lifshitz不变量（编码不对称对弛豫，并由涨落耗散定理锁定到Langevin噪声）两种途径引入非互易性。推导了Aslamazov-Larkin和Maki-Thompson通道非线性电流的广义主公式，简化为闭合形式的无量纲函数，并应用于Rashba型（C3v）和Ising型（D3h）超导体进行对称性分析。

Result: 发现圆偏振光可清晰区分两类机制：对互易无动量结构噪声，Aslamazov-Larkin通道对偏振不敏感且其圆光电流效应对任意对谱均为零，而Maki-Thompson通道和非互易噪声支持螺旋度奇（helicity-odd）整流电流，包括沿应变轴横向流动的涨落光伏霍尔电流。应用Rashba型（C3v）和Ising型（D3h）超导体的分析表明，点群对称性支配非线性电流允许的矢量结构，极化分析结合频率和退相位依赖性可在实验中分离各通道贡献。

Conclusion: 该理论为理解非中心对称超导体涨落区的非线性响应提供了统一框架，圆偏振光作为灵敏探针可区分Aslamazov-Larkin与Maki-Thompson通道，结合对称性分析和实验依赖关系可实现各通道的独立表征。

Abstract: We develop a theory of the nonlinear optical and transport responses of two-dimensional noncentrosymmetric superconductors in the fluctuation regime above the transition temperature, encompassing the photogalvanic effect, second-harmonic generation, and the photovoltaic Hall effect. In the vicinity of the transition these responses are strongly enhanced by preformed Cooper pairs, whose nonreciprocity enters the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau description in two physically distinct ways: through thermodynamic Lifshitz invariants, which encode an asymmetric pair spectrum, and through kinetic Lifshitz invariants, which encode an asymmetric pair relaxation and are locked to the Langevin noise by the fluctuation-dissipation theorem. We derive generalized master formulas for the paraconductivity (Aslamazov-Larkin) and the quantum-interference (Maki-Thompson) channels of the nonlinear current, valid at arbitrary drive frequency and to linear order in the nonreciprocal perturbations, and reduce them to closed-form dimensionless functions. Circular polarization discriminates sharply between the mechanisms: for reciprocal momentum-structureless noise the Aslamazov-Larkin channel is polarization insensitive and its circular photogalvanic response vanishes for any pair spectrum, whereas the Maki-Thompson channel and the nonreciprocal noise support helicity-odd rectified currents, including a fluctuation photovoltaic Hall current flowing transverse to the strain axis. Applications to Rashba-type ($C_{3v}$) and Ising-type ($D_{3h}$) superconductors demonstrate how the point-group symmetry dictates the allowed vector structures of the nonlinear currents, and how polarization analysis together with the frequency and dephasing dependences can be used to separate the individual channels experimentally.

</details>


### [6] [Strange Metal Hall Effect in Underdoped BaFe$_2$(As$_{1-x}$P$_x$)$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20325)
*Augusto Ghiotto,Darian Hall,Yuanqi Lyu,Kohtaro Yamakawa,Sophie Rodehutskors,Corina Dunn,Philip J. W. Moll,John Singleton,Nikola Maksimovic,James G. Analytis*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过研究奇异金属超导体BaFe₂(As₁₋ₓPₓ)₂的霍尔效应，发现一个与掺杂无关的霍尔贡献存在于假定量子临界点之上的扇形区域内，该"奇异金属霍尔"贡献与临界涨落相关，并与T线性电阻率和非常规超导性的出现相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异金属（strange metal）反常的输运性质暗示准粒子图像（quasiparticle picture）的失效，其理解仍是物理学中最为棘手的问题之一，需要新的实验证据来揭示其微观机制。

Method: 对奇异金属超导体BaFe₂(As₁₋ₓPₓ)₂进行电学霍尔效应（Hall effect）的测量研究，分析不同掺杂浓度下霍尔响应的行为，并比较其与反铁磁霍尔响应及费米面重构效应的差异。

Result: 发现在一个假定量子临界点（quantum critical point）之上的扇形区域内，存在一个与掺杂无关的霍尔效应贡献；该贡献复现了反铁磁霍尔响应的许多特性，但具有普遍的（universal）性质，使其区别于费米面重构（Fermi surface reconstruction）的效应。

Conclusion: 该"奇异金属霍尔"贡献与临界涨落（critical fluctuations）的存在相吻合，将其与T线性电阻率和非常规超导电性的观测联系起来，表明奇异金属的异常输运可能源于量子临界涨落而非费米面重构。

Abstract: The unusual transport properties of strange metals point to the breakdown of the quasiparticle picture, the understanding of which remains one of the most vexing problems in physics. Here, we report investigations of the electrical Hall effect of the strange metal superconductor BaFe$_2$(As$_{1-x}$P$_x$)$_2$. We show that a doping-independent contribution to the Hall effect exists within a fan shaped region above a putative quantum critical point. This `strange metal Hall' contribution echoes many of the properties of the antiferromangetic Hall response, but attains universal properties that distinguish it from the effects of Fermi surface reconstruction. This is consistent with an underlying origin connected to the presence of critical fluctuations, tying it to observations of $T$-linear resistivity and the appearance of unconventional superconductivity.

</details>

### [7] [Resonant state expansion for acoustic resonators. Part I. Eigenvalue problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19979)
*Egor Domoratskii,Vladimir Igoshin,Nikolay Solodovchenko,Mingzhao Song,Yong Li,Mihail Petrov,Andrey Bogdanov*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种基于格林函数（Green function）的声学共振态展开（resonant-state expansion, RSE）形式体系，以解析可解的圆柱参考系统为基函数，推导了密度和可压缩性的均匀、径向及扇形扰动的显式微扰矩阵元，并通过精确解析解和有限元仿真验证了其定量准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共振态展开（RSE）是开放谐振系统微扰分析的有力模态框架，在电磁学中已发展成熟，但声学中尚缺乏同等的系统性表述，因此需要建立对应的声学RSE形式体系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 方法给出$T_{\rm pc}^T=160.2(4)(3)$ MeV和$T_{\rm pc}^C=158.1(5)(3)$ MeV，且$T_{\rm pc}^\chi$与二者及此前基于泰勒展开的格点QCD结果一致。标度关系给出的$\kappa_2^B$估计相互一致；直接拟合$\mu_B$依赖性的曲率估计统计上一致但约束明显较弱。

Abstract: We present the first application of the unbiased exponential resummation method to the determination of the QCD pseudocritical temperature $T_{\rm pc}$ at vanishing baryon chemical potential $μ_B$. We also show that the pseudocritical temperature, $T_{\rm pc}^χ$ obtained from the peak of the fourth-order baryon number susceptibility $χ_4^B$ is consistent with these determinations and with previous lattice-QCD results based on Taylor expansions \cite{Bollweg:2022Pade}. Method: 实验采用均匀平面原子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，其中包含两个相干耦合的相互作用组分（自旋态），量子场描述其自旋自由度。在相互作用强于相干耦合的区间内，系统等效于（有质量相对论性）正弦-戈登场。通过对场的成像来直接观察不同长度尺度的同时性涨落。

Result: 成像结果显示场中存在同时发生于不同长度尺度的涨落，其尺度依赖的振幅与真空态的理论预测一致。实验在正弦-戈登极限下成功直接观测到了真空涨落。

Abstract: Heisenberg uncertainties lead to inevitable fluctuations in the measurement outcomes for quantum-mechanical observables. For quantum fields, these uncertainties result in random spatial structures in snapshots of a field, even when the field is in its ground (vacuum) state. Such `vacuum fluctuations' are at the heart of a wide range of phenomena, from spontaneous decay processes to the Casimir force and Hawking radiation. Their existence is a key manifestation of the quantumness of the physical world, but usually it is only their consequences that are directly observed. Here, we directly observe spatial vacuum fluctuations of a bosonic quantum field. Our experiments are based on a homogeneous planar atomic Bose--Einstein condensate. The condensate comprises two coherently coupled interacting components (spin states), and the quantum field describes its spin degrees of freedom. In the regime where the interactions dominate over the coherent coupling, our system emulates a (massive relativistic) sine-Gordon field. Images of the field reveal simultaneous fluctuations on different length scales, with scale-dependent amplitudes consistent with theoretical predictions for a vacuum state. Observing such fluctuations in the sine-Gordon limit opens many possibilities for laboratory simulations of relativistic fields in regimes that are presently not theoretically tractable.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dicke模型是集体光-物质物理和超辐射相变（superradiant phase transition）的范例模型，但由于全对耦合下关联时间的缓慢发散以及光子损耗驱动的交叉到不同耗散普适类，在实验可及的介观尺寸下提取临界指数极具挑战性。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了统一框架，用于解析闭合和开放量子系统中的静态与动力学临界标度，并具有对长程相互作用介观系统的更广泛适用性。

Abstract: The Dicke model is a paradigmatic setting for collective light-matter physics and the superradiant phase transition. Yet extracting the critical exponents is challenging at experimentally accessible mesoscopic sizes, due to the slow divergence of the correlation time under all-to-all coupling and a photon-loss-driven crossover to a distinct dissipative universality class. Here, we perform a large-$N$ analysis that identifies distinct coherent and dissipative fixed points for the closed and open Dicke models. We then develop a unified mesoscopic scaling framework that incorporates the leading irrelevant correction and, going beyond static and spectral probes, brings ramping dynamics under the same scaling description. It recovers the corresponding exponents, verifies Kibble-Zurek scaling, and clarifies how finite size, dissipation, and speed compete in the ramping dynamics. Our work thus establishes a unified framework for resolving static and dynamical critical scaling in closed and open quantum systems, with broader applicability to mesoscopic systems with long-range interactions.

The results presented here validate the structural complexity as an efficient and reliable tool for analyzing the outputs of SU(N) quantum gas microscopes, offering a theory-free property, immediately accessible to experiments.

Here we experimentally form competing triangular and stripe density-modulated states in a quantum gas of highly magnetic atoms confined in a surfboard-shaped trap by tuning contact interaction strength and dipole orientation. We define a structural order parameter and study its statistical behavior. Thereby, we identify both the triangular and stripe phases and the transition between them, the associated critical behavior being marked by enhanced non-Gaussian fluctuations. Furthermore, we observe each spatial structure in both the phase-coherent supersolid regime and the phase-incoherent insulating one, near and far from the unmodulated-to-modulated transition, respectively. Abstract: Conventional wisdom says that neutrino oscillations measure only mass-squared differences and not the absolute neutrino mass scale. The net jet baryon number, $\langle B_{\rm flow}\rangle_f\simeq \frac{r_{qq}}{1+r_{qq}}$, directly probes the relative probability of a diquark--antidiquark vacuum excitation, with $r_{qq}$ the diquark-to-quark production ratio. Likewise, the strange-to-light pair-production ratio, defined by $u\bar u:d\bar d:s\bar s=1:1:r_s$, is encoded in the measured jet strangeness $\langle S_{\rm flow}\rangle_f \simeq S_f+\frac{3r_s}{2+r_s} \left(\frac{1}{3}- \langle B_{\rm flow}\rangle_f\right)$, predicting a nonzero mean net strangeness even in $u$- and $d$-initiated jets.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙超弦(cosmic superstrings)是早期宇宙随机引力波(stochastic gravitational waves)的重要源，其现象学受弦张力(弦张力) Gμ 和交错概率(intercommutation probability) P 控制。需要检验LISA是否能够重建这些参数并在噪声和天体物理前景存在的情况下区分不同超弦信号模型。

Result: 发现减小的交错概率普遍产生强的Gμ–P相关性，因为数据通常约束的是类振幅组合。但当尖点–扭结谱差异落入LISA频带且信号足够强时，模型II可以被偏好且后验能保留真实的谱形信息。在乐观前景场景下，使用缩减的tanh银河前景模板计算贝叶斯因子，发现当前景形状被约束时模型判别能力得到改善。

Abstract: Cosmic superstrings are well-motivated early-Universe sources of stochastic gravitational waves, with phenomenology controlled by the string tension $Gμ$ and the intercommutation probability $P$. We study whether LISA can reconstruct these parameters and distinguish different superstring signal models in the presence of instrumental noise and astrophysical foregrounds. We consider two phenomenological models. In Model I, reduced intercommutation acts only as an amplitude enhancement of a cusp-dominated spectrum, $Ω_{\rm SS}^{\rm I}=P^{-β}Ω_{\rm cusp}$. In Model II, the signal is a cusp--kink mixture, $Ω_{\rm SS}^{\rm II} =P^{-β}[p_cΩ_{\rm cusp}+(1-p_c)Ω_{\rm kink}]$, so that $P$ controls both amplitude and spectral shape. Using simulated LISA data, we perform Bayesian inference with noise uncertainties, unresolved extragalactic compact-binary backgrounds, and a flexible Galactic double-white-dwarf foreground. We compare the models using Bayesian evidences and map the $(Gμ,P)$ posterior geometry with marginalized widths, correlations, covariance anisotropy, principal eigenvalues, posterior area, and bias. We find that reduced intercommutation generically produces strong $Gμ$--$P$ correlations, because the data often constrain an amplitude-like combination. However, when the cusp--kink spectral difference lies in the LISA band and the signal is sufficiently loud, Model II can be favored and the posterior can retain genuine shape information. As an optimistic foreground scenario, we also compute the Bayes factor using a reduced tanh Galactic foreground template, finding improved model discrimination when the foreground shape is constrained.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the specific heat and isentropic bulk modulus of finite-temperature pure SU(3) gauge matter within a lattice-data-driven phenomenological framework. The equation of state is formulated in terms of a temperature-dependent effective gluon mass constrained { by lattice QCD pressure data as input, allowing the pressure}, trace anomaly, gluon number density, energy per thermally active gluonic mode, and derivative-sensitive response functions to be derived in a thermodynamically consistent manner. The resulting pressure and trace anomaly reproduce the characteristic lattice behavior across the deconfinement region, while the effective gluonic degrees of freedom increase rapidly above $T_c$. The normalized specific heat $C_V/T^3$ develops a pronounced enhancement in the vicinity of $T_c$, reflecting the rapid temperature variation of the energy density across the deconfinement region. The isentropic bulk modulus $K_S/T^4$ also rises sharply across the transition region, indicating a substantial stiffening of the equation of state. At high temperatures, both response functions gradually approach values close to their massless conformal Stefan--Boltzmann reference values, with $\left(C_V/T^3\right)_{\rm SB}=32π^2/15\simeq 21.06$ and $\left(K_S/T^4\right)_{\rm SB}=32π^2/135\simeq 2.34$. These findings indicate that the specific heat and isentropic bulk modulus provide complementary constraints on the temperature evolution of nonconformal dynamics in pure SU(3) gauge matter.

Result: 研究表明，残余振荡源于FRG流中出现的负扩散系数，且这一负扩散是QDM模型的固有特征而非数值伪影。所提出的超扩散正则化方案能够有效消除有效势导数的强振荡，并在QDM相图研究中展现了良好效果。

Abstract: We investigate the Quark-Diquark Model (QDM) with the Functional Renormalization Group (FRG) in the Local Potential Approximation. In a recent work [arXiv:2510.01066 [hep-ph]], problems were reported in applying this method at low temperatures and large quark chemical potentials, which were attributed to numerical artifacts. In this work, we trace the origin of these problems to the occurrence of a negative diffusion coefficient during the FRG flow of the derivative of the effective potential, leading to strong oscillations of the latter quantity. We show that negative diffusion is a model feature and not a numerical artifact. We propose a regularization scheme which introduces a hyperdiffusion term to remove these oscillations and demonstrate its effectiveness for studies of the phase diagram of the QDM. Regularizing the negative diffusion in the FRG flow is particularly important to reliably study phenomena such as color superconductivity and inhomogeneous phases, which might emerge in the high-density, low-temperature region of the QCD phase diagram.

Method: 方案结合静电场（static electric field）与微波场（microwave field）产生强、可调的各向异性相互作用，对分子进行碰撞屏蔽（collisional shielding）；通过验证该屏蔽可消除所有长程束缚态以阻止三体重组，并适用于包括实验上限的线偏振在内的各种微波椭偏度（ellipticity）。随后施加附加电场梯度，诱导强相互作用分子受控地逐个溢出光镊，直至仅剩一个分子。

Conclusion: 电场辅助的碰撞屏蔽被证明是制备高填充率极性分子光镊阵列的有效工具，为大规模极性分子量子平台提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We propose the high-fidelity preparation of single bosonic molecules in optical tweezers starting from small tweezer-trapped molecular ensembles. Our scheme combines a static electric field and a microwave field to generate strong, tunable, anisotropic interactions that shield the molecules against two-body collisional loss. We show that this shielding eliminates all long-range bound states, preventing three-body recombination. This elimination persists for all microwave ellipticities, including the experimentally practical limit of linear polarization. Application of an additional electric field gradient can be used to induce controlled spilling of strongly interacting molecules out of the trap until one remains. With realistic experimental parameters, we estimate that single tweezer-trapped NaCs molecules can be isolated from a pair with fidelities exceeding 99\%, and $> 95\%$ per site across an array. These results establish collisional shielding with electric fields as an effective tool for preparing highly-filled tweezer arrays of polar molecules.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该结果将连通常数与黄金分割比的下界关系从立方图推广到了更一般的Grigorchuk群的凯莱图族，验证了先前猜想的自然扩展方向。

Abstract: The connective constant $μ(G)$ of a graph $G$ is the exponential growth rate of the number of self-avoiding walks starting at a given vertex. We prove upper and lower bounds for the connective constants of Cayley graphs $G_ω$ of a general Grigorchuk group encoded by a sequence $ω\in\{0,1,2\}^{\Bbb N}$. In particular, $μ(G_ω) > φ$ for any such Cayley graph (subject to a simple condition on $ω$), where $φ:= \frac12(1+\sqrt 5)$ is the golden mean. This extends earlier work of the author and Zhongyang Li in "Cubic graphs and the golden mean'', Discrete Math. 343 (2020), article 111638, where it was conjectured that $μ(G)\geφ$ for all infinite, vertex-transitive, cubic graphs. The current work includes an analysis of the proportions of appearances of given label-sequences in the orbital Schreier graphs of general Grigorchuk groups.

Abstract: Plane hypermaps are plane maps endowed with a proper coloration of their inner faces in black or white. We consider the problem of enumerating plane hypermaps with prescribed face degrees and a $k$-alternating boundary condition: by this we mean the colors of inner faces incident to the outer face alternates at most $2k$ times when turning around the hypermap. The present paper deals with the cases $k=1,2$, the general case being left to the forthcoming part II. Our approach relies on the so-called slice decomposition and uses crucially the notion of accessibility, which exploits the canonical orientation of hypermaps and the marking variable $t$ associated with vertices, to enumerate pointed hypermaps by decomposing them according to the set of all vertices that can access to the marked vertex. This process enables us to express the generating functions of hypermaps with mixed boundaries in terms of the generating functions of hypermap slices and to recover, in a purely combinatorial way, some formulas previously obtained through algebraic methods.

Abstract: We introduce a bilingual domain-specific language (DSL) for modelling compute kernels within a generic solver for hyperbolic partial differential equations (PDEs). Users express PDE terms, i.e.~the underlying physics, in a familiar native language such as C or C++, while the numerical scheme is specified in a Python-embedded DSL, DSLHyPE. DSLHyPE's compiler lowers the Python description to MLIR and introduces a translation pass that integrates it with native code likewise mapped to MLIR. Our approach keeps the numerical representation and the physics implementation separate for as long as possible, while delegating optimization to the compiler through existing MLIR optimization passes. This separation of concerns benefits researchers developing numerical schemes on top of existing PDE implementations or with applications involving nonlinear systems whose PDE terms must solve PDEs themselves. We demonstrate the feasibility of the approach using a gravitational-wave solver and a matter-evolution solver on x86 processors and H200 GPUs.

This ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line meets the critical temperature line of the Potts model, in a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori critical point -- a tricritical point at finite inference strength that separates a paramagnetic, a ferromagnetic and a 'spin-glass' phase. Finally, we discuss a general argument based on ${\it Elitzur's \; theorem}$ that establishes stability of the ordinary Nishimori critical points in their respective learning phase diagrams.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者观察到每个转移计数在减去历史依赖的平均事件倾向后，其剩余随机增量构成鞅增量（martingale increment），不同转移与不同时间的鞅增量相互正交，并形成任何可观测量的随机偏离的完全正交基。将某一事件对最终可观测量的因果关系定义为事件-后果核，并用事件强度归一化后即得到历史条件化的响应，由此建立精确的有限时间与有限频率涨落-响应关系（finite-time and finite-frequency fluctuation-response relations）。此外，通过对数速率敏感度进行上界约束，进一步得到受动力学活动（dynamical activity）控制的响应-动力学不确定关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于事件记录的非马尔可夫动力学提供了严格的涨落-响应框架，其关系式可通过自发轨迹进行估计，并给出了受动力学活动控制的响应-动力学不确定关系，具有广泛的实验适用性。

Abstract: Modern experiments often record nonequilibrium dynamics as sequences of discrete events whose rates depend on the realized past. We develop a fluctuation-response theory for such non-Markovian jump processes directly on the observed event record. Memory can destroy a closed master equation for the state probabilities. Each transition count nevertheless obeys an exact stochastic equation. After the history-dependent mean event tendency is subtracted, the remaining random increment is a martingale increment--the part of the event that cannot be predicted from the past. Martingale increments associated with different transitions and different times are orthogonal. These increments form a complete orthogonal basis for the random deviation of any observable measured from the record, such as a current, occupation time, or event count. The coefficient of a given increment is the event-consequence kernel. It measures how that event changes the predicted final observable, relative to continuing without the event, for the particular history already realized. Multiplying this kernel by the event intensity gives the history-conditioned response to perturbing the corresponding transition rate. Thus, the intensity-normalized response is exactly the expansion coefficient of that event in the observable fluctuation. This identification yields exact finite-time and finite-frequency fluctuation-response relations. Averaging over histories leaves a nonnegative response-heterogeneity gap. The gap measures how strongly the consequence of the same event varies across histories and tests whether a proposed memory coordinate is response-sufficient. Finite-history versions can be estimated from spontaneous trajectories. Bounding the logarithmic rate sensitivity further gives response-kinetic uncertainty relations controlled by dynamical activity.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非线性谐振器中，固定脉冲能量时驱动波形与非线性转换效率之间的关系缺乏明确的线性准则：短脉冲与窄共振耦合差，长脉冲稀释能量，两者之间存在需要精确求解的折中问题。该问题对于微环（microring）、超导电路及宽带光子对源（broadband photon-pair source）等应用具有实际意义。

Method: 针对振幅衰减率为κ的单模系统，通过消除驱动项将固定入射能量转化为仅关于存储场（stored field）的约束，进而将优化问题转化为一个严格的Gagliardo–Nirenberg不等式。该不等式的极值函数即非线性薛定谔方程的基态孤子，由此获得最优存储场的解析形式及相应的最优输入波形和最大转换能量。

Conclusion: 该上界具有普适性，适用于从微环到超导电路的各类系统，并为宽带光子对源的每脉冲亮度设定了理论上限。

Abstract: At fixed pulse energy, which drive waveform extracts the most $n$th-order nonlinear conversion from a resonator ($n=2$ for second harmonic)? A short pulse couples poorly to a narrow resonance, a long one dilutes its energy, and no linear rule fixes the compromise. We solve the problem exactly for a single mode of amplitude decay rate $κ$. Eliminating the drive turns fixed incident energy into a constraint on the stored field alone, and the optimization becomes a sharp Gagliardo--Nirenberg inequality whose extremal is the ground-state soliton of the nonlinear Schrödinger equation. The optimal stored field is $\mathrm{sech}^{1/(n-1)}[(n-1)κt]$, sustained by an asymmetric input that rises as $e^{κt}$ and falls as $e^{-(2n-1)κt}$; the largest converted energy follows in closed form. A rising exponential, the time-reversal recipe, retains at most $79.0\%$ of the bound at $n=2$ and $2/e$ at large $n$; a two-rate pulse retains above $97\%$. Critical coupling generalizes to $n$-fold overcoupling, with optimal input coupling $n$ times the intrinsic loss rate. The bound applies from microrings to superconducting circuits and caps the per-pulse brightness of broadband photon-pair sources.

Result: 主要结果包括四项：(i) 高于显式临界筛选率时，任何体积下极小元均存在；(ii) 低于显式体积阈值时，任何筛选率下极小元均存在；(iii) 在小体积且筛选率一致的前提下，球（ball）是唯一极小元（模平移）；(iv) 存在一个依赖于筛选率的精确体积阈值，决定球是否为稳定的体积约束临界点，该阈值在α=0时退化为已知的未筛选值。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式给出临界参数，完整刻画了带Yukawa非局部排斥项的周长泛函极小化问题的存在性与稳定性，并统一涵盖了未筛选（α=0）的已知情形。

Abstract: We study a long-range perturbation of the perimeter functional by a nonlocal repulsive term defined through the Yukawa kernel, minimized under a volume constraint. Because the kernel decays exponentially, the competition between surface tension and repulsion is governed by two independent quantities, the screening rate and the volume. Specifically we prove that (i) above an explicit critical screening rate, minimizers exist at every volume, whereas earlier work required the volume to be large; (ii) below an explicit volume threshold minimizers exist for every screening rate; (iii) at small volume, and uniformly in the screening rate, the ball is the unique minimizer up to translation; and (iv) there exists a sharp volume threshold, depending on the screening rate, for the ball to be a stable volume-constrained critical point. The threshold is given in closed form for every screening rate and reduces to the known unscreened value when $α=0$. Our proofs overcome new technical changes due to the lack of homogeneity in the Yukawa kernel.

</details>

Abstract: We study the dynamics of the kinetic wave equation associated to the three dimensional Schrödinger equation close to Rayleigh-Jeans equilibria. We first prove that the linearised operator generates a semigroup of contractions in $L^2((0,\infty);\sqrt ω\ddω)$. Considering the family of nonsingular Rayleigh-Jeans spectra, we prove that the linearised operator possesses a spectral gap, despite the non-compactness of the integral collisional operator, and thus obtain an exponential relaxation for the linear semigroup. We then prove bilinear and trilinear estimates in the relevant norm for the nonlinear terms and deduce global well-posedness and exponential relaxation for sufficiently small relative perturbations of Rayleigh-Jeans equilibria. To our knowledge, this is the first global strong well-posedness and asymptotic stability result near a nonzero thermodynamic equilibrium for the full spatially homogeneous four-wave kinetic equation associated with the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation.

Abstract: In this article, we have studied a particular criterion to establish if a particular class of nonlinear PDEs $\mathcal{O}_t u=\mathcal{O}_x u $ in unidimensional space-time $(x,t)$ admits elementary solutions respect to the spatial coordinate $x$, through the nested derivative method. Where a nested derivative is a generalization of the usual partial derivative of a order higher than one and, in terms of differentials operators, is a generalization of the Laguerre operator. This method integrated with Liouville's theorem and Galois theory e method by integrate by parts establish if solution of nested derivative equations admits elementary solutions respect with $x$. These solutions could help to study better evolutions of possible physical systems in space-time.

Abstract: Time-varying interaction delays are ubiquitous in active matter, yet their collective effects remain largely unexplored. We show that active particles with internal dynamics and Vicsek-like delayed alignment exhibit intermittent flocking, characterized by long episodes of coherent motion interrupted by brief disordering events. This collective behavior arises from laminar chaos, a form of chaotic dynamics unique to systems with time-varying delays. Changing only the delay parameters qualitatively reshapes collective motion, producing fractal changes in global flocking order. Our results establish the temporal structure of interaction delays as a new control parameter for active matter.

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 该论文研究了中等能量双摆（double pendulum）这一典型的混沌系统中臂翻转（flip）现象，提出了一种基于相空间流（phase space flux）的统计预测方法，用于预测平均翻转率。研究发现，对于等质量等臂长的“平等型”（egalitarian）双摆，统计预测与数值模拟高度吻合（整体平均误差约1%，单个混沌轨迹误差在几个百分点内），验证了遍历近似（ergodic approximation）的有效性。鞍点轨道（saddle orbits）在此方法中扮演核心角色，为定义翻转提供了天然的分割面（dividing surface），并避免了重交叉（recrossing）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中等能量双摆是典型的混沌系统，尽管其运动不规则，却表现出周期性的翻转事件（其中一臂越过顶端）。作者希望建立一种统计预测框架，从相空间层面定量预测平均翻转率，并验证遍历近似的适用性。该研究还与平等型三体系统（egalitarian three-body system）的流基方法密切相关，具有更广泛的物理意义。

Method: 作者提出基于相空间流的统计预测方法来计算平均翻转率。核心工具是鞍点轨道——这类周期轨道在能量接近鞍点能量时趋向稳定的鞍点本征模式，在高能时近似沿势能山脊（ridge of the potential）运动。这些轨道提供了定义翻转的天然分割面，从而避免重交叉问题。作者还在鞍点轨道基础上提出了两种简化的替代分割面，其中一种基于鞍点轨道的精确解析近似。

Result: 通过对等质量等臂长的“平等型”双摆进行验证，统计预测与数值模拟之间表现出极为优秀的一致性：系综平均翻转率的一致性在约1%水平，即使单个混沌轨迹的统计也在百分之几的误差范围内。该一致性对任一手臂的翻转以及宽泛的能量范围均成立。此外，基于鞍点轨道分割面得到的交叉时间（crossing time）分布呈现出明显的间隙（gap）。

Conclusion: 基于相空间流的翻转率统计预测方法在平等型双摆系统中得到验证，量化了遍历近似的有效性，并为理解混沌系统中的稀有但规律的动力学事件提供了理论工具。该方法与平等型三体系统所用的方法密切相关，有望推广至其他混沌系统。

Abstract: The intermediate-energy double pendulum is a prototypical chaotic system. Despite its irregular motion, it exhibits recurrent flips - events in which one of the arms passes over the top. We develop a statistical prediction for the mean flip rate in terms of phase space flux. Applying this flux-based approach to the equal-mass, equal arm ("egalitarian") double pendulum, we find excellent agreement between the statistical prediction and numerical simulations: ensemble-averaged flip rates agree at about the 1% level, while even the statistics of individual chaotic trajectories agree at the few-percent level, quantifying the validity of the ergodic approximation. This agreement holds for flips of either arm and over a broad range of energies. This flux-based approach is closely related to that used for the egalitarian three-body system.
  A central role is played by the saddle orbits: periodic orbits that tend to the stable saddle eigenmode as the energy approaches the saddle energy from above and approximately follow the ridge of the potential at higher energies. These orbits provide a natural dividing surface for defining flips while avoiding recrossings. Indeed, the resulting distribution of crossing times exhibits a distinct gap. We also present two alternative simplified dividing surfaces, one of which is based on an accurate analytic approximation to the saddle orbits.

</details>

Abstract: Bismuth selenide (Bi2Se3) is a topological insulator with potential applications in thermoelectrics, spintronics, and optoelectronics. However, its response to radiation remains poorly understood. We investigate cumulative X-ray damage in Bi2Se3 using simultane- ous transmission X-ray microscopy (TXM) and X-ray diffraction (XRD) at the Pohang Accelerator Laboratory X-Ray Free Electron Laser (PAL-XFEL) over 27,000 successive pulses. We observe distinct damage mechanisms: rapid hole formation via vaporization within 100 pulses, followed by slower grain refinement and material sputtering over thousands of thermal cycles. Williamson-Hall analysis reveals a progressive transformation from single-crystal to nanocrystalline structure, with grain sizes decreasing from mi- cron to nanometer scale. Finite-element modeling confirms that X-rays penetrate 13.47 μm, driving local temperatures above 1600 K with subsequent cooling between pulses. Scanning electron microscopy identifies three characteristic morphologies correspond- ing to different thermal histories: sputter streaks, prismatic crystals, and disordered microcrystals. Our results demonstrate that grain-boundary formation creates a feedback mechanism that accelerates damage in later pulses. This work establishes a method- ology for studying radiation damage across multiple length scales and provides insight into topological insulator stability under extreme conditions

Under limited screening budgets, FGW-guided registry selection consistently outperforms random search and is more robust across interface systems than selection guided by pretrained MACE energies. The workflow converts the qualitative notion of bulk-like continuity into a quantitative prescreening criterion, enabling efficient registry exploration and providing physically informed candidate structures for materials discovery workflows.

Key design considerations for mitigating parasitic inductive background signals are also presented. Instead, the interfacial analysis indicates that the strongest trend of the spin Hall angle is associated with the chemistry of the upper YPtBi/W boundary: the effective SOT response tracks the integrated W concentration at that YPtBi surface. Meanwhile, a two-spin source analysis shows that the Pt-W-rich interlayer provides only a small positive correction, insufficient to explain the large negative effective spin Hall angle by itself. The dominant control variable is therefore inferred to be the incorporation of W into the upper YPtBi interface, which plausibly modifies the local electronic structure of YPtBi and amplifies the stack-level response. These results provide a more physically constrained interpretation of the stack behavior and identify a BEOL-compatible route to disordered topological spin-source layers for scaled SOT memory and compute-in-memory hardware.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 表面终止结构可以重塑费米弧的形貌特征（arc fingerprint），但不会破坏其类弧物理（arc-like physics）。这一发现深化了对Na$_3$Bi表面体系中费米弧鲁棒性的理解，对拓扑材料的表面工程和自旋电子学应用具有参考价值。

Abstract: Whether a quantum system with a double-well effective potential undergoes spontaneous symmetry breaking depends not only on the potential landscape but also on the kinetics and the coupling with additional degrees of freedom. Exploiting the analytical solutions, we provide a strict definition of the quantum paraelectric regime and identify a distinct quantum ferroelectric regime in which symmetry breaking can be realized without tunneling features. We then show that explicit symmetry breaking cannot be inferred from the order-parameter Hamiltonian alone, but requires additional couplings. This leads us to identify a regime of \emph{quantum dissipative paraelectricity}, in which observable symmetry breaking is suppressed during the evolution toward the ground state, even when the double-well structure dominates over zero-point quantum fluctuations.

The number of independent components is significantly reduced by symmetry constraints, which in turn allows for explicit expressions describing spin-reversal processes and intensity modulations. Abstract: We establish an optimal Poincaré inequality for real-valued symmetric observables of the complex Ginibre log-gas. Equality is attained by the real and imaginary parts of the center-of-mass observable. Equivalently, we determine the exact spectral gap of the associated overdamped Langevin dynamics, for real symmetric observables. The Hessian of the energy of this log-gas is unbounded below, so standard convexity arguments do not directly apply. Our proof instead combines a Vandermonde transform, a holomorphic projection, and a complex Gaussian d-bar spectral-gap estimate, corresponding to the constant-curvature case of the Hörmander-Berndtsson estimate.

</details>

Conclusion: 该研究解决了Aurzada、Lifshits和Schickentanz提出的猜想，将一维二次情形的结果推广至任意有限维数和广泛束缚势类别。所提出的方法具有通用性，可适用于更广泛的可逆马尔可夫过程，具有良好的推广前景。

Abstract: We study Brownian motion in R^d conditioned so that the time average of a continuous confining potential remains below a fixed level. On every fixed initial time interval, we prove that the conditioned process converges in total variation to the ground-state diffusion associated with a suitable Schrödinger operator. We also obtain sharp asymptotics for the probability of the conditioning event, including bounded perturbations of the constraint. The proof is based on a local limit theorem for the corresponding Feynman-Kac measures. Our results extend the previously known one-dimensional quadratic case to arbitrary finite dimension and a broad class of confining potentials, therefore resolving a conjecture of Aurzada, Lifshits and Schickentanz. The presented approach also works when Brownian motion is replaced by suitable reversible Markov processes, including multidimensional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes, CIR processes and continuous-time Markov chains.

</details>

Result: 文章系统地整理了排他过程和零程过程中流体力学大数定律（LLN）与中心极限定理（CLT）型标度极限的证明框架，包括熵与相对熵方法的应用、无穷体积系统的构造、不变测度结构、占据时间与标记粒子等局部泛函的标度极限，以及平衡态下体质量涨落的结果。

Conclusion: 本文以教学笔记形式，为初学者提供了连接微观粒子系统行为与宏观连续统定律的完整方法论路径，涵盖从有限体积到无穷体积、从体质量到局部泛函、从大数定律到涨落的多层次极限理论框架，可作为现有经典教材的有益补充。

Abstract: In these lecture notes, we discuss various `hydrodynamic LLN' and `CLT' scaling limits, among others, in types of stochastic interacting particle systems, connecting `microscopic' behaviors to continuum laws. Via `short stories', the aim is to present some of the `basics' for students and those entering the field, as a complement to books such as Kipnis-Landim 1999, Komorowski-Landim-Olla 2012, Liggett 1985, Liggett 1999. To be concrete, attention is restricted to a few `mass conservative' systems on discrete spaces, namely exclusion and zero-range processes, that have proved robust in the study of different phenomena.
  After preliminaries, we discuss the `entropy' and `relative entropy' methods to prove hydrodynamic limits of the bulk mass in finite volume, as well as other items such as construction of systems in infinite volume and the structure of their invariant measures, and scaling limits of local functionals, such as occupation times of sites and the motion of a tagged particle. In the last part, we also discuss equilibrium fluctuations of the bulk mass when the process starts from an invariant measure.

</details>

Abstract: Combining thermodynamics and optics, we investigate the Van der Waals behaviors of non-commutative Schwarzschild black holes with global monopoles. Abstract: We investigate the holographic dictionary relating point particles carrying longitudinal momentum or angular momentum in asymptotically AdS$_3$ spacetimes to suitably regulated, time-evolved states created by local primary operators in the dual two-dimensional conformal field theory (2D CFT). We find that asymmetric left/right Euclidean smearing of local operators produces states carrying momentum, and the corresponding bulk excitation is a particle with conserved momentum. We compute the energy density and entanglement entropy in these states and in their dual back-reacted geometries, finding exact agreement between the CFT and gravity descriptions. We further extend this correspondence to particles with intrinsic spin, whose CFT duals are primary operators with unequal holomorphic and anti-holomorphic scaling dimensions. We again find a precise match between CFT and holographic calculations of energy densities and entanglement entropies. Finally, we explore applications of these setups beyond holography by deriving the evolution of Rényi entropies in 2D rational CFTs and introducing a new class of local quantum quench protocols with conserved longitudinal or angular momentum.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞内部（black hole interior）在三维引力中的生长与其边界理论的对偶描述是全息原理的重要问题；此前Wilson线与余维二（codimension-two）观测量的联系已被用于全息纠缠熵，但余维一观测量对应的边界构造尚不清楚。作者旨在探究算子Krylov复杂度是否能在三维引力中编码黑洞内部的几何增长。

Abstract: We investigate the growth of the black hole interior in three-dimensional gravity from the boundary theory. By contrast, the Krylov spread complexity of the thermofield double state, obtained from the semiclassical gravitational partition function, does not exhibit the linear growth of the bulk volume within the regime accessible to our analysis. Abstract: We examine the formulation of Kähler fermions on spacetimes with Lorentz signature. We propose to apply Coecke's notion of quantum information flow (QIF) in protocol-network geometry, developed within categorical quantum mechanics, to a holographic setup, and show that its trajectories within the discrete holographic bulk---the tensor network---obey the divergence-free and density-bound conditions of bit threads. From this process-centered perspective, bit-thread nonuniqueness becomes natural: for a given resource state, different protocols realizing the same distillation task can give rise to different QIF trajectories. We further analyze stabilizer-type holographic tensor networks using ZX string diagrams and show that QIF trajectories can probe finer entanglement structure beyond the level captured by entanglement entropy.

</details>

Abstract: We construct a semistrict higher Chern--Simons (HCS) gauge theory in $2n+2$ dimensions associated with balanced 2-term $L_\infty$-algebras. Starting from the homotopy Maurer--Cartan theory, we first introduce 2-term $L_\infty$-algebra gauge theory, and show that there is a four-dimensional HCS construction. Its transgression yields an explicit $(2n+2)$-dimensional HCS form. We further establish a higher Chern--Weil theorem that generates higher transgression forms, and prove that the HCS theory is a distinguished instance of the higher transgression gauge theory. Finally, we apply the extended Cartan homotopy formula in this semistrict setting, and show that it is a common origin of both the higher Chern--Weil theorem and the associated triangle equation.

Abstract: We introduce tropical and stringy integrals for fixed-graph contributions to cosmological in-in correlators of conformally coupled scalars. The full-time representation factorizes into a graph-dependent vertex-space Laplace integral, with one real variable for each graph vertex, and an elementary edge-space Laplace integral, with one real variable for each internal edge. The vertex-space exponent is a sum of absolute values associated with sites and relative edge times; as a piecewise-linear function, it is the support function of the in-in zonotope. Each absolute value is also the tropical limit of a positive Laurent binomial. Retaining these binomials before tropicalization defines a finite-$α'$ vertex-space stringy integral, so both the polytope and its stringy integral are read directly from the physical time integral. In the $α' \to 0$ limit this integral becomes the normalized dual volume of the in-in zonotope, while the edge-space factor deforms independently into a product of beta integrals and restores the elementary propagator normalization. We derive the field-theory rational form from augmented-graph chambers, as well as exact finite-$α'$ parallel-edge reduction, factorization formulas for edge-energy and partial-energy poles, and even descendant towers. As an alternative geometric realization of fixed-graph correlators, we find an ambient Minkowski-sum and stringy-integral realization of the graph correlahedron for a tree graph as the so-called graph cubeahedron of its line graph. For completeness, we also record the logarithmic critical equations and generic reference degrees of the associated affine divisor arrangement.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机在于将已有的Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg对应从普通格拉斯曼流形（Grassmannian）推广到超格拉斯曼流形（super-Grassmannian）以及超群（supergroup）情形，从而为弦论中的几何对偶提供更广泛的数学框架。尽管所构造的规范线性sigma模型是非幺正的，作者仍希望通过研究其超对称态空间来建立新的数学对应关系，并探索其在代数几何和物理中的潜在应用。

Conclusion: 本文证明了尽管所构造的超规范线性sigma模型是非幺正的，其超对称态空间仍能产生有意义的数学对应。研究成果建立了超格拉斯曼流形/超群框架下Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg对应的多个推广，涵盖完全交、超曲面、超向量丛和quadrics完全交等不同情形，为弦论与代数几何之间的更深层联系提供了新的证据。

Abstract: We construct 2d $\mathcal{N}=(2,2)$ gauged linear sigma models with $\mathrm{U}(1|1)^N$ supergauge group possibly with superpotential. Despite being nonunitary, one can still study their space of supersymmetric states and explore their applications to mathematics. In particular, we find a relation between a nonlinear sigma model on a Calabi-Yau complete intersection of hypersurfaces in a super-Grassmannian and a supergauged Landau-Ginzburg orbifold, which can reduce to a regular Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg correspondence for complete intersections. This defines a super-Grassmannian/supergroup generalization of the correspondence proved by Clader [1] and Zhao [2]. Similarly, we find a relation between a nonlinear sigma model on a Calabi-Yau hypersurface in a product of super-Grassmannians and a hybrid NLSM/supergauged Landau-Ginzburg orbifold, which can reduce to a regular hybrid Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg correspondence for hypersurfaces in product space. This defines a super-Grassmannian/supergroup generalization of the correspondence proved by Fan-Jarvis-Ruan [3]. We also find that Calabi-Yau supervector bundles over a super-Grassmannian can undergo a physically related mild topology change which is reducible to a regular Atiyah-type flop transition. This defines a super-Grassmannian generalization of a birational equivalence of Calabi-Yau vector bundles in mathematics. Similarly, we find that a Calabi-Yau complete intersection of quadrics in a super-Grassmannian can also undergo a physically related topology change which is reducible to a regular conifold transition. This defines a super-Grassmannian generalization of a homological projective duality for Calabi-Yau quadrics by Kuznetsov-Perry [4] in mathematics.

</details>


### [88] [A Stationary Composition Law for Schwinger-Keldysh Effective Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19795)
*Denis Comelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究开放系统与封闭系统在非平衡量子场论（nonequilibrium quantum field theory）中有效作用量的关系。环境诱导贡献 $W_{\rm IF}$ 定义为开放系统与封闭系统连通生成泛函之差，但其Legendre变换不满足简单的加法关系，而是满足一个平稳组合法则（stationary composition law）：$\Gamma_{\cal O}[\Phi] = \operatorname*{Stat}_\Psi \{ \Gamma_{\cal C}[\Psi] + \Gamma_{\rm IF}[\Phi-\Psi]\}$。该变分组合适用于局部与非局部的Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量，并通过二次理论和一般时间无关有效作用量分别展示其线性与非线性实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平衡量子场论中，开放量子系统的有效作用量通常通过Schwinger-Keldysh形式论计算，但环境引入的贡献与封闭系统贡献之间在有效作用量层面的精确关系尚不明确，特别是Legendre变换后加法关系是否成立的问题需要澄清。

Method: 作者定义环境诱导的连通生成泛函差 $W_{\rm IF}[J] \equiv W_{\cal O}[J] - W_{\cal C}[J]$，对其做Legendre变换后，推导出有效作用量满足一个带平稳条件的变分组合公式。该公式通过二次理论（线性实现）和一般时间无关有效作用量（非线性实现）两个例子加以验证和说明。

Result: 证明了有效作用量 $\Gamma_{\cal O}$ 不满足与生成泛函 $W$ 类似的简单加法关系，而是满足平稳组合法则。该法则对局部和非局部的Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量均适用，并通过线性与非线性两种实现进行了具体展示。

Conclusion: 开放系统有效作用量的环境贡献不能通过简单相加获得，而需通过变分平稳条件组合得到，这一结果统一描述了开放与封闭系统在非平衡量子场论框架下的结构关系。

Abstract: Let $W_{\cal O}[J]$ and $W_{\cal C}[J]$ denote the connected generating functionals of an open system and its closed system counterpart, and define the environment-induced contribution $W_{\rm IF}[J]$ by the difference: $ W_{\rm IF}[J]\equiv W_{\cal O}[J]-W_{\cal C}[J].$ The corresponding Legendre transforms, $Γ_{\cal O}$, $Γ_{\cal C}$, and $Γ_{\rm IF}$, do not obey an analogous additive relation but satisfy a stationary composition law given by:
  \[
  Γ_{\cal O}[Φ]
  =
  \operatorname*{Stat}_Ψ
  \left\{
  Γ_{\cal C}[Ψ]
  +
  Γ_{\rm IF}[Φ-Ψ]
  \right\} \] where ${\rm Stat}$ denotes evaluation at a solution of the stationarity condition with respect to $Ψ$. This variational composition applies to both local and nonlocal Schwinger-Keldysh effective actions in nonequilibrium quantum field theory. We illustrate its linear and nonlinear realizations through quadratic theories and general time independent effective actions, respectively.

</details>

Result: 在平面标架中，共尺度关联函数方程被识别为膨胀Ward恒等式；在纠缠熵部分，相对尺度方程由横向双曲面积控制，且在二维情形下约化为已知的纠缠重整化群方程。

Abstract: We identify CFT counterparts of Synge's identity for finite AdS geodesics in arbitrary dimensions. Starting from exact finite bulk--boundary pairs, the Synge equation induces endpoint scale equations for a Weyl-frame scalar two-point function and for the finite entanglement entropy between disjoint balls. In the planar frame, the common-scale correlator equation is the dilatation Ward identity. In the entanglement sector, the corresponding relative-scale equation is governed by a transverse hyperbolic area and reduces in two dimensions to the previously identified entanglement RG equation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 研究表明，上述方法完全确定了整个构造，消除了所有与场重定义相关的模糊性。

Conclusion: 该结果说明最小导数约束结合展开方程方法为超对称大引力构造提供了确定性的框架，可完整固定相应的相互作用结构。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the interaction of massless $N=1$ supergravity with a massive (2,3/2,3/2,1) supermultiplet, as a possible candidate for a supersymmetric extension to bigravity. A gauge invariant description for massive spin-2, spin-3/2 and spin-1 fields is used. As a result of this, ambiguities arise related to possible field redefinitions, which are fixed by using a recently proposed method based on unfolded equations, and by restricting ourselves to interactions with the minimum number of derivatives (two for bosonic and one for fermionic vertices). It appears that this completely fixes the entire construction.

</details>


### [92] [A Note on a High-Energy Limit of Gravitational Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19941)
*Poul H. Damgaard,Ludovic Plante*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论后闵可夫斯基展开（post-Minkowskian expansion）中最近推导出的二元散射动量冲击全阶公式，证明其是一个渐近展开（asymptotic expansion），虽然级数发散但给出了其为Borel可求和（Borel summable）的证据，并发现该函数在积分形式下是有限的，同时确定了展开系数中的超越性（transcendentality）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后闵可夫斯基展开将广义相对论中的度规在牛顿常数G围绕平坦时空展开，但当前研究关注如何通过对微扰级数求和到全阶来扩展其适用范围。一个特定高能极限区域中的动量冲击公式需要确定其数学性质，以评估其可靠性和向强耦合（strong coupling）拓展的可能性。

Method: 通过数学分析证明所研究的全阶公式是一个渐近展开；利用Borel求和（Borel summation）方法论证级数虽然发散但可求和；将完整函数以积分形式表示并证明其有限性；通过对展开式系数的分析确定其超越性性质。

Result: 证明了该公式对应的是一个渐近展开；级数虽然是发散的，但存在Borel可求和的证据；以积分形式表示的完整函数是有限的；作为副产品，确定了展开表达式中各系数的超越性性质。

Conclusion: 该动量冲击公式作为一种渐近展开，虽然级数发散但仍可通过Borel求和获得有限结果，这为后闵可夫斯基展开在高能极限下的应用提供了坚实的数学基础，并为进一步向强耦合区域推进提供了可能性。

Abstract: The post-Minkowskian expansion of general relativity expands the metric in Newton's constant G around flat space-time, but much effort currently focuses on ways to extend its range of applicability by summing the perturbative series to all orders. A regime of particular interest is a specific high-energy limit where a formula derived recently provides the momentum kick of binary scattering problem to all orders, and even suggests an approach towards strong coupling. We show that this is an asymptotic expansion. Although the series diverges we give evidence that it is Borel summable, and the full function in integral form is finite. As a by-product we determine the transcendentality properties of the coefficients in the expanded expression.

</details>


### [93] [Hilbert space relation from dS to AdS through Reflection Positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19948)
*Yu-ki Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究自由标量场在固定de Sitter（dS）背景上单粒子扇区中de Sitter幺正性与反射正定性的逻辑独立性及其关系。通过将不变Hilbert积与赤道反射复合，作者发现主级数（principal series）中该形式恒为零，而互补级数（complementary series）中反射核在单位球上仅在满足标量共形幺正性界时为正半定。所得Hilbert空间为AdS等距群的正能量表示，其取值范围与从物理角度识别的Klebanov-Witten交替量子化窗口一致，从而提出AdS与dS之间无需半径解析延拓的全新表示论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: de Sitter量子场论中的幺正性与反射正定性（reflection positivity）是逻辑上相互独立的概念，二者之间的关系在单粒子层面上尚未被系统厘清。受Witten的de Sitter配对及Bousso、Maloney和Strominger的径向伴随工作启发，作者希望从表示论（representation theory）视角建立AdS与dS之间的新联系，绕开传统的半径解析延拓。

Method: 作者在固定de Sitter背景上的自由标量场中，将注意力限缩至单粒子扇区，用赤道反射（equatorial reflection）复合不变Hilbert积来构造新的内积形式。针对主级数与互补级数两种级数实现，分别研究该反射形式的行为及其Osterwalder-Schrader商空间，并借助Neeb和Ólafsson的正半定性判据分析反射核的谱性质。

Result: 主级数实现中反射形式恒等于零，其Osterwalder-Schrader商空间不含任何态。互补级数中反射形式给出单位球上的非零反射核，当低权重满足标量共形幺正性界时该核为正半定。所得Hilbert空间按照Neeb和Ólafsson的结果承载AdS等距群的正能量表示，且其参数范围在物理上恰好对应Klebanov-Witten交替量子化窗口。

Conclusion: 该对应关系提示，从表示论角度看，AdS与dS之间可以建立一种无需常规半径解析延拓（analytic continuation of radius）的新联系。这一结果在数学上统一了互补级数的粒子全性与AdS正能量表示的存在性，为理解dS/AdS对应提供了新的结构视角。

Abstract: De Sitter unitarity and reflection positivity are logically independent. We study their relation for a free scalar field on a fixed de Sitter background, restricting attention to the one-particle sector. Motivated by Witten's de Sitter pairing and the radial adjoint of Bousso, Maloney and Strominger, we compose the invariant Hilbert product with equatorial reflection. In the standard principal-series realization, this form vanishes identically and its Osterwalder-Schrader quotient contains no states. For the complementary series, the reflected product becomes a nonzero reflected kernel on the unit ball. A result of Neeb and Ólafsson shows that this kernel is positive semidefinite precisely when the lower weight satisfies the scalar conformal unitarity bound. The resulting Hilbert space with the reflected inner product furnishes a positive-energy representation of AdS isometries according to Neeb and Ólafsson. We identify that the range coincides with the Klebanov-Witten alternative-quantization window from physics point of view. This correspondence suggests a novel relation from the representation viewpoint between AdS and dS without the conventional analytic continuation of radius.

</details>


### [94] [Singularity-Rank Signatures in Generalized Dirac Oscillators near a BTZ Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20071)
*João V. R. Alencar,Allan R. P. Moreira,João B. R. Silva,Abdullah Guvendi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非极值BTZ黑洞近地平线区域中的奇异广义Dirac-振子耦合，建立了与空时地平线汇相对应的有效协变结构，分析了两种奇异剖面（1/ρ和1/ρ²阶）的谱性质，发现p=1为Fuchsian型可约化为Whittaker方程，p=2为第一类非正则奇点情形且具有阶乘级数增长，并推导出有限半径响应函数满足精确的Riccati方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究奇异广义Dirac-振子耦合在非极值BTZ黑洞近地平线区域的物理与数学特性，理解不同奇异阶数的谱体系及其为后续完整BTZ外部匹配提供局域自旋数据的基础。

Method: 采用适用于静止地平线汇（stationary horizon congruence）的有效协变构造方法，将两个奇异剖面与固有加速度及其径向梯度联系起来；通过分析p=1与p=2两种情形的正则/非正则奇点结构，使用常数旋量变换和消元法分别导出Whittaker方程和合流Heun表示，并对p=2情形进行局部分支展开与系数渐近分析，最后构造有限半径响应函数并推导其满足的Riccati方程。

Result: p=1系统具有正则奇点（Fuchsian型），可精确约化为Whittaker方程，同时消元后得到带表观有限奇点的合流Heun表示；p=2为第一类非正则情形，局部支包含e^{±β₂/ρ}ρ^{-1/2}基本因子，系数展开呈现一般阶乘级数晚阶增长；响应函数Z_p(Ω;ρ₀)满足的Riccati方程在大阻尼展开中于γ^{-3}阶首次敏感于相互作用的径向梯度，且即便耦合在ρ₀处匹配，响应仍保留不同极点阶数的信息。

Conclusion: 奇异Dirac-振子耦合在BTZ黑洞近地平线区域产生两种截然不同的数学结构（Fuchsian与非正则），所构造的响应函数为后续匹配完整BTZ外部提供局域自旋数据基础，且其Riccati方程结构为区分不同奇异相互作用阶数提供了可观测的诊断。

Abstract: We study singular generalized Dirac-oscillator couplings in the near-horizon region of a nonextremal BTZ black hole. An effective covariant construction adapted to the stationary horizon congruence relates the two singular profiles to the proper acceleration and its radial gradient, whose leading near-horizon behaviors are $1/ρ$ and $1/ρ^2$. This motivates the family $\mathcal{L}_p(ρ)=λ_0+β_p/ρ^p$, with $p=1,2$. The $p=1$ system has a regular singular point and is Fuchsian, whereas $p=2$ is the first irregular case; more generally, a leading $ρ^{-p}$ interaction with $p>1$ has Poincaré rank $p-1$. For $p=1$, a constant spinor transformation reduces the matrix-Coulomb system exactly to the Whittaker equation, while elimination in the original basis gives an equivalent confluent-Heun representation with an apparent finite singularity. For $p=2$, the local branches contain the essential factors $e^{\pmβ_2/ρ}ρ^{-1/2}$, and their coefficient expansions exhibit generic factorial late-order growth. The finite-radius response $\mathcal{Z}_p(Ω;ρ_0)=ψ_{2,p}(ρ_0)/ψ_{1,p}(ρ_0)$ obeys an exact Riccati equation whose large-damping expansion first becomes sensitive to the radial gradient of the interaction at order $γ^{-3}$. Even when the two couplings are matched at $ρ_0$, this response retains information about their different pole orders. The response functions provide local spinorial data for subsequent matching to the complete BTZ exterior.

</details>


### [95] [Maximal Torus Topological Entanglement Entropy in WZW Theories at Bounded Ground-State Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20333)
*Ce Shen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究Wess–Zumino–Witten（WZW）模张量范畴在环面真空磁通态上的普适熵贡献Γ_{T²}=2log𝒟，在固定简单对象数上限R的条件下最大化该熵，证明了渐近上界Γ_max(R)∼[7ζ(3)/(4π²)]q_R²，其中q_R=log₂R，并由Sp(2n)_n序列达到该系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在确定给定简单对象数目约束下，WZW模张量范畴的总量子维数（total quantum dimension）所能达到的最大普适熵贡献，从而刻画该类系统在强约束下的熵增长渐近规律。

Method: 作者在所有单李代数（simple Lie algebra）和正整数级（positive integral level）上，以r(g,k)≤R为约束最大化Γ_{T²}。通过分析Sp(2n)_n序列的Fourier展开，发现类型C根积分的偶模式消失，得到系数7ζ(3)/(4π²)。利用熵–谱不等式（entropy–spectral inequality）证明经典族的最优性，并通过秩–级对偶（rank–level duality）与固定秩估计控制非平衡序列和例外（exceptional）序列。

Result: 得到渐近公式Γ_max(R)∼[7ζ(3)/(4π²)]q_R²（R→∞），该系数由Sp(2n)_n序列精确达到。等秩等级时，类型C根积分的Fourier展开仅保留奇数模式，由∑_{m odd}m⁻³=7ζ(3)/8的恒等式给出了该数值系数。

Conclusion: 该系统在给定简单对象数上限下，最大熵贡献呈q_R²量级的对数平方增长，且Asymptotic系数具有精确的普适常数7ζ(3)/(4π²)，由辛族（symplectic family）Sp(2n)_n实现最优。

Abstract: For an untwisted Wess--Zumino--Witten modular tensor category $\cC(\mathfrak g,k)$, let $r(\mathfrak g,k)$ be the number of simple objects and $\cD(\mathfrak g,k)$ its total quantum dimension. The vacuum-flux state on a torus divided into two cylinders has positive universal entropy contribution $Γ_{T^2}=2\log\cD$. We maximize this quantity over all simple Lie algebras and positive integral levels subject to $r(\mathfrak g,k)\leq R$. If $q_R=\log_2R$, then $Γ_{\max}(R)\sim[7ζ(3)/(4π^2)]q_R^2$ as $R\to\infty$.
  The sequence $Sp(2n)_n$ attains the asymptotic coefficient. At equal rank
  and level, the Fourier expansion of the type-$C$ root product loses its even modes, leaving $2π^{-2}\sum_{m\,\mathrm{odd}}m^{-3}=7ζ(3)/(4π^2)$. An entropy--spectral inequality proves optimality among the classical families, while rank--level duality and fixed-rank estimates control unbalanced and exceptional sequences.

</details>

### [96] [Observation of an excess at the top quark pair production threshold in the single-lepton channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19546)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究基于CMS实验在质心能量13 TeV下收集的质子-质子对撞数据，在单轻子加喷注末态中搜索接近运动学产生阈值的顶夸克-反顶夸克（tt̄）准束缚"顶夸克偶素"（toponium）态。利用顶夸克间的相对速度和两个角观测量，观测到相对于标准模型预测的显著超出，对应总截面为5.1±0.9 pb，观测显著度为6.1个标准差，与基于非相对论QCD的颜色单态赝标量顶夸克偶素简化模型一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索顶夸克偶素（toponium）态是检验非相对论量子色动力学（nonrelativistic QCD）预言的重要途径，同时该结果可为双轻子道中已报道的超出现象提供独立验证。

Method: 分析使用CMS实验在CERN LHC上收集的√s=13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb⁻¹），在含单个电子或μ子加喷注的末态中筛选事件。通过分析顶夸克与反顶夸克之间的相对速度以及两个对tt̄系统自旋和宇称敏感的角观测量，将观测结果与基于微扰QCD次次阶领头阶（NNLO）计算的标准模型预言进行比较。

Result: 观测到相对于标准模型预言的显著事件超出，对应总截面为5.1±0.9 pb，观测显著度达6.1个标准差。该超出与基于非相对论QCD启发的颜色单态赝标量顶夸克偶素简化模型相容，并为双轻子道中已报道的超出现象提供了独立确证。

Conclusion: 单轻子加喷注末态中的观测结果独立确证了双轻子道中顶夸克偶素候选态的证据，其显著度达到6.1个标准差，支持该超出可能来源于颜色单态赝标量toponium态的物理解释。

Abstract: A search is presented for top quark-antiquark ($\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$) quasi-bound ``toponium'' states, near the kinematic $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ production threshold in final states with a single electron or muon and jets. The study uses proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis examines the relative velocity between the top quark and antiquark, along with two angular observables sensitive to the parity and spin of the $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ system. A significant excess of events is observed relative to the standard model prediction for $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ production calculated at next-to-next-to-leading order in perturbative quantum chromodynamics (QCD). The excess corresponds to an observed total cross section of 5.1$\pm$0.9 pb and is consistent with a simplified model of a color singlet pseudoscalar toponium motivated by nonrelativistic QCD, with a resulting observed significance of 6.1 standard deviations. The result provides an independent confirmation of the excess reported in the dilepton channel.

</details>


### [97] [Search for top quark flavor-changing neutral currents in multilepton final states using an effective field theory approach in proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19547)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下开展了首个全局搜索顶夸克味改变中性流（FCNC）相互作用的研究，利用CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb⁻¹）中，通过双同号轻子或三轻子末态，同时对24个Wilson系数进行联合拟合，未观察到FCNC信号，并给出了迄今最严格的顶夸克FCNC衰变分支比上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 味改变中性流（flavor-changing neutral current, FCNC）相互作用涉及顶夸克，是超出标准模型新物理的敏感探针，但在顶夸克FCNC领域中此前缺乏对所有相关有效算符的全局联合约束。

Method: 在标准模型有效场论（standard model effective field theory, SMEFT）框架下，对所有相关的顶夸克FCNC算符进行单个全局拟合；数据分析基于CMS探测器在质心能量√s=13 TeV下采集的质子-质子碰撞数据，积分亮度为138 fb⁻¹；在双同号带电轻子或三轻子末态中探测信号，使用多变量判别量（multivariate discriminant）区分信号与背景，其分布进入似然拟合以确定24个Wilson系数的95%置信区间。

Result: 未观察到FCNC相互作用的证据；该分析首次对完整算符集给出同时约束，并通过双夸克-双轻子（two-quark–two-lepton）相互作用对顶夸克FCNC衰变分支比给出了迄今最严格的限制。

Conclusion: 该研究为LHC上顶夸克FCNC领域的未来测量与组合研究提供了框架，同时确立了目前最严格的顶夸克FCNC上限。

Abstract: We present a search for flavor-changing neutral current (FCNC) interactions involving the top quark in production and decay. This analysis establishes the first global search in the top quark FCNC sector within the standard model effective field theory where all relevant operators are constrained simultaneously in a single fit to data, providing a framework for future measurements and combinations at the LHC. The analysis uses proton$-$proton collision data collected at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV by the CMS detector, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Signal contributions are probed in final states with two same-sign charged leptons or three leptons. Multivariate discriminants are used to separate signal from background, and their distributions enter a likelihood fit to determine 95% confidence intervals for 24 Wilson coefficients. No evidence for FCNC interactions is observed. These results provide the first simultaneous constraints on the complete operator set and place the most stringent limits to date on FCNC top quark decay branching fractions via two-quark$-$two-lepton interactions.

</details>


### [98] [Heavy-flavour production and correlations in pp collisions: precision tests of pQCD and hadronisation with ALICE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19714)
*Deependra Sharma,for the ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了在质子-质子（pp）碰撞中重夸克强子产额的初步和最终测量结果，包括低横动量下B⁰介子产额截面、D介子产生，以及D⁰–J/ψ对伴随产生，用于检验微扰量子色动力学（pQCD）并约束重夸克产生、强子化和多部分子相互作用动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克（粲夸克和底夸克）主要产生于硬部分子散射中，其产额截面可在微扰量子色动力学（perturbative quantum chromodynamics, pQCD）框架下计算，从而为pQCD提供严格检验；同时，单次碰撞中两个粲强子的伴随产生可用于区分单部分子散射（single parton scattering, SPS）与双部分子散射（double parton scattering, DPS）过程，进而灵敏地探测多部分子相互作用（multiparton interaction）动力学。

Method: 在中快度下报告了即时D介子产生的初步测量结果，以及低至横向动量（transverse momentum）p_T = 1 GeV/c的B⁰介子产生截面的最终结果；通过计算与LHCb前向快度测量结果的比值来研究B介子产生的快度依赖。同时，在√s = 13.6 TeV的pp碰撞中测量了D⁰–J/ψ对的伴随产生，其中D⁰介子在中快度重建，J/ψ候选体在前向快度测量。

Result: 测量结果与pQCD计算和唯象模型（phenomenological models）进行了比较，为重夸克产生、强子化（hadronisation）以及多部分子相互作用动力学提供了关键的约束条件。

Conclusion: 这些测量为检验pQCD预言、理解重夸克产生与强子化机制，以及区分单部分子散射和双部分子散射过程提供了重要实验依据。

Abstract: Heavy quarks (charm and beauty) are predominantly produced in hard partonic scatterings, making their cross sections in proton--proton (pp) collisions calculable in perturbative quantum chromodynamics (pQCD) and thus providing stringent tests of pQCD. Furthermore, the associated production of two charm hadrons in a single collision offers a sensitive probe of multiparton interaction dynamics, distinguishing between single parton scattering (SPS) and double parton scattering (DPS) processes.
  In this contribution, preliminary measurements of prompt D-meson production are reported, together with the final results of the $\rm B^{0}$-meson production cross section down to $p_{\rm T}$ = 1 GeV/$c$ at midrapidity. The rapidity dependence of B-meson production is investigated by computing the ratio with respect to LHCb measurements at forward rapidity. The associated production of $\rm D^{0}$--$\mathrm{J}/ψ$ pairs in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV is presented as well, where $\rm D^{0}$ mesons are reconstructed at midrapidity, while $\mathrm{J}/ψ$ candidates are measured at forward rapidity. These measurements are compared with pQCD calculations and phenomenological models, providing crucial constraints on heavy-quark production, hadronisation, and multiparton interaction dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [The Search Budget of the BSM Resonance Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19764)
*Theresa Reisch,Tobias Golling*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过统计有效独立分辨单元来量化ATLAS反常标准模型共振谱（BSM resonance）搜索计划中的"寻找别处效应"（look-elsewhere effect）试验因子（trials factor）。已发表的记录涵盖104个质谱，共7.9×10³次"look"，对应5σ全局显著性需要局部Z=6.55；而完全组合式扫描（覆盖至多四个重构物体构成的全部质量，结合Run 2和Run 3数据）则需要3.6×10⁵次look和Z=7.11，即多付出46倍试验次数仅增加0.56σ代价。对于估计器本身不完善（如机器学习搜索）而无法枚举试验因子的情形，两阶段揭盲（two-stage unblinding）是更为灵敏的防护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据导向的扫描、异常搜索和普适搜索同时探测大量质谱，其中"寻找别处效应"设定了发现阈值。本文旨在基于公开数据，量化ATLAS BSM共振程序的试验因子，并评估完全组合式扫描所需的统计代价，以及机器学习搜索中无法枚举试验因子时的替代方案。

Method: 从公开输入数据出发，以有效独立分辨单元为单位统计ATLAS BSM共振程序的试验因子；在此基础上推算完全组合式扫描（每次选择一个事件级选择条件，扫描由至多四个重构物体构建的全部质量组合，并同时在Run 2和Run 3数据上定价）的等效试验次数。对于机器学习搜索中估计器不完善的情形，分析两阶段揭盲机制的灵敏度。

Result: 已发表记录扫描104个质谱，合计N_trials=7.9×10³，5σ全局发现所需局部Z=6.55。完全组合式扫描需3.6×10⁵次look，对应Z_local=7.11，即额外增加46倍试验次数折合0.56σ显著性代价。对于缺陷率与已发表的凸起检测网络相当的机器学习搜索，两阶段揭盲是更灵敏的程序。

Conclusion: 完全组合式扫描增加的试验因子代价相对有限（46倍次数仅折合0.56σ），表明此类扫描在实际中可行；而当试验因子无法枚举（如机器学习搜索）时，两阶段揭盲可作为防止虚假信号的有效保障，且灵敏度更优。

Abstract: Data-directed scans, anomaly searches and general searches probe many mass spectra at once, and for them the look-elsewhere effect sets the discovery bar. We count the trials factor of the ATLAS BSM resonance program in effective independent resolution elements, from public inputs alone, and then work out what a fully combinatorial scan would cost in the same units. The published record, summed over the 104 spectra it scans, amounts to $N_{\mathrm{trials}} = 7.9 \times 10^{3}$ looks, so a $5σ$ global discovery today costs a local $Z_{\mathrm{local}} = 6.55$. Scanning instead every mass built from up to four reconstructed objects, one event-level selection at a time and priced on Run 2 and Run 3 together, amounts to $3.6 \times 10^{5}$ looks and $Z_{\mathrm{local}} = 7.11$. A factor 46 more looks therefore costs $0.56σ$. If the trials factor cannot be enumerated because the estimator is itself imperfect, as in machine-learning searches, two-stage unblinding is a safeguard against its spurious signals: at the defect rate measured for a published bump-hunting network it is the more sensitive procedure.

</details>


### [100] [Distilling Normalizing Flows for Real-Time Anomaly Detection at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19912)
*Tara P. A. Tahseen,Noah Clarke Hall,Nikolaos Konstantinidis,Paula Martínez Suárez*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出将大规模归一化流（normalizing flow）的似然估计蒸馏到轻量级学生估计器中，使其可在FPGA硬件触发器上部署，从而克服大型强子对撞机（LHC）触发系统中标准流似然方法面临的延迟和资源限制，同时提升物理性能和降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化流是原理性的异常检测器，通过概率逐事件似然来选择异常。然而，极端延迟和资源约束阻碍了流似然方法在大型强子对撞机硬件触发系统中的部署。

Method: 作者将大型归一化流的似然蒸馏（distill）为轻量级学生估计器，用于在FPGA上部署；采用条件归一化流（conditional normalizing flow）以在输入特征缺失时实现精确似然估计；分别考虑决策树和神经网络两种学生模型，其中神经网络在先进量化（quantization）技术下进行优化。

Result: 同时提升似然质量并降低推理成本，相比现有基于流的方法，在物理性能和延迟方面均达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏结合轻量级学生模型与量化技术，可有效将归一化流似然部署到硬件触发器中，实现高性能低延迟的异常检测。

Abstract: Normalizing flows are principled anomaly detectors, selecting anomalies using a probabilistic per-event likelihood. Extreme latency and resource constraints have prevented the deployment of flow likelihoods within the hardware triggers at the Large Hadron Collider. We bypass these limitations by distilling the likelihood from a large normalizing flow into lightweight student estimators suitable for deployment on a field-programmable gate array. Our use of a conditional normalizing flow enables precise likelihood estimation in the presence of missing input features. Both decision tree and neural network students are considered, the latter optimized under advanced quantization techniques. By simultaneously improving likelihood quality and lowering inference cost, we demonstrate both state-of-the-art physics performance and latency compared with existing flow-based approaches.

</details>

### [101] [Dynamic Pseudogap Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19592)
*E. Z. Kuchinskii,M. V. Sadovskii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个由动态有限套叠矢量自旋涨落导致的赝能隙形成的微观理论。通过将电子耦合到经典高斯随机场，在阿贝尔近似下推导出单粒子格林函数的双重级数表示，并揭示了动态产生的退相干尺度Γ_eff，该尺度支配相干动力学边带与准静态涨落赝能隙两种不同赝能隙区域之间的渡越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赝能隙（pseudogap）现象是强关联电子系统中的核心问题，其形成机制尚未完全厘清。已有理论多采用静态或准静态涨落图像，缺乏统一的微观动态描述。

Method: 从描述电子耦合到经典高斯随机场（classical Gaussian random field）的哈密顿量出发，在精确SU(2)时间演化的阿贝尔（Abelian，可对易）近似下，推导出单粒子格林函数的显式双重级数表示。该传播子自然获得广义的Bogoliubov结构，每个随机散射历史由有效动态能隙表征，产生具有复Poisson权重的相干叠加动态展宽边带。

Result: 理论的核心结果是动态产生的退相干尺度Γ_eff，它控制两个不同赝能隙区域之间的渡越：当ω₀>Γ_eff时，涨落场被相干分辨，双重级数表示提供对动态边带形成的有控描述；当Γ_eff≥ω₀时，相干性逐步丧失，理论跨入由精确连分数解描述的准静态涨落能隙区域。对于不同模型参数的电子谱密度和态密度数值计算结果确认了该渡越行为在广泛参数范围内的成立。

Conclusion: 相干与准静态两种描述被解释为同一微观动态赝能隙模型的两个互补渐近极限，从而统一了此前看似不同的赝能隙理论框架。

Abstract: We formulate a microscopic theory of pseudogap formation generated by dynamic finite nesting vector ${\bf Q}$ fluctuations with characteristic oscillation frequency $ω_0$, and damping $γ$. Starting from a Hamiltonian describing electrons coupled to a classical Gaussian random field, we derive explicit double-series representations of the single-particle Green's function within an Abelian (commuting) approximation to the exact SU(2) time evolution. The resulting propagator naturally acquires a generalized Bogoliubov structure in which every stochastic scattering history is characterized by an effective dynamic gap, leading to a coherent superposition of dynamically broadened sidebands with complex Poisson weights. A central result of the theory is the emergence of a dynamically generated decoherence scale $Γ_{\rm eff}$ governing the crossover between two qualitatively different pseudogap regimes. For $ω_0>Γ_{\rm eff}$ the fluctuating field is resolved coherently and the double-series representation provides a controlled description of dynamic sideband formation. Conversely, when $Γ_{\rm eff}\gtrsimω_0$, coherence is progressively lost and the theory crosses over to the quasistatic fluctuating-gap regime described by the exact continued-fraction solution. The coherent and quasistatic descriptions are therefore interpreted as two complementary asymptotic limits of the same microscopic dynamic pseudogap model. The detailed results of numerical calculations for electron spectral density and density of states are presented for different sets of model parameters confirming this crossover over the broad range of model parameters.

</details>
