### [102] [Multivariate amplitude analysis of the cascade particle decays based on the Nearest Neighbors fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19267)
*I. V. Yeletskikh,A. O. Vasyukov*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于粒子衰变级联多维振幅分析（amplitude analysis）的最近邻（Nearest Neighbors）似然估计方法，通过为蒙特卡洛模拟事件赋予依赖理论拟合模型参数的权重来构建多维概率密度函数，避免了解析概率密度函数或逐次迭代重新生成模拟事件的需求，计算资源需求低且可高效多线程并行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多维振幅分析需要解析概率密度函数（p.d.f.）或在每一步拟合中重新生成N维概率密度函数，前者难以准确描述复杂重建效应，后者计算开销大、耗时长。因此需要一种既能精确考虑探测器重建效应又节省计算资源的方法。

Method: 采用最近邻估计方法实现似然函数：蒙特卡洛模拟事件被赋予依赖于理论拟合模型参数的权重，每个加权事件被视为在参数空间中对其邻域内的N维概率密度函数做出贡献，从而无需解析p.d.f.或逐次重新生成N维p.d.f.即可完成拟合。

Result: 结果表明该方法能够准确计入重建效应，仅需适度的CPU资源，且可被有效地多线程并行化处理，并给出了B介子衰变振幅分析的玩具示例加以验证。

Conclusion: 该方法为粒子衰变级联的多维振幅分析提供了一种计算高效且精确的替代方案，尤其适用于对重建效应要求准确描述且计算资源受限的场景。

Abstract: The Nearest Neighbors estimation of likelihood is implemented for the multivariate amplitude analysis of the cascades of particle decays. In this approach Monte Carlo simulated events that are used to describe data are assigned weights dependent on the parameters of the theoretical fitting model. Each of such weighted events is considered as contributing to N-dimensional probability density function in it's neighborhood in the parameter space. No analytic p.d.f. or cpu-intensive re-generation of N-dimensional p.d.f. at each fit step are required. The method allows accurate accounting for reconstruction effects, demands modest cpu resources and can be effectively multi-threaded. General setup of the fit as well as toy example of the amplitude analysis of B-meson decays are demonstrated.

</details>

### [103] [Proof of the hiding conjecture for Gaussian boson sampling with an arbitrary number of squeezed input modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19314)
*Laura Shou,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Victor Galitski,Sarah H. Miller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完成了高斯玻色采样（Gaussian boson sampling, GBS）中隐藏猜想（hiding conjecture）对任意挤压模式数 K 的证明，表明其对称积矩阵在总变差距离下接近复高斯矩阵；同时指出密度型实例生成方法在特定参数下失效，改用近似实例生成实现经典硬度归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯玻色采样是被提出用于演示量子优势（quantum advantage）的采样任务。证明其经典计算硬度需要完成隐藏猜想的严格证明，此前该猜想仅在部分参数条件下成立，本文旨在针对任意挤压模式数 K 完成证明。

Method: 作者证明了：对任意 K 和 N=o(√K)，由 M×M Haar 随机酉矩阵（Haar random unitary）U 的左上 N×K 子矩阵构成的对称积 MK^{-1/2}U_{NK}U_{NK}^T，在总变差距离（total variation distance）下同时接近两个目标分布：具有独立元素的 N×N 对称复高斯矩阵，以及 GG^T/√K（其中 G 为 N×K 独立同分布标准复高斯矩阵）。此外，作者分析了经典密度型实例生成方法的失效条件，并采用近似实例生成（approximate instance generating）替代。

Result: 完成隐藏猜想对任意挤压模式数 K 的证明；同时发现 [Aaronson and Arkhipov] 的密度型实例生成方法在 K=cM（c<1/2）时失败，需改用近似实例生成来实现隐藏过程，以支撑常规经典硬度归约（classical hardness reduction）。

Conclusion: 本文为高斯玻色采样的经典硬度论证补全了关键的隐藏猜想证明环节，同时指出了原有实例生成方法的局限性并提供了替代方案，为 GBS 量子优势的理论基础增添了完整性。

Abstract: Gaussian boson sampling (GBS) is a sampling task proposed to demonstrate quantum advantage. We consider Gaussian boson sampling on $M$ optical modes, with $K$ equally squeezed input modes and $N$ observed photon counts. We complete the proof of the hiding conjecture for Gaussian boson sampling with an arbitrary number of squeezers $K$, which is a part of the argument for classical hardness of GBS. In particular, we show that for any $K$ and $N=o(\sqrt{K})$, the symmetric product $MK^{-1/2}U_{NK}U_{NK}^T$, for $U_{NK}$ the top left $N\times K$ submatrix of an $M\times M$ Haar random unitary $U$, is close in total variation distance to both an $N\times N$ symmetric complex Gaussian matrix $\mathbf G$ with independent entries, and the symmetric product $GG^T/\sqrt{K}$ for $G$ an $N\times K$ matrix of iid standard complex Gaussians. We show however that the density-based instance generating method of [Aaronson and Arkhipov, Theory Comput. 9, 143 (2013), Lemma 5.8] used to efficiently implement a hiding procedure fails for Gaussian boson sampling with $K=cM$ if $c<1/2$. Instead we use approximate instance generating to implement the hiding for the usual classical hardness reduction.

</details>


### [104] [Thermal Throttling of Quantum State Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19315)
*Twesh Upadhyaya,T. C. Mooney,Yifan Hong,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子比特晶格上量子态传输（quantum state transfer）在热噪声影响下的基本极限，推导了对易子范数（commutator norms）增长所需的紧致下界，并针对一维幂律系统（1D power-law systems）证明态传输运行时间对温度随系统尺寸的标度关系高度敏感，将对数界改进为代数界，将此前精确态传输的结果推广到更实际的近似态传输（approximate state transfer）情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态传输是众多量子信息处理任务中的关键步骤，但实际器件中的不完美因素（尤其是中间辅助位点（ancilla sites）的热噪声）如何阻碍传输尚不清楚，理解其基本原理极限是一个亟待解决的重要开放问题。

Method: 作者推导了实现近似态传输所需的算符对易子范数增长（growth of commutator norms）的紧致下界，并将其专门应用于具有热辅助态的一维幂律相互作用系统。

Result: 研究发现态传输运行时间依赖于温度与系统尺寸的标度关系（scaling of temperature with system size），从而将对数界的下界估计改进为代数界。

Conclusion: 该工作将此前关于精确态传输（exact state transfer）的研究结果扩展至定性不同且更贴近实际应用的近似态传输领域，为理解热噪声下量子态传输的极限提供了新的理论约束。

Abstract: Quantum state transfer on qubit lattices is a crucial step in a panoply of quantum information processing tasks. Understanding its fundamental limits in the presence of practical imperfections remains a pressing open question. In particular, it is unclear how thermal noise in the intermediate ancilla sites impedes transfer. In this work, we derive tight lower bounds on the necessary growth of commutator norms to achieve approximate state transfer. Specializing to 1D power-law systems with thermal ancilla states, we demonstrate that state transfer runtimes depend sensitively on the scaling of temperature with system size, improving logarithmic bounds to algebraic ones. Our work extends previous results on exact state transfer to the qualitatively different and more practically relevant regime of approximate state transfer.

</details>


### [105] [Rethinking Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19370)
*Steven Rayan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以讲义形式发展出理解量子电路（quantum circuit）的四种相互关联的方式：从算子组合、图示演算、保护过程（结合纠错）到几何化（将连通性、拓扑与边界数据视为物理设计参数）。内容从比特与量子比特出发，经由 Deutsch 与 Grover 算法、字符串图（string diagrams）与 ZX 演算（ZX-calculus）、Knill–Laflamme 条件与同调表面码（homological surface codes），最终抵达双曲量子码（hyperbolic quantum codes）及超导电路实验，无需任何量子信息前置知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为量子电路提供一个多层级的、从操作到几何的统一解读框架，将量子计算、量子纠错与装置物理（device physics）联系起来。文中试图论证这些不同视角（代数、图解、纠错、几何）相互贯通，并评估超导装置实验在多大程度上实现了前文所介绍的基本 ZX 图。

Method: 以渐进递进的讲义结构展开：先以比特和量子比特为基础，引入 Deutsch 算法与 Grover 算法作为基本样例；随后采用图示化诠释，引入紧致闭字符串图（compact closed string diagrams）和 ZX 演算；再通过 Knill–Laflamme 条件、同调表面码等概念讨论量子电路纠错；最后探讨双曲量子码的数学性质，并报告原始研究（original research）——即超导电路模拟负曲率晶格的实验前景。

Result: 文章展示了四种阅读量子电路的方式如何在逻辑上逐层构建：从门操作的组合，到局部等式可作计算工具的图示，再到区分错误、综合征与逻辑自由度后的受保护过程，最终把连通性、拓扑和边界数据作为物理设计参数以形成几何层。文末评估了器件物理实验对早期 ZX 图的操作化程度，使全篇形成近乎闭环。

Conclusion: 作者认为这四种视角共同构成一种将几何层赋予量子电路的途径，且全文不预设任何量子信息、量子计算或量子纠错的前置知识。文中后段包含原创研究内容，数学程度随章节推进逐步加深。

Abstract: These notes develop four interconnected ways of reading a quantum circuit. A circuit for us begins as an operational composition of gates; then, it becomes a diagram whose local equalities may be used as calculations; next, it becomes a protected process once errors, syndromes, and logical degrees of freedom are separated; and finally, it becomes geometric when its connectivity, topology, and boundary data are treated as physical design parameters. The development begins at the level of bits and qubits before appealing to Deutsch's and Grover's algorithms as basic examples of quantum circuits. With the basics in hand, we interpret quantum circuits diagramatically, leading us to compact closed string diagrams and the ZX-calculus. After that, we consider how to correct quantum circuits by introducing the Knill--Laflamme condition, homological surface codes, and related concepts with a view towards thinking of these as operations on diagrams. The lectures eventually arrive at the properties of hyperbolic quantum codes and the prospect of physical superconducting circuits emulating the negatively-curved lattices needed to support those codes. These mathematical ideas and physical experiments, taken together, represent one way to impart a geometric layer onto quantum circuits. By the very end, we bring the ideas nearly full circle by assessing the extent to which these device physics experiments operationalize the basic ZX diagrams encountered much earlier in the story. While the later material reports on original research, and while the discussion becomes increasingly mathematical as the sections progress, no prior knowledge of quantum information, quantum computing, or quantum error correction is actually assumed.

</details>


### [106] [Efficient Classical Simulation of Weakly Interacting Fermion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19448)
*Chu Zhao,Iman Marvian,Yu Tong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多项式时间经典算法，用于模拟弱相互作用费米子系统的实时动力学。通过分析相互作用强度λ下微扰展开的收敛性，在λ|t|^{2D+1}=O(1)条件下（该条件与系统大小无关）可高效估计局部可观测量期望值；在更宽松的λ|t|=O(1)条件下可获得准多项式时间复杂度；若非相互作用部分具有安德森局域化（Anderson localization），则算法可扩展至λ|t|=O(1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱相互作用费米子系统的实时动力学模拟在量子多体物理中具有重要地位，但现有算法的采样复杂度缺乏严格的系统可控性保证。本文旨在识别一类物理条件——弱相互作用、几何局域性和局域化——使得该问题经典可解，同时提供严格的复杂度分析。

Method: 本文结合连续时间量子蒙特卡洛（continuous-time QMC）、图论量子蒙特卡洛（diagrammatic QMC）和Majorana传播（Majorana Propagation）的思想，提出了一种基于海森堡图像（Heisenberg picture）算符增长分析的新算法。该方法通过分析相互作用强度λ下微扰展开的收敛性质，使得采样方差在系统大小无关的弱相互作用区间内保持有界，从而严格控制采样复杂度。

Result: 在D维几何局域哈密顿量下，当λ|t|^{2D+1}=O(1)时，算法能在多项式时间内估计局部可观测量的期望值，且该条件与系统大小无关。若以归一化Frobenius范数近似时间演化的算符为目标，收敛区间可扩展至λ|t|=O(1)，对应准多项式运行时间。当非相互作用部分呈现安德森局域化时，多项式算法可扩展至λ|t|=O(1)（忽略多对数因子）。

Conclusion: 这些结果界定了弱相互作用、局域性和局域化可被用于经典可解的真实时费米子动力学的广泛参数区间，为弱相互作用费米子系统提供了一类可严格保证计算效率的经典模拟方案。

Abstract: We consider the task of simulating the real-time dynamics of weakly interacting fermionic systems. In particular, we focus on computing the expectation value of a local observable $A$ at time $t$. By analyzing the convergence of the perturbative expansion in the interaction strength $λ$ for the Heisenberg-picture observable, we propose a polynomial-time algorithm for estimating this expectation value in the weakly interacting regime $λ|t|^{2D+1}=\mathcal{O}(1)$, when the Hamiltonian is geometrically local on a $D$-dimensional lattice. Importantly, this condition is independent of the system size. If the goal is instead to approximate the time-evolved observable in normalized Frobenius norm, we extend the convergence regime to $λ|t|=\mathcal{O}(1)$ with quasi-polynomial runtime. When the non-interacting part exhibits Anderson localization, our polynomial-time algorithm can be extended up to $λ|t|=\mathcal{O}(1)$, modulo polylogarithmic factors. Our algorithm brings together ideas from continuous-time QMC, diagrammatic QMC, and Majorana Propagation, but with a new Heisenberg-picture operator-growth analysis that makes the sampling complexity rigorously controllable. This leads to provably efficient classical algorithms in regimes where the interaction is weak enough that the sampling variance remains bounded independently of system size. Together, these results identify broad regimes in which weak interactions, locality, and localization can be leveraged to make real-time fermionic dynamics classically tractable.

</details>


### [107] [Is the Quantum-Entangled Universe a Small World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19459)
*Gregory S. Duane*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出将部分纠缠（partial entanglement）作为宇宙中构建小世界网络（small-world network）的连接机制，并通过粒子平均自由程和纠缠连接概率分布的分析，发现除恒星和行星等最小尺度外，纠缠连接在几乎所有尺度上均表现出小世界或随机网络结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究部分纠缠是否足以提供足够的宇宙连接，以满足小世界的定义，从而从网络科学视角理解宇宙的全局连通性。

Method: 在给定空间类超曲面上定义粒子网络：任意两个粒子之间若存在经过少于某个小最大交互次数的交换粒子链，则建立长程连接。结合不同空间区域的粒子平均自由程（mean free path），计算纠缠连接概率随距离的分布。

Result: 在所有尺度（除了恒星和行星对应的最小尺度）上，均发现小世界或随机网络结构的证据；在最小尺度上，可能需要补充其他类型的连接才能完善整体的小世界图景。

Conclusion: 部分纠缠在绝大多数宇宙尺度上足以产生小世界结构，但在恒星和行星尺度上需要额外连接机制来补充，以实现完整的小世界网络。

Abstract: Partial entanglement may provide enough connections in the universe to satisfy the definition of a small world. To investigate this possibility, we define a network of particles on a given space-like hyper-surface with a long-range link between any two particles that are connected by a chain of exchanged particles involving less than some small maximum number of interactions. Considering the mean free paths of particles in different regions of space and the resulting probability distributions of entanglement connections vs. distance, we find evidence of small-world or random network structure on all but the smallest scales - corresponding to stars and planets - on which other types of connections would need to be added to complete the small world picture.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Integrability Preserving Renormalization-Group Protocol in Non-Hermitian Hamiltonians with Time-Dependent Interaction Strengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19519)
*Parameshwar R. Pasnoori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了含时可积模型的范畴，将其推广至非厄米（non-Hermitian）量子系统。作者利用广义贝特拟设框架证明，在非厄米含时系统中，保持可积性（integrability）的时间依赖相互作用强度的集合比重整化群（RG）协议所对应的集合更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知在含时量子系统中，保持可积性的含时相互作用强度与对应静态模型的重整化群轨迹一致（即RG协议），此前该结论仅限于厄米系统。作者旨在将这一范畴推广至非厄米含时量子模型，探索可积性约束在更一般系统中的表现形式。

Method: 采用近期提出的广义贝特拟设（generalized Bethe ansatz）框架[P. R. Pasnoori, Phys. Rev. B 112, L060409 (2025)]，对非厄米含时系统的可积性条件进行分析，比较静态模型中经历RG流的相互作用强度与RG不变的相互作用强度在对应含时模型中的时间依赖行为。

Result: 在非厄米含时系统中，静态模型中经历RG流的相互作用强度在对应含时模型中仍遵循各自的RG轨迹；而静态模型中RG不变的相互作用强度在含时模型中既可以保持常数，也可以具有由可积性约束的特定时间依赖形式。由此建立了保持可积性的含时强度集合严格大于RG协议对应集合的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在含时非厄米系统的背景下，可积性保持的时间依赖驱动方式比传统的RG协议更为丰富多样，拓展了含时可积模型的分类体系。

Abstract: It is well established that in time-dependent quantum systems, integrability preserving time-dependent interaction strengths are identical to the renormalization group (RG) trajectories of the corresponding static model when time `$t$' in the driven model is identified with the logarithm of the cutoff `$\logΛ$' of the static model. We refer to this integrability preserving driving as the RG protocol. In this work we extend the class of time-dependent integrable models to include non-Hermitian quantum models with time-dependent interaction strengths. Using the recently formulated generalized Bethe ansatz framework [P. R. Pasnoori, Phys. Rev. B 112, L060409 (2025)], we show that the constraints imposed by integrability are more general: The interaction strengths of the static model that flow in the RG follow the respective RG trajectories in the corresponding time-dependent model as described above. In addition, the interaction strengths of the static model that are RG invariant can either be constant or have a specific time-dependence in the corresponding time-dependent model which is constrained by integrability. Thus we establish that in the context of time-dependent non-Hermitian systems, the set of integrability preserving time-dependent strengths is larger than the set corresponding to the RG protocol.

</details>


### [109] [A charge selection rule fixes what a squeezed-light reservoir computer can compute and afford](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19668)
*Daniel Soh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究光学量子储层计算（optical quantum reservoir computing）中读数代价与能力的关系，发现读取一个光学量子储层的成本随特征阶数超指数增长，其能力由探测器而非光学系统决定。通过守恒律分析，揭示了不同读数方案下储层可达的函数空间与代价，并在硬件保真的数字孪生中验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储层计算在参数化泵浦相位中编码数据，但其可读性与读取成本之间的根本约束尚不清晰。理解探测器与光学之间的分工对于设计高效的量子储层计算架构至关重要。

Method: 本文利用配对光子交换（pairwise photon exchange）所遵从的整数相位电荷守恒律，从理论上刻画不同阶数读数（order-D readout）可达的函数空间；采用一阶零差读数（homodyne readout）分析弱压缩族上的通用性；并通过未制造硬件（no device was built）的数字化双胞胎（digital twin）进行验证。

Result: 推导出以下结果：阶数D读数恰好可达至多含D个单位电荷核的装配；一次零差读数在弱压缩族上对衰减记忆泛函具有通用性，且每发（shot）成本随精度多项式增长；在固定压缩下，有限阶数无法到达所有扇区，且距离可计算；每阶探测器的每发方差固定。在开放RF语料库上，同等光子数下位移编码控制比电荷代数预测损失17.7个准确度点。

Conclusion: 光学量子储层的可读能力由探测器的物理约束（电荷守恒）决定，而非光学非线性本身；非线性的贡献在于光学部分，探测器每阶方差恒定。这为量子储层计算的架构设计与成本-能力权衡提供了守恒律层面的统一理论框架。

Abstract: Reading an optical quantum reservoir costs repetitions growing super-exponentially with feature order: what it can afford is set by its detector, not its optics. For reservoirs encoding data in a parametric pump's phase, one conservation law fixes what is readable and what it costs. Pairwise photon exchange conserves an integer phase charge: across an ensemble of input masks, order-D readout reaches exactly the assemblies of at most D unit-charge kernels; degree-one homodyne readout is universal for fading-memory functionals along weak-squeezing families, at shot cost polynomial in accuracy; and at fixed squeezing no finite degree reaches every sector, at a computable distance. Nonlinearity sits in the optics, not the detector, whose per-shot variance is fixed at every order. In a hardware-faithful digital twin-no device was built-the rule is visible: on an open RF corpus a displacement-encoded control at identical photon number loses 17.7 accuracy points, as the charge algebra predicts.

</details>


### [110] [Geometric phase of open paths and a geodesic-selection rule at a level degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19679)
*Hyeonseok Yang,Changsuk Noh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在封闭形式下解决了开放路径几何相位（open-path geometric phase）中测地线规则（geodesic rule）的歧义问题：当控制场扫过级简并点时，使用无坐标单极子联络（coordinate-free monopole connection）定义开放路径立体角，并证明只有唯一一条闭合测地线（即密切平面内的大圆）才满足几何相位与封闭立体角之间的相等关系，从而将启发式的闭合规则转化为可计算的处方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当量子比特（qubit）、极化态或自旋-1/2系统的控制场扫过能级简并时，其方向在布洛赫球（Bloch sphere）上描绘出一条端点对径的开放曲线。此时开放路径几何相位的测地线规则存在歧义：有无数条测地线可闭合该路径，且不同闭合方式围成的立体角各不相同，亟需消除这一歧义。

Method: 采用无坐标单极子联络在内部定义开放路径立体角Ω[C]；通过将简并点位移εû来闭合路径，得到闭合立体角Ω(εû)=Ω[C]+2α+O(ε)的解析表达式，其中α为û横分量的方位角，并据此证明只有密切平面中曲率所提供的唯一大圆闭合适用于立体角-相位恒等式。

Result: 几何相位与封闭立体角的恒等式仅对唯一一条闭合测地线成立，即α=0时的密切平面大圆。Berry的π在位移反向下不变，以及反射对称下±π/2的值作为推论得出；有限温度Uhlmann相位的纯态极限自动选择密切平面闭合。

Conclusion: 该结果将开放路径文献中长期依赖的启发式闭合规则转化为明确可计算的处方，为开放路径几何相位的计算提供了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: When the control field of a qubit, a polarization state, or a spin-$\tfrac12$ system is swept through a level degeneracy, its direction traces an open curve on the Bloch sphere whose endpoints are antipodal, and the geodesic rule for the open-path geometric phase becomes ambiguous: infinitely many geodesics close the path, and different closures enclose different solid angles. We resolve this ambiguity in closed form. A coordinate-free monopole connection defines the open-path solid angle $Ω[C]$ intrinsically, and displacing the degeneracy by $ε\uhat$ closes the path with enclosed solid angle $Ω(ε\uhat)=Ω[C]+2α+O(ε)$, where $α$ is the azimuth of the transverse part of $\uhat$ measured from the principal normal of the control curve at the crossing. The identity between geometric phase and enclosed solid angle therefore holds for exactly one closing geodesic---the great circle in the osculating plane ($α=0$)---supplied by the curvature at the degeneracy. Berry's $π$ invariant under reversal of the displacement and the values $\pmπ/2$ under a reflection symmetry follow as corollaries, and the pure-state limit of the finite-temperature Uhlmann phase selects the osculating-plane closure automatically, turning the heuristic closing rules of the open-path literature into a computable prescription.

</details>


### [111] [Krylov Tomography and Finite-Uncertainty Certification of Exceptional-Point Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19761)
*Aritra Ghosh,M. Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Krylov层析（Krylov tomography）框架，用于回答奇异点（exceptional point, EP）动力学中激发可访问程度、探测器可区分的响应以及缺失信号是否真实缺失等问题。该框架利用时间分辨数据和具有明确不确定性限度的模型，既能界定由偏移引起的对精确EP行为偏离的程度，又通过有限精度模拟的红失谐光机（optomechanical）模型进行了示例验证，其中二阶相干度扇区包含三阶EP，实证了制备敏感的二维和三维方向访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异点能产生显著响应，但定位奇异点自身无法揭示：激发对其动力学访问了多少、探测器能区分哪些响应、以及缺失信号究竟是真实缺失还是未被检测到。这一信息缺口构成了研究动机。

Method: 引入Krylov层析，一种兼顾制备与测量效应的框架，使用时间分辨数据（time-resolved data）和带有明确不确定性限度的模型来回答上述问题，同时给出由偏移导致的精确EP行为偏离的上界。作为示例，对红失谐光机模型进行有限精度（finite-precision）模拟，该模型二阶相干度扇区包含三阶EP。

Result: 有限精度模拟表明，在包含三阶EP的光机模型中，可以认证制备敏感的二维和三维方向访问，验证了Krylov层析框架在非厄米系统（non-Hermitian systems）中的有效性。

Conclusion: Krylov层析将EP结构与有限精度测量连接起来，为非厄米系统提供了普适框架，弥补了奇异点理论上可预测响应与实验上有限精度观测之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) can produce striking responses, but locating one does not reveal how much of its dynamics an excitation accesses, which responses a detector distinguishes, or whether a missing signal is absent or undetected. We introduce Krylov tomography, a preparation- and measurement-aware framework that uses time-resolved data and models with stated uncertainty limits to answer these questions, while also bounding offset-induced departures from exact-EP behavior. As an explicit illustration of the general framework, we present finite-precision simulations of a red-sideband optomechanical model, whose second-moment coherence sector contains a third-order EP, certifying preparation-sensitive access to two and three directions. Krylov tomography thus bridges EP structure and finite-precision measurements, providing a general framework for non-Hermitian systems.

</details>


### [112] [Dissipation-enhanced scrambling in the SYK model coupled to a lossy cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19310)
*Pietro Pelliconi,Bastien Lapierre,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在耗散（dissipation）时Yukawa-SYK模型的动力学行为，该模型将N个Majorana费米子与R=γN个玻色子耦合。通过分析晚时间费米子弛豫率和Lyapunov指数，发现对于任意耗散率κ，Lyapunov指数始终为正，且在p>2时甚至随κ增大而增大；同时识别出一个临界玻色-费米子比γc≈2/p²划分了不同的动力学区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期量子模拟器实现SYK模型的实验提案对无序量子系统的实际动力学行为提出了新问题，尤其是耗散效应（如玻色子泄漏）如何影响SYK模型的混沌特性尚未被充分理解。本研究旨在通过Lindblad主方程框架探究耗散下Yukawa-SYK模型的动力学特征。

Method: 采用Lindblad主方程（Lindblad master equation）描述耗散，将玻色子泄漏建模为速率为κ的过程。系统初始化为稳态，随后分别通过数值求解和p大时的解析方法分析大N理论，计算晚时间费米子弛豫率与Lyapunov指数。

Result: 发现丰富的动力学行为谱系：Lyapunov指数在所有κ取值下保持为正，且在p>2时可随κ增大而增长；p=2情形（对应QED）介于完全混沌（p>2）与可积（p=1）之间，具有特殊性质；识别出临界比值γc≈2/p²划分不同动力学区域。

Conclusion: 耗散并不必然抑制SYK模型的混沌特征，甚至在特定条件下可增强Lyapunov指数。p=2的QED情形具有独特地位，且存在一个普适的临界比值γc标志着动力学转变。

Abstract: We study the Yukawa-Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model, a disordered model of $N$ Majorana fermions and $R=γN$ bosons in which the bosons are linearly coupled to independent realizations of SYK $p$-body interactions, in the presence of dissipation, modeled by a Lindblad master equation. Motivated by recent proposals for implementing SYK models in quantum simulators, we focus on bosonic leakage at rate $κ$. Initializing the system in the steady state, we analyze the late-time fermionic relaxation rate and the Lyapunov exponent, solving the large-$N$ theory both numerically and for $p$ large, finding a rich landscape of dynamical behaviors. Most notably, the Lyapunov exponent remains positive for every value of $κ$ and, for $p>2$, can even grow as $κ$ increases. The QED case $p=2$, which lies between the fully chaotic regime $p>2$ and the integrable case $p=1$, exhibits special features. We also identify a critical value of the boson-to-fermion ratio $γ_c \approx 2/p^2$ separating distinct dynamical regimes.

</details>


### [113] [Simulating Black Hole Thermality and Interior Scrambling on a Superconducting Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19318)
*Ryan Smith,Ewan Forbes,Iason Sofos Andrew Hallam,Jiannis Pachos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM超导量子硬件上实现了手性自旋链黑洞模拟器，在统一的微观框架内同时探测了半经典视界物理和相互作用量子 scrambing（量子混沌散射），通过观测色散关系演化、Hawking热性动力学估计器以及从自由费米子到强相互作用的算子展宽转变，建立了研究黑洞物理的通用可编程量子平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子硬件为研究黑洞视界几何、Hawking热性和相互作用量子 scramblng 提供了潜在的平台，但缺乏一个能在统一微观框架内同时探测半经典视界物理与量子 scramblng 的可编程模拟方案。

Method: 在IBM超导量子硬件上实现手性自旋链（chiral spin-chain）黑洞模拟器，通过测量外部、视界和过倾斜内部三个区域的色散关系来重现有效光锥结构；通过在视界内制备局域激发并监测外部格点的密度响应来估计Hawking温度；通过连续调节相互作用区分自由费米子极限下的非指数算子展宽与强相互作用手性区间的类Lyapunov OTOC（out-of-time-order correlator）衰减。采用基于同一母手性模型（包括其平均场和坐标等价XY描述）导出的可观测特定Floquet（Floquet）电路来降低电路深度。

Result: 实验成功复现了有效光锥结构跨三个区域的预测演化；通过视界内局域激发的密度响应观测到峰值到达时间与表面引力之间的反比关系，建立了Hawking温度的校准动力学估计器；在连续调节相互作用的实验中，观察到了自由费米子极限的非指数算子展宽与强相互作用手性区间的Lyapunov类OTOC衰减之间的清晰区分。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的、可编程的量子平台，用于在量子硬件上研究视界几何、Hawking热性和相互作用 scramblng，验证了利用可观测特定Floquet电路在降低电路深度的同时保持物理相关性的可行性。

Abstract: We implement a chiral spin-chain black hole simulator on IBM superconducting quantum hardware and probe, within a common microscopic framework, both semiclassical horizon physics and interacting quantum scrambling. We first measure the dispersion relation across the exterior, horizon and over-tilted interior regimes, reproducing the predicted evolution of the effective light-cone structure. To probe Hawking thermality, we prepare a localised excitation inside the horizon and monitor its density response at an exterior site, observing the predicted inverse relation between the peak arrival time and the surface gravity, thereby establishing a calibrated dynamical estimator of the Hawking temperature. Beyond the semiclassical regime, we continuously tune the interactions and distinguish non-exponential operator spreading in the free-fermion limit from Lyapunov-like OTOC decay in the strongly interacting chiral regime. These measurements use observable-specific Floquet circuits derived from the same parent chiral model, including its mean-field and coordinate-equivalent XY descriptions, to reduce circuit depth while preserving the physics relevant to each probe. Our results provide a unified programmable platform for studying horizon geometry, Hawking thermality and interacting scrambling on quantum hardware.

</details>


### [114] [A Zoology of Quantum Turing Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20151)
*Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子图灵斑图（quantum Turing pattern）的形态学谱系，在同一个林德布拉德方程（Lindblad equation）中可产生条纹、斑点、孔洞、迷宫和缺陷等多种形态。以稳定的条纹形态为定量案例，发现形态学判据与高斯目击（Gaussian witness）消失之间存在参数化分离，且见证阈值随晶格尺寸N的倒数下降，形态阈值与见证阈值的比值随N增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子图灵斑图的完整形态学谱系（morphology atlas），并定量刻画形态学特征与量子非经典性见证（Gaussian witness）之间的关系，理解二者在参数空间中是否具有可分离性。

Method: 采用完全正定林德布拉德晶格（completely positive Lindblad lattice）模型，通过解析和数值手段分析稳定条纹形态下的形态学阈值（morphology threshold）与两种高斯目击阈值（Gaussian witness thresholds），并进行不同晶格尺寸N下的标度分析（scaling analysis）。

Result: 可见的图灵条纹可在与同一k*模相关联的两种高斯目击余量（Gaussian witness margins）越过零点后仍然存在；确切壳层（exact shell）上的见证阈值随N^{-1}衰减；条纹向列阈值（stripe nematic threshold）在固定晶格尺寸、时间窗口和形态学判据下趋于非零值；因此形态阈值与见证阈值之比随N增长。

Conclusion: 形态学特征与高斯非经典性证据在参数上可分离，二者可通过成像（imaging）和动量分辨协方差测量（momentum-resolved covariance measurements）分别探测，表明量子图灵斑图的形态判据不必然伴随经典高斯目击的消失。

Abstract: We explore quantum Turing pattern zoology, where the same Lindblad equation supports a morphology atlas of stripes, spots, holes, labyrinths, and defects. The stable stripe species provides a quantitatively controlled case in which morphology and Gaussian witness loss separate parametrically. In particular, visible Turing stripes can remain after two Gaussian witness margins associated with the same $k_*$ mode cross zero in a completely positive Lindblad lattice. The witness thresholds on the exact shell fall as $\mathcal{N}^{-1}$. The stripe nematic threshold tends to a nonzero value at fixed lattice size, time window, and morphology criterion. The ratio of the morphology threshold to either witness threshold therefore grows with $\mathcal{N}$. Imaging and momentum-resolved covariance measurements probe these sectors separately.

</details>


### [115] [Rigorous existence and location of quantum phase transitions in lattice Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20209)
*Massimo Ostilli,Carlo Presilla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了可将量子相变（QPT）解释为态空间凝聚的理论框架，通过推广先前研究中的论证方法，证明了一般单参数晶格哈密顿量中QPT的存在性并给出了其位置的下界估计。该扩展同时涵盖二阶QPT，并以横场伊辛模型作为显式示例，提出在典型物理条件下，晶格系统中的任何QPT均可被解释为态空间凝聚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（Ostilli 和 Presilla，2021）仅针对一类特定的QPT建立了态空间凝聚解释，其适用范围有限。作者希望将这一理论框架推广到更一般的单参数晶格哈密顿量，并证明其有效性覆盖包括二阶在内的更广泛QPT类别。

Method: 通过推广先前发表于Phys. Rev. Lett.的论证方法（原有方法基于简单的下界估计），将态空间凝聚解释从特定QPT类别扩展到一般单参数晶格哈密顿量。作者利用下界（simple bounds）确定QPT的存在及其位置，并以横场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model）作为二阶QPT的显式示例来验证拓展后的理论框架。

Result: 证明了一般单参数晶格哈密顿量中QPT的存在性，并可通过简单下界确定其位置；该证明涵盖了二阶QPT的情形，横场伊辛模型为其提供了具体验证案例。

Conclusion: 在物理背景下通常满足的条件下，晶格系统中发生的任何QPT都可能被解释为态空间中的凝聚现象，该理论框架具有普适性。

Abstract: We extend the analysis of the class of quantum phase transitions (QPTs) that can be interpreted as condensations in state space, first introduced in [M. Ostilli and C. Presilla, J. Phys. A 54, 055005 (2021)], by generalizing the arguments of [M. Ostilli and C. Presilla, Phys. Rev. Lett. 127, 040601 (2021)] to prove the existence and determine the location (via simple bounds) of QPTs in general one-parameter lattice Hamiltonians. Unlike our original formulation, this extension also encompasses second-order QPTs, for which we provide the explicit example of the transverse-field Ising model. Our analysis suggests that, under conditions typically satisfied in physical contexts, any QPT taking place in lattice systems can be interpreted as a condensation in state space.

</details>


### [116] [Renormalization group ontology in quantum foundations: a non-interacting spin-0 toy model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19458)
*Gary Kapilevich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩充本体论（expanded ontology）的量子场论（quantum field theory, QFT）实在论反ψ解释，该本体论受重整化群（renormalization group）启发。通过经典非相互作用标量场玩具模型，在规范系综（canonical ensemble）下证明观察者在特定极限条件下将看到QFT，并以逆重整化群（inverse renormalization group, IRG）为核心工具，给出普朗克常数随固定点流动、依赖温度及观察者的新定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为量子场论提供一种实在论的、反对波函数（anti-psi）本体论的解释，以解决量子基础问题，并为发展新型QFT提供处方。

Method: 采用一个受重整化群启发的扩充本体论框架，以经典非相互作用标量场玩具模型为基础，在规范系综中进行分析；利用逆重整化群（IRG）作为核心工具，既用于解释该本体论，也用于构造新的QFT示例。

Result: 证明在该扩充本体论中，观察者在一定极限条件下会观测到QFT；给出普朗克常数（Planck's constant）的新定义，该常数随趋近固定点（fixed point）而流动，以温度形式表达，并依赖于所研究的观察者。

Conclusion: 该思路有望为量子基础研究提供新洞见，同时为开发新型QFT提供方法论指引。

Abstract: A realist, anti-psi interpretation of quantum field theory is introduced, based on an expanded ontology that was inspired by the renormalization group. A goal will be to work with a classical non-interacting scalar field "toy model" in the canonical ensemble and show that an observer in this expanded ontology, under certain limiting conditions, will see a QFT. The inverse renormalization group (IRG) will be key in both interpreting this expanded ontology and furnishing novel examples of QFTs. This will lead to a definition of Planck's constant that flows as we head towards a fixed point, is expressed in terms of a temperature, and depends on the observer being studied in this expanded ontology. Our hope is that this approach will lead both to insights into quantum foundations and to prescriptions for developing new QFTs.

</details>


### [117] [Analytical Solutions of the Generalized Klein-Gordon Oscillator in Som-Raychaudhuri Space-Time via the Extended Nikiforov-Uvarov Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20273)
*Hale Karayer,Tolga Celik,Dogan Demirhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于扩展的Nikiforov-Uvarov方法，在Som-Raychaudhuri时空背景中、均匀磁场存在下，解析求解了广义Klein-Gordon振子问题，获得了线性和Cornell势模型下的精确本征态解，能量本征值及相应的本征函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空背景下求解相对论性波动方程的解析解是量子场论与引力交叉领域的重要课题，尤其是磁场与势场共同作用下的Klein-Gordon方程，此前缺乏系统的解析处理。

Method: 采用广义Nikiforov-Uvarov（Nikiforov-Uvarov）方法求解含有均匀磁场的Som-Raychaudhuri时空中广义Klein-Gordon振子问题，分别对线性势（linear potential）和Cornell势（Cornell potential）两类模型进行解析求解。

Result: 获得了两种势模型下精确的（exact）本征态解，确定了对应的能量本征值（energy eigenvalues），并对关联的本征函数（eigenfunctions）进行了分析和图形化展示。

Conclusion: 研究证明了扩展Nikiforov-Uvarov方法在弯曲时空背景下求解含磁场Klein-Gordon方程的适用性与有效性，为相关量子系统提供了精确的解析解。

Abstract: In this study, we present an analytical approach based on the extended Nikiforov-Uvarov method to solve the generalized Klein-Gordon oscillator in the presence of a uniform magnetic field within the Som-Raychaudhuri space-time. Exact eigenstate solutions are obtained for two distinct potential models, namely the linear potential and the Cornell potential. The corresponding energy eigenvalues are determined, and the associated eigenfunctions are analyzed and illustrated graphically.

</details>


### [118] [Strongly coupled atom-cavity systems under boundary modulation: simulating gravitational-wave effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19333)
*Patryk Michalski,Jerzy Paczos,Navdeep Arya,Magdalena Zych*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了引力波背景下强耦合腔中原子-场相互作用的调制效应，证明模式频率的时间调制可在拉比振荡中产生共振增强，并在原子跃迁概率上留下可测量的印记，为在量子光学系统中探测类广义相对论效应提供了实验上可行的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子效应与广义相对论效应同时可观测的平台之一是引力波背景中与电磁场相互作用的原子。理解时空度规变化对原子发射谱的影响，特别是探索其在强耦合条件下的可观测效应，是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 通过模拟边界条件（如移动腔镜）来模拟平面引力波引起的模式频率随时间调制，并在强原子-腔耦合（strong atom-cavity coupling）区域——即拉比振荡（Rabi oscillations）发生的区域——解析分析这种调制对原子-场相互作用的影响。

Result: 解析证明该调制被共振增强，导致原子跃迁概率中出现可测量的印记。

Conclusion: 该方案建立了一个现实且实验可及的平台，用于在量子光学系统中探测类广义相对论（general relativistic）效应对应物。

Abstract: One of the proposed platforms in which both quantum and general relativistic effects can become observable is an atom interacting with the electromagnetic field in a gravitational-wave background. The periodic modulation of field modes induced by variations of the spacetime metric modifies the atomic emission spectrum. Notably, the temporal modulation of the mode-frequency induced by a plane gravitational wave can be simulated through modulated boundary conditions, such as moving cavity mirrors. We analyze the impact of this modulation on atom-field interactions in the strong atom-cavity coupling regime, where Rabi oscillations occur. We show analytically that the modulation is resonantly enhanced, leading to measurable imprints in the atomic transition probability. This establishes a realistic and experimentally accessible platform for probing analogue general relativistic effects in quantum optical systems.

</details>


### [119] [Statistical Mechanics of Non-Abelian Learnability Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19325)
*Ruochen Ma,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个具有SU(2)对称性的1+1维受监测量子动力学理论，发现"自旋锐化"转变与纠缠转变重合为同一转变，并预测两种相中总自旋的测量学习时间分别为$t\sim L^3$（长程有序相）和$t\sim L^2$（无<unk>序相）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监测的多体量子系统可发生尖锐的可学习性转变，但当动力学守恒非阿贝尔荷（如SU(2)自旋）时，观测者如何学习总电荷仍是一个未解决的重要问题。与阿贝尔情形不同，SU(2)对称读出是非对易的融合测量，使学习成为一个真正的量子推断问题。

Method: 提出一个由副本配对场和携带SU(2)荷的扩散背景扇区（动力学指数$z=2$）组成的有效副本环模型，该背景扇区在整个相图中保持无质量。分析由大环逸度展开控制。

Result: 理论预测"自旋锐化"转变与纠缠转变重合为单一转变。配对场有序时产生体积律纠缠并遮蔽背景扇区，总自旋学习时间为$t\sim L^3$；配对场无序时，背景扇区单独给出对数纠缠，学习时间呈扩散标度$t\sim L^2$。

Conclusion: 该理论表明非阿贝尔荷守恒的受监测动力学中存在两个截然不同的相，对应不同的学习时间和纠缠标度，且两种相变合并为单一转变。

Abstract: Monitored many-body quantum systems can undergo sharp learnability transitions characterized by how much information can be learned by the observer. When the dynamics conserves a non-Abelian charge, such as an $SU(2)$ spin, understanding how the observer learns the total charge remains an outstanding problem. Unlike the Abelian case, where charge measurements on distinct sites commute, the $SU(2)$-symmetric readouts are noncommuting fusion measurements, making learning a genuinely quantum inference problem. In this work, we propose a theory of $1+1d$ monitored quantum dynamics with $SU(2)$ symmetry, and show that it can be described by an effective replicated loop model comprised of a replica-pairing field and a diffusive ($z=2$) background sector that carries the $SU(2)$ charge and remains gapless throughout the phase diagram. Our theory predicts that the "spin-sharpening'' and entanglement transitions coincide as a single transition. Ordering of the pairing field produces volume-law entanglement and hides the background sector from measurements, leading to a learning time of $t\sim L^{3}$ for the total spin. When the pairing field disorders, the background sector alone gives logarithmic entanglement and a diffusive learning time $t\sim L^{2}$. Our analysis is controlled by a large-loop-fugacity expansion.

</details>


### [120] [Glassy dynamics with softened kinetic constraints on a noisy quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19335)
*Marcel Cech,Igor Lesanovsky,Federico Carollo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在超导量子处理器上利用中途测量（mid-circuit measurement）技术实现了Floquet-East模型，通过对测量轨迹的分析观察到动力学异质性（dynamical heterogeneity）——玻璃态动力学（glassy dynamics）的标志性特征，并揭示了从面积主导到周长主导尺度行为的交叉，表明系统接近动力学一阶相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中途测量能够直接获取轨迹级（trajectory-level）可观测量，揭示在多体系统中被系综平均量所掩盖的动力学结构；本研究旨在利用这一能力在量子处理器上实现并研究Floquet-East模型，从而在中等规模含噪量子（NISQ）设备上探究玻璃态动力学等复杂多体现象。

Method: 在超导量子处理器上实现Floquet-East模型，结合中途测量、动力学约束幺正操作（kinetically constrained unitary operations）与硬件噪声；通过时间与空间分辨的中途测量获取轨迹数据，并分析非活性时空区域（inactive space-time regions）的出现概率及其尺度标度行为。

Result: 在测量轨迹中识别出动力学异质性这一玻璃态动力学标志；非活性时空区域概率呈现出从面积主导（area-dominated）到周长主导（perimeter-dominated）标度的交叉，该交叉是玻璃的特征性质，且与动力学一阶相变的邻近性相关。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当前中等规模含噪量子设备可作为可扩展的实验平台（scalable testbeds），用于在测量轨迹层面研究关联多体现象。

Abstract: Mid-circuit measurements provide direct access to trajectory-level observables, revealing dynamical structures in many-body systems that are invisible in ensemble-averaged quantities. We exploit this capability to realize and study an instance of the Floquet-East model on a superconducting quantum processor. Here, the combination of mid-circuit measurements, kinetically constrained unitary operations and hardware noise gives rise to intricate many-body phenomena. Analyzing trajectories obtained from temporally and spatially resolved mid-circuit measurements, we identify dynamical heterogeneity --- a hallmark of glassy dynamics. We quantify this emergent behavior by studying the probability of finding inactive space-time regions of a given size. This quantity displays a crossover from an area- to perimeter-dominated scaling, which is a characteristic property of glasses and is associated with the proximity to a dynamical first-order phase transition. Our results establish current noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices as scalable testbeds for investigating correlated many-body phenomena at the level of measurement trajectories.

</details>


### [121] [Multipolar Light-Matter Hamiltonians in Symmetry-Breaking Photonic Vacuums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19336)
*Liu Yang,Jiadu Lin,Qing-Dong Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了当光子真空破坏空间反演对称性或时间反演对称性时，传统多极哈密顿量（multipolar Hamiltonian）会被定性修改。作者通过显式应用 Power-Zienau-Woolley 变换，推导了两种手性光子环境中的多极哈密顿量，并展示其产生的对称性依赖项可导致特征性光谱位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多极哈密顿量在描述光与物质相互作用时假设光子真空具有完整的对称性，但当真空打破反演或时间反演对称性时，该框架可能失效。作者旨在建立一种普适框架来描述手性量子电动力学中的光-物质相互作用，并识别相关的对称性依赖效应。

Method: 作者显式应用 Power-Zienau-Woolley 变换，推导了两种理想化手性光子环境中的多极哈密顿量：空间手性真空（破坏反演对称性）和时间手性真空（破坏时间反演对称性）。以腔中的俘获氢样原子和带电谐振子作为最小示例进行分析。

Result: 在空间手性情况下，变换产生了反演对称性破坏的自能项（self-energy）；在时间手性情况下，则产生了额外的类塞曼能（Zeeman-like energy）。这些对称性依赖的项在最小示例中导致特征性光谱位移。

Conclusion: 该工作为描述手性量子电动力学中的光-物质相互作用提供了普适框架，并识别出腔中嵌入的原子、分子和量子材料上相关的对称性依赖效应。

Abstract: We show that the conventional multipolar Hamiltonian is qualitatively modified when the photonic vacuum breaks inversion or time-reversal symmetry. By explicitly applying the Power-Zienau-Woolley transformation, we derive the resulting multipolar Hamiltonians for two idealized chiral photonic environments: a spatial-chiral vacuum, which breaks inversion symmetry, and a temporal-chiral vacuum, which breaks time-reversal symmetry. In the spatial-chiral case, the transformation generates an inversion-breaking self-energy, whereas in the temporal-chiral case it produces an additional Zeeman-like energy. Using a trapped hydrogen-like atom and a charged harmonic oscillator in cavities as minimal examples, we show that these symmetry-dependent terms lead to characteristic spectral shifts. Our work provides a general framework for describing light-matter interactions in chiral quantum electrodynamics and identifying the associated symmetry-dependent effects on cavity-embedded atoms, molecules, and quantum materials.

</details>


### [122] [Stability and squeezing of the three-photon degenerate parametric down-conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19494)
*Vinícius V. Seco,Alencar J. de Faria*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了腔内简并三光子参量下转换（degenerate three-photon parametric down-conversion）的非线性多光子转换过程，采用相空间方法（phase-space method）和随机微分方程（stochastic differential equation）分析其稳态稳定性，并通过正Wigner函数方法计算光谱密度，识别了三光子下转换的压缩特性及非高斯性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性多光子转换过程在量子光学及相关领域具有理论和实验上的挑战性，这些现象具有显著的量子特性，可能在量子信息和量子计算应用中发挥基础性作用。作者基于近期实验进展及广义三光子压缩的可能应用，旨在深入理解腔内受相干驱动并由腔自发辐射耗散的三光子参量下转换系统的量子性质。

Method: 采用相空间方法和随机微分方程对系统动力学进行处理，确定稳态的稳定性条件；围绕稳定稳态对动力学进行线性化，计算正交分量的光谱密度；由于高阶多光子过程的正准概率微分方程含有高阶导数，难以直接映射为随机微分方程，作者改用正Wigner函数（positive Wigner function）方法，计算与三时关联函数（three-time correlation function）相关的二维光谱密度以研究非高斯性质。

Result: 系统不表现出多稳态（multistability），而是在有限参数区域内存在单一稳定稳态分支，同时伴随多个不稳定分支；在稳定稳态附近通过线性化计算了正交分量光谱密度，识别并表征了三光子下转换的压缩特性；通过正Wigner函数方法获得了二维光谱密度。

Conclusion: 该系统在给定条件下仅存在单一稳定稳态，三光子下转换的压缩性质得到确认与表征，且通过正Wigner函数途径为研究高阶多光子过程的非高斯特性提供了可行的理论框架。

Abstract: Nonlinear multiphoton conversion processes are theoretically and experimentally challenging systems in quantum optics and related areas. These phenomena bring remarkable quantum properties and can be of fundamental relevance in quantum information and computation applications. Based on recent experimental advances and the possible uses in generalized three-photon squeezing, we investigate degenerate three-photon parametric down-conversion generated inside a cavity fed by a classical pump. In addition, we consider that the process is stimulated by a coherent driving mode and subjected to dissipation by spontaneous emission of the cavity. A treatment using phase-space methods and stochastic differential equations is applied and the stability conditions of its steady states are found. We find that the system does not exhibit multistability, instead it has a limited parameter region leading to a single stable steady branch, alongside multiple unstable branches. Focusing on the stable steady states and linearizing the dynamics around them, we calculate the spectral densities of the quadrature variables. We identify and characterize the squeezing of three-photon down-conversion. Moreover, since the quasi-probability differential equations of the higher-order multiphoton processes have higher-order derivatives, hindering their mapping into stochastic differential equations, we make use of a positive Wigner function approach to calculate two-dimensional spectral densities associated with three-time correlation functions, with the goal of studying non-Gaussian properties of the system.

</details>


### [123] [Physics-guided machine learning for sim-to-real calibration of NV diamond magnetometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19582)
*Jonathan Daniel,Martin Y. Kim,Jesse Hernandez,Emanuel Suarez,Sangwoo Lee,Jinhee Lee,Je-Hyung Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理引导的混合机器学习框架（physics-guided hybrid machine learning framework），将塞曼分裂（Zeeman splitting）直接嵌入学习流程，用于金刚石氮-空位（nitrogen-vacancy, NV）色心传感器的矢量磁力测量，在不依赖外部数据参考和复杂校准的情况下，将平均跟踪误差较纯统计基线提升372倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石NV色心系综可在非屏蔽环境中实现鲁棒的矢量磁力测量，但部署受限于复杂校准和对外部数据参考的依赖；传统统计机器学习需要海量训练数据，且存在仿真与现实不匹配（simulation-to-reality mismatch）的严重问题，亟需更高效、更自适应的训练方法。

Method: 提出物理引导的混合机器学习框架，将塞曼分裂的物理规律直接嵌入学习管线；混合架构结合稀疏物理测量与可扩展的合成数据生成，并将真实硬件非理想性（hardware non-idealities）纳入模型。

Result: 物理引导模型将平均跟踪误差显著降低，较纯统计基线实现372倍的精度提升；在部署解码未校准的原始实验光探测磁共振（ODMR）数据时，框架对标量磁场展现出卓越的预测精度。

Conclusion: 本工作为自校准（self-calibrated）传感器铺平道路，同时建立了一种适用于其他数据稀缺物理系统的机器学习训练方法。

Abstract: Ensemble nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond enable robust vector magnetometry in unshielded environments, yet deployment remains bottlenecked by complex calibration and a reliance on external data references. Conventional statistical machine learning requires an exorbitantly large volume of training data and suffers from severe simulation-to-reality mismatches. To address this, we introduce a physics-guided hybrid machine learning framework that embeds the Zeeman splitting directly into the learning pipeline. Our physics-guided model significantly reduces the average tracking error demonstrating a 372-fold precision improvement over purely statistical baselines. Furthermore, our hybrid architecture pairs a sparse physical measurement with scalable synthetic data generation, seamlessly incorporating real-world hardware non-idealities. When deployed to decode uncalibrated, raw experimental ODMR data, our framework delivers exceptional predictive accuracy for the scalar magnetic field. This work paves the way toward self-calibrated sensors while establishing a machine learning training method applicable to other data-scarce physical systems

</details>


### [124] [Dissipation-tunable extended and localized steady states in a non-disordered lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19694)
*Ming-Jie Tao,Yi-Ting Wang,Jing Li,Hongsheng Hou,Xiang-Ping Jiang,Lei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在无随机无序（non-disordered）的一维晶格中，通过精心设计的耗散（dissipation）可以选择性地挑出局域或扩展态。研究者引入带可调相对相位的非局域键跳跃算符，利用其相位选择性地偏好具有不同空间相位关联的本征态，从而在不改变任何哈密顿量参数的情况下，将长时间密度矩阵引导至局域或扩展哈密顿本征态主导的扇区，并展示了该选择性质在撤除耗散后仍可保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将耗散视为退相干（decoherence）的来源，会抑制量子干涉和局域化。本文旨在反其道而行，探讨能否通过适当设计的耗散通道来主动选择量子态的性质（局域或扩展），从而为无无序系统中的态制备和输运操控提供新思路。

Method: 作者在具有空间非均匀跳跃（spatially inhomogeneous hopping）的清洁一维晶格中引入一个非局域键跳跃算符，其相对相位可调。通过数值和解析分析，研究该相位如何选择性地偏好不同空间相位关联的本征态，并以距离l的配对位点中与耗散通道相位匹配的比例来量化选择性的微观起源；同时利用量子保真度（quantum fidelity）表征耗散淬灭过程。

Result: 研究发现，耗散的相对相位能够在不改变任何哈密顿量参数的情况下，将长时间密度矩阵引导至局域态或扩展态主导的扇区。所选取的态的特征可以在移除耗散之后持续存在，表明该选择具有持久性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了相位选择性键耗散（phase-selective bond dissipation）作为在无随机无序晶格中实现可控态制备和输运操纵的有效途径，扩展了耗散在量子系统中的应用范式。

Abstract: Dissipation is usually regarded as a source of decoherence that suppresses quantum interference and localization. Here we show that suitably engineered dissipation can instead be used to select localized or extended states in a strictly non-disordered one-dimensional lattice. The underlying clean lattice has spatially inhomogeneous hopping and supports both extended bulk states and localized boundary states, including an algebraically localized bound state in the continuum. We introduce a nonlocal bond jump operator with a tunable relative phase and show that this phase selectively favors eigenstates with different spatial phase correlations. As a result, the long-time density matrix can be steered toward sectors dominated by localized or extended Hamiltonian eigenstates without changing any Hamiltonian parameter. The microscopic origin of the selection is quantified by the fraction of site pairs separated by a distance $l$ that are phase matched with the dissipative channel. We further characterize the dissipative quench through the quantum fidelity and show that the selected character of the steady state can persist after the dissipation is removed. Our results establish phase-selective bond dissipation as a route to controllable state preparation and transport manipulation in non-disordered lattices.

</details>


### [125] [Gradient flow towards quantum states with ideal quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19770)
*T. Shiga,T. Fukui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对格点模型中量子态的梯度流方法，由量子度规（quantum metric）和贝里曲率（Berry curvature）平方两项生成，驱使谱投影子分别趋于Bogomolny饱和与均匀贝里曲率，并在Wilson-Dirac模型和Hofstadter模型中验证了该方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点模型中的量子态通常难以同时满足均匀贝里曲率和Bogomolny界饱和两个几何条件，作者希望构造一种动力学方法来逼近这一平衡。

Method: 作者提出由量子度规项和贝里曲率平方项构成的梯度流，作用于谱投影子（spectral projector）；由于有限维投影子存在no-go定理，两项无法完全同时满足，流将趋向非平凡不动点。随后从流动后的投影子构造短程截断的展平哈密顿量（flattened Hamiltonian）。

Result: 在Wilson-Dirac模型中，流动后的投影子表现出近似均匀的贝里曲率，同时接近Bogomolny界；由之构造的展平哈密顿量得到近平坦能带和近均匀贝里曲率。在Hofstadter模型中，该方法验证了度规项和贝里曲率项在高陈数（Chern number）陈带中的作用。

Conclusion: 该梯度流方法能够在格点模型中有效平衡量子度规与贝里曲率两个几何约束，为构造具有近平坦能带和近均匀贝里曲率的格点模型提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: We propose a gradient-flow method for quantum states in lattice models, generated by an action consisting of the quantum-metric and the square of the Berry curvature. These two terms drive the spectral projector toward Bogomolny saturation and uniform Berry curvature, respectively. We show that, due to a no-go theorem for finite-dimensional projectors, the two conditions cannot in general be satisfied simultaneously in lattice models. Hence the flow is expected to approach a nontrivial fixed point that balances the two geometric requirements. For the Wilson-Dirac model, we demonstrate that the flowed projector exhibits almost uniform Berry curvature while remaining close to the Bogomolny bound. We further construct a short-range truncated flattened Hamiltonian from the flowed projector and obtain a lattice model with nearly flat bands and nearly uniform Berry curvature. We also apply the method to the Hofstadter model and confirm the roles of the metric and Berry-curvature terms in a Chern band with higher Chern number.

</details>


### [126] [Resource-Efficient Bio-Molecular Docking on a NISQ-era Digital Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19868)
*Tianqi Chen,Adrian M. Mak,Jianguo Li,Jian Feng Kong,Chandra Verma,Sebastian Maurer-Stroh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分子对接（molecular docking）的混合量子-经典（hybrid quantum-classical）方法，将分子对接问题映射为最大顶点加权团问题（maximum vertex-weighted clique problem, MVWCP），并采用变分全基编码（full-basis encoding, FBE）策略，用布洛赫球向量高效编码经典二元变量，最后在IBM量子计算机上进行了电路执行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子对接是药物发现中的关键计算任务，但由于可能的结合构型数量呈组合爆炸式增长，对大型柔性分子的对接计算在规模上仍是计算密集型难题，亟需更高效的求解方法。

Method: 将分子对接问题重构为相容图上的MVWCP问题，提出变分全基编码（FBE）策略，利用布洛赫球向量（Bloch sphere vectors）有效编码经典二元变量；将分子对接映射到代价哈密顿量（cost Hamiltonian），在变分框架内最小化，采用随机化虚时演化（imaginary time evolution, ITE）启发的暖启动（warm start）方法结合梯度优化技术进行求解。

Result: 证明了FBE目标的全局最小化器总可以选择为纯乘积态（pure product state），为此方法使用幺正变分电路（unitary variational circuit）优化提供了严谨的理论依据；并在IBM量子计算机上实际执行了电路，验证了量子辅助优化的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了变分量子优化在基于结构的药物设计（structure-based drug design）中的可行性与潜力，同时指出了先进编码技术在计算生物学（computational biology）量子优化中的更广泛用途。

Abstract: Molecular docking is a vital computational task in drug discovery, wherein the objective is to efficiently identify optimal binding poses between a ligand and a target receptor protein. Due to the combinatorial explosion of possible binding configurations, docking of large and flexible molecules remains a computationally intensive problem, especially at scale. Early studies have revealed that the molecular docking can be re-cast as a maximum vertex-weighted clique problem (MVWCP) problem on a compatibility graph to be solved classically. In this work, we proposed a hybrid quantum-classical approach for molecular docking leveraging the MVWCP formalism with a variational full-basis encoding (FBE) strategy, which enables efficient encoding of classical binary variables with Bloch sphere vectors. We further prove that a global minimizer of the FBE objective can always be chosen to be a pure product state, thereby providing a rigorous justification for its optimization using a unitary variational circuit. The molecular docking problem is first mapped to a cost Hamiltonian that is minimized within a variational framework, optimized via a randomized imaginary time evolution (ITE)-inspired warm start, and gradient-based techniques. Finally, we also executed the circuit on an IBM quantum computer, underlying the feasibility and of quantum-assisted optimization for structure-based drug design and point towards the broader utility of advanced encoding techniques in quantum optimization for computational biology.

</details>


### [127] [Imaginary time evolution of a quantum system through analytic continuation from real-time quantum simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19943)
*Peng Guo,Anto Shibu,Joshua Lin,Yong Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过解析延拓（analytic continuation）实测实时关联函数来对一般量子哈密顿量实现虚时演化（Imaginary-Time Evolution, ITE）的算法，并在IBM量子硬件上以1D Fokker-Planck方程和1D量子力学散射中的积分关联函数为例进行了模拟演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算对实时量子系统模拟天然具有优势，但实现虚时演化（ITE）相对困难。然而量子实现ITE既可用于经典实现存在符号问题（sign problem）的情形，也可作为在量子计算机上精确制备本征态的方法，因此需要一种可行的量子ITE算法。

Method: 该算法通过对所测量的实时关联函数（real-time correlation functions）进行解析延拓，从而获得一般量子哈密顿量的虚时演化。此方法避免了直接实现虚时演化的困难，转而利用量子计算机在实时模拟上的天然优势。

Result: 作者在IBM量子硬件上进行了模拟和演示：以1D Fokker-Planck方程模拟经典扩散过程，以及1D量子力学散射中积分关联函数的虚时演化，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了利用实时关联函数的解析延拓来实现量子虚时演化的可行路径，为在量子计算机上制备本征态及处理存在符号问题的系统提供了一种有效工具。

Abstract: Though quantum computing naturally offers an advantage for simulations of real-time quantum systems, implementing Imaginary-Time Evolution (ITE) is comparatively more difficult. Nevertheless, quantum implementations of ITE are useful both in cases where classical implementations have associated sign problems, and also as an exact method of preparing eigenstates on quantum computers. In this work we present an algorithm to obtain ITE of a generic quantum Hamiltonian by performing analytic continuation of measured real-time correlation functions. We present simulations and demonstrations on IBM quantum hardware of 1D Fokker-Planck equations for classical diffusion process and imaginary-time evolution of integrated correlation functions in 1D quantum mechanical scattering as examples to demonstrate the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [128] [Dynamical one-from-many quantum metrology: Sum rule and matrix-free precision bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19988)
*Ziyu Xie,Junjie Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对实用量子单参数估计中普遍存在干扰参数（nuisance parameters）的“一对多”估计问题，提出了一种无需矩阵运算的紧致量子精度界。该界仅涉及标量量，避免了传统方法中量子Fisher信息（QFI）矩阵求逆和权重矩阵选取的困难，并在QFI矩阵奇异时依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用量子单参数估计几乎总是涉及干扰参数，需将其视为“从多个参数中估计一个”的（one-from-many）问题。现有方法依赖多参数计量学中的量子Cramér-Rao界，但受限于QFI矩阵求逆的计算负担、权重矩阵的选择要求，且当QFI矩阵奇异时方法失效。

Method: 作者发现，在动力学“一对多”估计中，存在一个此前被忽视的求和规则（sum rule），它将所有模型参数的QFI与关于时间的QFI联系起来，因而必须引入时间相关的QFI以构建增广QFI矩阵，但该矩阵天然奇异——恰为传统方法失效的情形。基于此，作者推导了一个紧致的、无需矩阵运算的量子精度界，仅涉及标量量。

Result: 该量子精度界具有广泛适用性，并将现有结果作为特例涵盖其中。在幺正（unitary）和含噪声（noisy）两种设置下均通过了验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为实用量子单参数计量学（practical quantum single-parameter metrology）提供了一个改进的操作性框架，有效克服了传统方法在奇异QFI矩阵场景下的局限。

Abstract: Practical quantum single-parameter estimation is rarely a pristine task; it almost invariably involves nuisance parameters, casting it as a one-from-many problem. Existing approaches to this problem rely on multi-parameter metrology, reducing the matrix quantum Cramér-Rao bound to obtain scalar quantum precision limits. However, these methods are often hampered by the demanding inversion of the quantum Fisher information (QFI) matrix and the requisite choice of a weight matrix, and they break down when the QFI matrix becomes singular. Here, we show that for dynamical one-from-many estimation, a previously overlooked sum rule connecting the QFI about all model parameters to the QFI about time necessitates including the latter to consider an augmented QFI matrix while simultaneously rendering it inherently singular--precisely the scenario where conventional approaches fail. To meet this challenge, we derive a tight, matrix-free quantum precision bound that involves only scalar quantities, offers broad applicability, and subsumes existing results as special cases. Validated in both unitary and noisy settings, our findings provide a refined operational framework for practical quantum single-parameter metrology.

</details>


### [129] [Boltzmann counting in Hilbert space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20136)
*Raúl O. Vallejos,Isadora Veeren,Frederico Brito,Fernando de Melo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对量子制备（quantum preparation）的几何熵（geometric entropy），定义为在给定约束条件下与纯态（pure states）相容的希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）体积的对数，将玻尔兹曼计数视角（Boltzmann's counting perspective）推广到量子情形，并为包括子空间投影、自旋期望值、部分求迹（partial trace）和缺陷探测器映射在内的三类约束条件推导了显式标度律和闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子熵概念（如基于密度矩阵的冯·诺依曼熵和粗粒化熵）并不直接反映量子制备层面的信息缺失，缺乏一个从几何/计数视角量化"与给定约束相容的纯态数目"框架。摘要旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将玻尔兹曼计数思想推广到量子设置，以希尔伯特空间中与给定约束相容的纯态所构成的体积之对数定义几何熵。文章分析三类约束——限制在子空间、固定期望值、粗粒化子系统描述，并对代表性例子（子空间投影、自旋期望值、部分求迹和不完美探测器映射）推导标度律和闭式体积表达式。

Result: 对于上述代表性约束例子，获得了相应的熵表达式的显式标度定律和闭式形式；研究表明该框架在制备层面提供了对量子无知的几何度量。

Conclusion: 该几何熵框架在量子制备层面提供了一种几何化的无知度量，与基于密度矩阵和粗粒化的熵概念形成互补，为量子信息与统计力学交叉领域提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We introduce a geometric entropy for quantum preparations, defined as the logarithm of the Hilbert-space volume of pure states compatible with a given set of constraints. This construction extends Boltzmann's counting perspective to the quantum setting, where compatible states need not be orthogonal and the relevant notion of "number of states" is naturally replaced by a volume in state space. We analyze three classes of constraints: restriction to a subspace, fixed expectation values, and coarse-grained subsystem descriptions. For representative examples, including subspace projection, spin expectation values, partial trace, and an imperfect detector map, we obtain explicit scaling laws and closed-form expressions for the associated volumes. The resulting framework provides a geometric measure of quantum ignorance at the level of the preparation and complements entropy notions based on density matrices and coarse graining.

</details>


### [130] [Reinforcement LearningtoHarness Approximation Errors for Long-Time QuantumSimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20139)
*Yu-Bo Shi,Markus Heyl,Roderich Moessner,Marin Bukov,Hongzheng Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 RL-Trotter 框架，利用强化学习（reinforcement learning）将量子模拟中不可避免的近似误差视为可用于纠错的资源而非仅需抑制的缺陷，通过优化整个长时间演化过程而非单个时间步，学习自纠错序列以提升长时间数字量子模拟的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间下的精确数字量子模拟（digital quantum simulation）受限于近似模拟固有的误差累积，传统方法在长时间演化中精度迅速下降。这激发了利用守恒律（conservation laws）提供低维信息来指导误差管理的想法。

Method: 作者引入 RL-Trotter 框架，该框架在不访问目标波函数（target wave function）的条件下，仅利用能量与能量方差（energy variance）等守恒量信息作为学习信号，驱动智能体自适应调整单一标量——下一个 Trotter 步长（Trotter step size），以优化整个长时间演化的全局精度。

Result: 学习到的策略能发现自纠错序列（self-correcting sequences），使后续误差补偿此前累积的误差，显著提升长时间动力学精度；策略对测量噪声具有内在鲁棒性，大幅降低测量开销；学习到的策略可泛化至未见过的物理相似初始态，并能从小型经典可模拟系统迁移至大一个数量级的系统。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种基于经典预训练后直接部署或有限微调于量子硬件的实用协议，确立了量子算法的新视角：近似演化中的误差可以被编排为资源，用于实现精确且资源高效的量子动力学模拟。

Abstract: Accurate digital quantum simulation at long times is limited by the accumulation of errors inherent to approximate simulation. Here we introduce RL-Trotter, a reinforcement-learning framework that treats unavoidable approximation errors as resources for error correction rather than merely imperfections to suppress. We show that low-dimensional information from conservation laws, such as the energy and energy variance, provides a sufficient learning signal to guide the agent, which learns to adapt a single scalar---the next Trotter step size---without access to the target wave function. By optimizing the entire long-time evolution rather than individual steps, RL-Trotter discovers self-correcting sequences in which later errors compensate for those accumulated earlier, increasing the accuracy of the long-time dynamics. The learned policies are intrinsically robust to measurement noise, substantially reducing measurement overhead. They also generalize to previously unseen, physically similar initial states and transfer from small, classically simulable systems to systems an order of magnitude larger. This enables a practical protocol based on classical pretraining followed by direct deployment or limited fine-tuning on quantum hardware. Our results establish a broader perspective for quantum algorithms: errors in approximate evolution can be orchestrated into resources for accurate and resource-efficient quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [131] [The ebbs and flows of quantum learning and sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20155)
*Matias Karjula,Teemu Ojanen,Tapio Ala-Nissila,Moein N. Ivaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过研究一族最小可调的后变分量子电路（postvariational quantum circuits），发现谱非平坦性（spectral nonflatness）与计量学响应（metrological response）直接控制量子系综典型的信息处理能力，揭示了一个先于量子混沌出现的中间"学习相"（learning phase）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究子体系量子复杂度与计算上有用结构涌现之间的关系，理解随机量子动力学特征如何作为可扩展非线性计算的计算资源。

Method: 研究一族最小可调参数的后变分量子电路，分析系综典型（ensemble-typical）的信息处理能力，考察谱非平坦性与计量学响应所扮演的控制作用。

Result: 发现一个介于可学习性与量子混沌之间的"学习相"，该阶段具有显著的非平坦性与读出态敏感性；最优信息处理容量随系统尺寸增大而提升，而深度置乱（deep scrambling）会抑制可观测量响应。

Conclusion: 随机量子动力学的上述特征可被视为可扩展非线性计算的计算资源，为理解量子复杂度与计算有用结构之间的关系提供了新视角。

Abstract: What is the relation between subsystem quantum complexity and the emergence of computationally useful structure? We address this by studying a family of minimally tunable postvariational quantum circuits, and show how spectral nonflatness and metrological response directly control the ensemble-typical information processing power. This unveils an intermediate "learning phase" that precedes the onset of quantum chaos, characterized by pronounced nonflatness and sensitivity of readout states. The optimal information processing capacity improves with system size, while deep scrambling suppresses observable response. The results reveal how such features of random quantum dynamics can be viewed as computational resources for scalable nonlinear computation.

</details>


### [132] [Nonthermal Dynamics of a One-dimensional Rydberg-atom Chain with Constraint Four-body Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20233)
*Tianyi Yan,Weibin Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由约束四体相互作用驱动的里德伯原子（Rydberg atoms）线性链的动力学，发现该系统支持能量近似等间距的量子多体疤痕态（quantum many-body scar states），并通过里德伯阻塞（Rydberg blockade）与反阻塞（antiblockade）的组合在实验上提供了实现途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索四体相互作用约束下的非遍历动力学，为研究量子多体疤痕态和受约束非遍历现象提供新的理论框架和实验实现路径。

Method: 对由约束四体相互作用驱动的线性里德伯原子链，采用多体基态的试探波函数（ansatz）将低能哈密顿量化为三对角形式，在热力学极限下解析求解基态能量，并通过精确对角化验证；使用Peierls阵列（交替键长）中里德伯阻塞与反阻塞的联合机制实现该约束模型。

Result: 基态能量随链长L线性标度，热力学极限下的解析结果与精确对角化一致；模型支持能量近似等间距的量子多体疤痕本征态，且疤痕态与基态|0⟩= |0⋯0⟩有强重叠；状态依赖的四体相互作用使重叠分布发生倾斜，导致初始态的复苏（revival）和非遍历动力学。

Conclusion: 通过结合里德伯阻塞与反阻塞，该四体约束模型为探索受约束的非遍历动力学提供了可行的实验路径。

Abstract: We investigate dynamics of a linear chain of Rydberg atoms driven by a constrained four-body interaction, where two neighboring atoms are excited simultaneously from the electronic ground state $|0\rangle$ to Rydberg state $|1\rangle$ only when their closest neighbors are in $|0\rangle$ state. By employing an ansatz for the many-body ground state, the low-energy Hamiltonian is given by a tridiagonal form. The many-body ground state energy, which scales linearly with the chain length $L$, is obtained analytically in the thermodynamic limit and agrees with the one of the exact diagonalization. Our model supports quantum many-body scar eigenstates that are nearly equally spaced energetically. The scar states overlap strongly with the basis state $|\mathbf{0}\rangle=|0\cdots 0\rangle$. We show that the overlap distribution is tilted by the state-dependent four-body interaction. This leads to non-ergodic dynamics, evidenced by the revival of the initial state. The four-body constrained model can be realized with Rydberg atoms in a Peierls array with alternating bond lengths, where atoms on the shorter and longer bonds experience Rydberg blockade and antiblockade, respectively. Our study provides a pathway to explore constrained non-ergodic dynamics with four-body interactions by combining the Rydberg blockade and antiblockade.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum-mechanical wave functions in singular potentials: linear and nonlinear states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20282)
*Hidetsugu Sakaguchi,Boris A. Malomed*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了三维薛定谔方程中引力奇异反平方势引起的量子坍缩（quantum collapse）如何被抑制、以及由此产生原本缺失的基态（ground state, GS）的理论结果，并讨论了奇异势与线性/非线性束缚态的相关研究进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维线性薛定谔方程中的吸引奇异反平方势已知会引发临界量子坍缩，本文旨在通过理论综述展示抑制该坍缩并创造原本缺失的地面态的机制，为奇异势作用下的束缚态研究提供系统化视角。

Method: 在平均场近似（mean-field approximation）下，粒子间的排斥接触相互作用用Gross-Pitaevskii（GP）方程中的三次项表示；同时利用线性薛定谔方程和GP方程分别分析一维和二维情形下在r趋于无穷处具有奇异性的排斥势产生的束缚态。

Result: 在三维玻色子气体系中，排斥接触相互作用可抑制由奇异反平方势引发的坍缩，并产生原本缺失的基态；此外，增长速度快于负谐振子势的奇异排斥势能够产生完整谱系的反直觉可归一化局域束缚态。

Conclusion: 文章提出了在r趋于0或r趋于无穷处具有奇异性的势作用下，线性与非线性束缚态未来研究的展望。

Abstract: It is known that the attractive singular inverse-square potential gives rise to the critical quantum collapse in the framework of the three-dimensional (3D) linear Schroedinger equation. This article summarizes theoretical results which demonstrate suppression of the collapse, caused by this singular potential, and the creation of the otherwise missing ground state (GS) in a 3D gas of bosonic particles, carrying an electric dipole moment, which are pulled to the central electric charge, with repulsive contact interactions between the particles. In the mean-field approximation, the repulsive interactions are represented by the cubic term in the respective Gross-Pitaevskii (GP) equation. In addition to the GS, excited states with angular momentum are briefly considered too. Another topic considered in the article is 1D and 2D bound states in the linear Schroedinger and GP equations with the repulsive potential, which demonstrates a singularity at r --> infinity. A very recent result is that such a potential, growing faster than the negative harmonic-oscillator potential, produces a full spectrum of counter-intuitive normalizable (localized) bound states. The article puts forward perspectives for further studies of linear and nonlinear bound states existing under the action of the potentials with the singularity at r --> 0 or r --> infinity.

</details>


### [134] [Programmable cavity QED with a fiber-integrated atomic array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20291)
*Stephan Roschinski,Johannes Schabbauer,Franz von Silva-Tarouca,Marvin Holten,Damien Bloch,Julian Léonard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将$^{87}$Rb光学镊子阵列与高协同性光纤法布里-珀罗微腔结合，实现了最多五个原子与腔的耦合，观察到集体增强的真空拉比劈裂，并实现了基于腔的非破坏性原子数读出，为腔量子电动力学（cavity QED）多体物理和量子网络节点奠定了可扩展架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学腔中的强原子-光子相互作用是量子信息处理、量子网络和量子光学效应探索的关键资源。光学镊子阵列虽能提供可扩展、位点分辨的中性原子操控，但其与高协同性腔量子电动力学系统的集成仍面临挑战，因此有必要实现两者的有效结合。

Method: 研究构建了一个由十二个位点的$^{87}$Rb光学镊子阵列与高协同性光纤法布里-珀罗微腔组成的混合系统。阵列被定位在腔模内，各位点可通过亚波长精度控制，利用驻波场中的确定性位移实现对单原子耦合强度的连续调谐。

Result: 在最多五个原子与腔耦合的情况下，测量到集体增强的真空拉比劈裂（collectively enhanced vacuum Rabi splitting），并实现了基于腔的耦合原子数非破坏性读出（non-destructive readout）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种可扩展的架构，用于腔介导的纠缠生成和具有单原子控制的腔量子电动力学多体物理研究，同时为光纤集成量子网络节点奠定了基础。

Abstract: Strong atom-photon interactions in optical cavities are a key resource for quantum information processing, quantum networking, and the exploration of quantum optical effects. Optical tweezer arrays offer scalable, site-resolved control of neutral atoms, but their integration with high-cooperativity cavity QED systems remains challenging. Here we combine a twelve-site $^{87}$Rb optical tweezer array with a high-cooperativity fiber Fabry-Pérot microcavity. The array is positioned within the cavity mode and individual sites are controlled with subwavelength precision, enabling continuous tuning of the single-atom coupling strength via deterministic displacement through the standing-wave field. For up to five atoms coupled to the cavity, we measure collectively enhanced vacuum Rabi splitting and implement cavity-based non-destructive readout of the number of coupled atoms. These results establish a scalable architecture for cavity-mediated entanglement generation and many-body cavity QED with single-atom control, and they lay the foundation for fiber-integrated quantum network nodes.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Extension of the Shockley-Queisser Limit for Nanostructured Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19284)
*Rivo Herivola Manjakamanana Ravelonjato,Jean Patrice Rakotoniaina,Ravo Tokiniaina Ranaivoson,Wilfrid Chrysante Solofoarisina*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将Shockley-Queisser极限推广至纳米结构太阳能电池，利用量子相空间形式主义（quantum phase space formalism），通过参数B_ll将纳米结构几何与热力学性质联系起来，推导出最大效率的分析表达式，并对PbS量子点（quantum dot）在四种几何构型下的效率进行了数值模拟，结果显示最大效率可达49.1%，远超体相PbS的15.8%及经典极限值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Shockley-Queisser极限（Shockley-Queisser limit）仅适用于体相材料，无法反映纳米尺度下量子限域（quantum confinement）效应对太阳能电池效率的影响。作者旨在建立一个新的理论框架，将纳米结构几何参数与光伏效率联系起来，探索超越传统光电转换极限的途径。

Method: 本文采用量子相空间形式主义推导最大效率的分析表达式，以参数B_ll表示每个限域方向的动量方差，并将其作为联系纳米结构几何与热力学性质的方差-协方差矩阵。电子-电子相互作用通过带有可调系数theta的交换关联能（exchange-correlation energy）纳入考虑。对于圆柱几何，精确限域能采用Bessel函数（Bessel function）的第一个零点j_0,1。数值模拟使用Python 3.8.1、NumPy和Matplotlib进行，积分通过精确收敛级数展开求值。

Result: 针对PbS量子点在立方体、正方形平行六面体、圆柱和球四种几何构型下的数值结果表明：最大效率在5纳米立方体为48.7%，在扁平平行六面体和圆柱构型为49.0%，在3纳米球体为49.1%。这些值远超体相PbS的15.8%且超过经典Shockley-Queisser极限。对于等体积形状，两种纵横比（aspect ratio）下出现两个效率峰值；对大尺寸，模型能正确回退到经典值。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了优化纳米结构太阳能电池的理论框架，论证了量子限域效应是超越传统光伏极限（photovoltaic limit）的一条有前景的路径。模型在尺寸、形状、温度和掺杂等参数间的系统性联系中，实现了从经典到量子尺度的统一描述。

Abstract: This article extends the Shockley-Queisser limit to nanostructured solar cells using the quantum phase space formalism. The parameter B_ll represents the momentum variance in each confinement direction and acts as a variance-covariance matrix linking the nanostructure geometry to thermodynamic properties. Electron-electron interactions are included via an exchange-correlation energy with an adjustable coefficient theta. The authors derive an analytical expression for the maximum efficiency as a function of size, shape, temperature, and doping. For the cylindrical geometry, the exact confinement energy uses the first zero of the Bessel function j_0,1. Numerical simulations are performed with Python 3.8.1, NumPy, and Matplotlib for PbS quantum dots in four geometries: cube, square parallelepiped, cylinder, and sphere. The integral is evaluated using an exact convergent series expansion. Results show that the maximum efficiency reaches 48.7 percent for a 5 nanometre cube, 49.0 percent for flattened parallelepiped and cylinder shapes, and 49.1 percent for a 3 nanometre sphere. These values greatly exceed the bulk PbS efficiency of 15.8 percent and surpass classical Shockley-Queisser limits. For constant-volume shapes, two efficiency peaks appear corresponding to different aspect ratios. The model correctly returns to classical values for large sizes. This approach provides a theoretical framework for optimising nanostructured solar cells and demonstrates that quantum confinement offers a promising route to surpass traditional photovoltaic limits.

</details>


### [136] [Channel-selective magnetic filtering in a nodal-line semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19362)
*Hironmoy Pratihar,Alessandro De Martino,Arijit Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究节点线半金属（nodal-line semimetal）中通过磁势垒（magnetic barrier）的量子输运。当费米能靠近节线环（nodal ring）时，费米面呈环面几何，在平行于节线环的平面内每个截面由内、外两条同心轮廓构成两个独立的输运通道。磁势垒能够区分这两个通道：因内外轮廓包围的动量空间面积不同，磁场诱导的横向动量位移在两者中产生不对称效应，内通道在较弱的势垒强度下即被截断，而外通道仍能传输。本文利用两带有效哈密顿量（two-band effective Hamiltonian）和波函数匹配方法，获得通道分辨的闭合形式透射振幅，证明在有限势垒强度窗口内磁势垒可作为通道选择性滤波器，其顺序截断行为塑造了两端电导随势垒强度递减的阶梯式特征，为实验上识别节点线半金属的环面费米面提供了可观测的指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 节点线半金属具有独特的环面费米面（toroidal Fermi surface），但如何利用输运手段在实验中区分该拓扑费米面的不同通道特征仍缺乏明确方案。本文旨在利用磁势垒的动量空间位移效应，实现对费米面内外通道的选择性调控，从而为探测环面费米面提供可操作的实验途径。

Method: 采用两带有效哈密顿量描述节点线半金属的低能准粒子，通过波函数匹配（wave-function matching）方法计算磁势垒作用下的散射问题，推导出每个输运通道（内、外同心轮廓）的闭合形式透射振幅，并由此分析两端电导与势垒强度的依赖关系。

Result: 磁势垒以不同强度的阈值分别截断内、外两个输运通道：内通道在较弱的势垒强度下即被阻断，而外通道在更强势垒下仍保持传输。在有限的势垒强度窗口内，系统表现为通道选择性滤波器（channel-selective filter），两通道的顺序关闭导致两端电导随势垒强度呈阶梯式递减，直至外通道也被截断后电导归零。

Conclusion: 磁势垒的通道选择性阻断行为为环面费米面的内外通道结构提供了实验上可观测的特征指纹（fingerprints），可用于鉴别节点线半金属的费米面拓扑几何。

Abstract: We study quantum transport through a magnetic barrier in a nodal-line semimetal. When the Fermi energy lies near the nodal ring, the Fermi surface has toroidal geometry. Each cross-section in a plane parallel to the nodal ring consists of two concentric contours, inner and outer, carrying distinct transport channels. We show that a magnetic barrier resolves these two channels: because the contours enclose different momentum-space areas, they accommodate the field-induced transverse-momentum shift unequally, and the inner channel is cut off at a weaker barrier strength than the outer. Using a two-band effective Hamiltonian and a wave-function matching approach, we obtain closed-form, channel-resolved transmission amplitudes. Over a finite window of barrier strength the inner contour is fully blocked while the outer still transmits, so the barrier acts as a channel-selective filter. This sequential quenching shapes the two-terminal conductance, which decreases with barrier strength as the two channels close in turn and terminates once the outer channel is cut off, providing experimentally accessible fingerprints of the toroidal Fermi surface of a nodal-line semimetal.

</details>


### [137] [Altermagnetic memcapacitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19476)
*Martin Latorre,Alvaro S. Nunez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于交变磁性多铁材料（altermagnetic multiferroics）的自旋电子忆电容（spintronic memcapacitance）效应，以稀土钒酸盐RVO₃为具体平台，在振荡电场下电荷电流与自旋电流均呈现过零闭合的针状磁滞回线，具有忆电容（memcapacitor）行为，且电荷流密度约为优化自旋转移矩磁隧道结最低确定性开关电流密度的3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自旋电子器件（如自旋转移矩磁隧道结）需要较高的确定性开关电流，效率受限。作者旨在探索一种兼具电荷与自旋响应、无需独立控制的自旋电子存储机制，以实现更高效的非易失性电与自旋协同存储。

Method: 理论模型采用二聚化双轨道d波交变磁性晶格，通过Su–Schrieffer–Heeger型键调制（bond modulation）构建，并借鉴自旋依赖的Rice–Mele模型，将交变磁性序与场可切换的电荷及自旋极化耦合，从理论上分析RVO₃的响应特性。

Result: 在振荡电场下，电荷与自旋电流均呈现零场处相切闭合的针状磁滞回线，即II型忆器件（type-2 memdevice）的标志性行为；电荷电流密度可比优化自旋转移矩磁隧道结的最低确定性开关电流密度高约3.6倍。极化回线同样零场相切闭合，并产生符号变化、具历史依赖性的“蝴蝶型”微分电容，确认其为真正的忆电容器。

Conclusion: 电荷与自旋通道受同一反演对称性保护，可同时切换而无需独立控制。交变磁性多铁材料（以RVO₃为具体实现）是兼具电存储与自旋电子存储功能的效率高、非易失的平台。

Abstract: We propose a spintronic memcapacitance effect based upon altermagnetic multiferroic materials. We identify the rare-earth vanadates RVO$_3$ as a concrete platform, with all key parameters tied to measured properties. Under an oscillating electric field, the resulting charge and spin currents trace pinched hysteresis loops that close tangentially at zero field -- the hallmark of memcapacitive, type-2 memdevice behavior -- with charge current densities exceeding, by a factor of about 3.6, the lowest deterministic switching current density reported for optimized spin-transfer-torque magnetic tunnel junctions. We model the system theoretically as a dimerized two-orbital $d$-wave altermagnetic lattice via a Su--Schrieffer--Heeger-type bond modulation, in the spirit of the spin-dependent Rice--Mele model, thereby coupling the altermagnetic order to field-switchable charge and spin polarizations. The associated polarization loops close tangentially at zero field and yield a sign-changing, history-dependent ``butterfly'' differential capacitance, identifying the device as a genuine memcapacitor. Both responses are protected by the same inversion symmetry, so charge and spin channels switch simultaneously with no separate control needed. These results establish altermagnetic multiferroics, realized concretely in RVO$_3$, as an efficient, non-volatile platform for combined electric and spintronic memory.

</details>


### [138] [Electronic and chemical phase identification in photoemission experiments using unsupervised machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19503)
*Matthew Staab,Joseph Pandur,Eli Rotenberg,Chris Jozwiak,Aaron Bostwick,Inna Vishik*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了AARDVARK框架，一种基于维度降维和高斯过程回归（Gaussian process regression）的通用样本探索方法，用于在空间分辨光电子能谱实验中引导初始样本搜索，实时识别光谱上不同区域的边界，优化数据采集并支持自主样本探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真空紫外光电子能谱（vacuum ultraviolet photoemission spectroscopy）实验信息量丰富，但表面敏感性导致数据常在未表征的表面上采集。传统栅格扫描方法定位最优测量区域耗时较长，需要更高效的样本探索策略。

Method: 提出AARDVARK框架，利用维度降维技术UMAP作为高斯过程回归的目标，将两者结合以动态适应样本特征的变化，高效识别光谱上不同区域的边界，并在测量选择中实现实时决策。

Result: 该算法能够实时识别光谱上不同区域的边界，动态适应样本特征变化，实现测量选择的实时决策，并优化数据采集过程，为光电子能谱实验中的未来自主样本探索提供了稳健框架。

Conclusion: AARDVARK为光电子能谱实验中的样本探索提供了一种通用且可扩展的框架，能够显著缩短初始区域搜索时间，并为未来自主化、智能化的实验数据采集奠定了基础。

Abstract: Vacuum ultraviolet photoemission spectroscopies are very information-rich experiments, but due to their surface sensitivity, data are often collected on an initially uncharacterized surface. Traditional raster-grid approaches for locating optimal measurement regions can be time-consuming. In this work, we introduce AARDVARK, a generalizable framework for sample exploration that leverages dimensionality reduction and Gaussian process regression to guide initial sample searches in spatially-resolved photoemission experiments. By utilizing UMAP as a target for a Gaussian process, the algorithm efficiently identifies boundaries of spectroscopically distinct regions, dynamically adapting to variations in sample characteristics. The algorithm enables real-time decision making in measurement selection, optimizes data acquisition, and presents a robust framework for future autonomous sample exploration in photoemission experiments.

</details>


### [139] [Twisted magnon frequency combs in ferromagnetic nanorings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19647)
*Liu Xuejuan,Zheng Xingen,Li Zhengyi,Zhang Zhizhi,Li Xiaoguang,Sun Haipeng,Li Hui,Zhou Cangtao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了铁磁纳米环中由涡旋核心回转（vortex-core gyration）与方位角自旋波（azimuthal spin-wave）模式间强非线性耦合产生的扭曲磁振子频率梳（twisted magnon frequency comb, tMFC）及其高阶模式，其中梳线携带间隔为1的量子化轨道角动量，其形成遵循能量与角动量同时守恒的选择定则。研究进一步表明，通过调节孔径尺寸可将边带多重性提升一个数量级，而外加面内磁场可连续可逆地调控梳间距，为非线性磁振子学（nonlinear magnonics）提供了多功能平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索磁振子非线性动力学中新型相干频率梳结构的产生机制，为可调谐频率梳生成与精密计量提供新的物理平台。

Method: 在铁磁纳米环系统中，通过强非线性耦合涡旋核心回转与方位角自旋波模式实现tMFC，并系统调节孔径直径以及外加面内磁场强度与极性来研究梳结构的调控行为。

Result: 发现减小孔径保持常规tMFC，增大孔径则通过四波混频（four-wave mixing）引入额外磁振子模式使梳显著致密化，侧带多重性提升一个数量级；外加磁场通过位移涡旋核心和改变约束势实现梳间距的连续可逆调谐，而孔径诱导的几何钉扎（geometric pinning）导致相反场极性下的不对称切换与磁滞现象。

Conclusion: tMFC被确立为非线性磁振子学中的通用平台，在可调谐频率梳生成和精密计量领域具有潜在应用前景。

Abstract: We report the emergence of twisted magnon frequency combs (tMFCs) and their higher-order modes in ferromagnetic nanorings, arising from strong nonlinear coupling between vortex-core gyration and azimuthal spin-wave modes. The comb lines carry distinct orbital angular momentum with quantum numbers spaced by unity, and their formation obeys selection rules governed by simultaneous conservation of energy and angular momentum. We demonstrate that the hole diameter serves as a powerful tuning parameter: reducing the hole size preserves the conventional tMFC, whereas increasing it introduces an additional magnon mode that dramat?ically densifies the comb via four-wave mixing, boosting the sideband multiplicity by an order of magnitude. Moreover, an external in-plane magnetic field enables continuous, reversible tuning of the comb spacing by dis?placing the vortex core and modifying its confinement potential, with the hole-induced geometric pinning giving rise to asymmetric switching and hysteresis under opposite field polarities. Our results establish the tMFC as a versatile platform for nonlinear magnonics, with potential applications in tunable frequency comb generation and precision metrology.

</details>


### [140] [Giant orbital Hall effect from cubic Dresselhaus orbital coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19648)
*Gwen Sevilen,Kyoung-Min Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发现三次德雷塞尔豪斯轨道耦合（cubic Dresselhaus orbital coupling）是增强轨道霍尔电导（OHC）的新途径，通过有效双轨道带模型证明其可在动量空间产生热点，使OBC显著增强，并将OHC相对线性耦合情况提升一个数量级以上，且在小劈裂极限下OHC以线性耦合十七倍的普适系数发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道霍尔效应（orbital Hall effect）是轨道电子学（orbitronics）的核心物理量，但以往增强轨道霍尔电导的方法仅依赖线性的拉什巴型（Rashba-type）轨道耦合，缺乏新路径的探索。

Method: 采用有效双轨道带模型（effective two-orbital band model），系统比较线性与三次动量依赖的德雷塞尔豪斯轨道耦合在轨道贝里曲率（OBC）和轨道霍尔电导中的表现，并分析小能级劈裂极限下的发散行为。

Result: 三次德雷塞尔豪斯耦合在动量空间生成纯线性情形下不存在的热点（hot spots），在这些热点处OBC被强烈增强；结合热点多重性，OHC相对线性耦合情形提高一个数量级以上；在小劈裂极限下OHC随能级劈裂反比发散，且发散系数普适地是线性情形的十七倍。

Conclusion: 三次德雷塞尔豪斯耦合为产生巨轨道霍尔响应（giant orbital Hall response）提供了全新途径，为轨道电子学器件应用开辟了新方向。

Abstract: The orbital Berry curvature (OBC) governs the intrinsic orbital Hall conductivity (OHC), a central quantity in orbitronics. Previous approaches for enhancing the OHC have primarily relied on a linearin-momentum, Rashba-type orbital coupling. Here, we show that cubic Dresselhaus orbital coupling offers a new route to enhancing the OHC. Using an effective two-orbital band model, we show that the cubic coupling generates momentum-space hot spots, absent in the purely linear case, at which the OBC is strongly enhanced.
