This local enhancement, together with the multiplicity of the hot spots, boosts the OHC by more than an order of magnitude relative to the linear-coupling value. We further find that the OHC diverges inversely with the level splitting in the small-splitting limit, with a divergence coefficient universally seventeen times larger than that of the linear case. These results establish cubic Dresselhaus coupling as a route to giant orbital Hall responses, opening new avenues for orbitronic device applications.

</details>


### [141] [Coherence protection of a silicon hole spin qubit with phase-modulated microwave driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19696)
*Sayyid I. Ibad,Yusuke Sato,Takuma Kuno,Itaru Yanagi,Toshiyuki Mine,Ryuta Tsuchiya,Digh Hisamoto,Hiroyuki Mizuno,Raisei Mizokuchi,Jun Yoneda,Tetsuo Kodera*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文针对硅量子点中空穴自旋量子比特（hole spin qubit）对低频噪声敏感的问题，通过相位调制的级联连续驱动（concatenated continuous driving, CCD）技术抑制低频噪声，稳定了拉比振荡并延长了振荡衰减时间，同时在CCD框架下实现了噪声保护与相干控制的双重目标，为噪声鲁棒的空穴自旋量子比特提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅量子点中的空穴自旋因其强大的本征自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）而可实现全电学快速操控，是量子计算的理想平台；然而该耦合也增强了对电荷噪声的敏感性，加之硅衬底残余核自旋引起的超精细相互作用（hyperfine interaction）构成不可忽略的低频噪声来源，限制了相干时间，亟需有效的噪声抑制方案。

Method: 本文在空穴自旋量子比特上实现了相位调制的级联连续驱动（CCD）技术，通过微波相位调制来抑制低频噪声，以稳定拉比振荡并延长振荡衰减时间。

Result: 实验结果显示，与常规方法相比，所提出的CCD方法能够稳定拉比振荡并延长振荡衰减时间；进一步地，通过在CCD框架中定义量子比特，实现了在同时保护量子比特免受噪声干扰的情况下进行相干控制，确认了门操作期间的相干保护效应。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种实现噪声鲁棒（noise-robust）空穴自旋量子比特的可行路线，为硅基空穴自旋量子计算平台的发展提供了重要的噪声抑制策略。

Abstract: Hole spins in silicon quantum dots are a promising platform for quantum computing due to their strong intrinsic spin-orbit coupling (SOC), which enables fast, all-electrical control. However, this coupling also increases their susceptibility to charge noise, thereby limiting coherence times. Moreover, holes in silicon are also affected by hyperfine interactions with residual nuclear spins in the silicon substrate, introducing a non-negligible source of low-frequency noise. Here, we implement a phase-modulated concatenated continuous driving (CCD) technique for hole spin qubits to suppress low-frequency noise through microwave phase modulation. This approach stabilizes Rabi oscillations and extends the oscillation decay time compared to the conventional method. Furthermore, by defining a qubit in the CCD frame, we achieve coherent control while simultaneously protecting the qubit from noise, confirming coherence protection during gate operations. These results demonstrate a viable route toward noise-robust hole spin qubits.

</details>


### [142] [Search for Majorana Bound States in Short Chains of Proxmitised Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19911)
*Bogdan R. Bułka,Karol I. Wysokiński*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由量子点链夹在s波超导体与强自旋轨道半导体之间并施加塞曼磁场的新型平台，用于实现马约拉纳零模；通过忽略自旋守恒跳跃和局域点内超导关联，哈密顿量可分解为两个等价子系统，并利用手性马约拉纳表象中的格林函数获得四量子点短链的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维p波超导体末端出现的马约拉纳零模（Majorana zero modes, MZM）在理论和实验上均受到广泛关注，已有平台包括邻近化半导体线和超导体间隔的量子点短链，本文旨在提出一种由量子点链（quantum dots, QDs）构成的新平台。

Method: 将量子点链夹在s波超导体与强自旋轨道半导体之间并施加塞曼磁场（Zeeman field），忽略自旋守恒跳跃和局域点内超导关联后，哈密顿量分解为两个具有独立希尔伯特空间的等价哈密顿量；模型因相邻点间的自旋翻转过程t_so和交叉安德烈耶夫反射（cross-Andreev reflections）Δ_CAR而具有交错结构，通过手性马约拉纳表象中的格林函数和转移矩阵方法进行分析。

Result: 针对四量子点短链并耦合两个外部储库，获得了由手性马约拉纳表象中格林函数导出的相图；零能下全局推迟格林函数的模包含了马约拉纳零模的拓扑与空间特征信息，其观测特征与转移矩阵方法结果高度一致，特别是马约拉纳零模振荡波函数的参数区域以及费米子宇称变化的边界均得到良好复现。

Conclusion: 所提出的量子点链-超导体-自旋轨道半导体平台能够承载马约拉纳零模，格林函数方法可有效探测其拓扑性质和空间特征，且与转移矩阵方法及其他手段的结果相吻合，表明该平台有望成为马约拉纳零模研究的新实验载体。

Abstract: Majorana zero modes (MZM) appearing at the ends of artificially created one-dimensional p-wave superconductors have been intensively studied recently both theoretically and experimentally. Among possible platforms, proximitised semiconducting wires, and short chains of quantum dots with a superconductor in between were investigated. Here, we propose a different platform consisting of a chain of quantum dots (QDs) sandwiched between an s-wave superconductor and a strong spin-orbit semiconductor, subject to a Zeeman magnetic field. Neglecting spin-conserving hopping processes between QDs and local on-dot superconducting correlations induced by the superconducting proximity effect, reduces the Hamiltonian to the sum of two equivalent Hamiltonians with two independent Hilbert spaces. The resulting model has a staggered structure due to spin-flipping processes $t_{so}$ and cross-Andreev reflections $Δ_{CAR}$ between neighbouring dots. Our central result is the phase diagram of a short chain consisting of four QDs and coupled to two external reservoirs, obtained by means of the Green function in chiral Majorana representation. The modulus of the retarded Green function, probing the whole chain and calculated for zero energy, is shown to contain information on topology and spatial character of Majorana zero modes. The features observed in the Green function nicely agree with those obtained from the transfer matrix approach. In particular the region in parameter space in which Majorana zero modes display oscillatory wave functions are well reproduced. Likewise the borders of the fermion parity changes obtained by the Green function agree with those obtained by other means.

</details>


### [143] [Floquet Theory for Light-Driven Rotation of Dipolar and Multipolar Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20197)
*Amane Takano,Minoru Kanega,Masahiro Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用 Floquet 理论和模式分离方法，对受圆偏振激光照射的电偶极或多极粒子建立朗之万型（Langevin-type）方程并进行微观动力学分析，数值模拟了粒子转动的时间演化，给出了激光频率、强度、粒子质量、温度和摩擦对激光驱动旋转（laser-driven rotation）的定量依赖关系，并绘制了由三种不同标度律区构成的“非平衡相图”（nonequilibrium phase diagram）。理论结果与过阻尼极限下的实验结果定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光驱动纳米或微米粒子旋转在光学操控和光学物理领域早已为人所知，被视为光向物质的角动量转移，但基于哈密顿量或运动方程（equation of motion）的微观分析尚不充分。作者希望通过简单模型对旋转的微观机制进行系统分析，并估计各物理参数对旋转行为的影响。

Method: 作者采用由圆偏振激光照射的电偶极或多极粒子这一简单构型，建立并分析朗之万型运动方程，并利用面向耗散经典系统的 Floquet 理论（Floquet theory）以及模式分离方法（mode separation method）对系统进行解析研究。此外，作者还通过数值方法计算了粒子的时间演化以验证解析结果。

Result: 作者精确估计了激光频率、激光强度、粒子质量、温度以及摩擦（耗散）对光驱动旋转的依赖关系，并在广参数区间内确定了光驱动旋转的“非平衡相图”。该相图包含三个区域：旋转频率 Ω∝ω⁻¹、Ω∝ω⁻³，以及 Ω=ω（其中 ω 为激光频率）。与若干实验结果对比后发现，过阻尼朗之万方程（overdamped Langevin equation）的结果与实验定性一致。

Conclusion: 本文从微观动力学角度系统地揭示了光驱动粒子旋转的标度行为与相图结构，为理解光与物质角动量转移提供了微观理论框架。过阻尼极限下理论结果与实验的定性一致性表明，该模型可用于描述实际实验观测，但更多参数区间与多极效应仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Nano- or micro-particle rotation driven by light has been well known in the fields of optical manipulation and optical physics since the end of the last century. It is viewed as a sort of angular-momentum transfer from light to material, but its microscopic analysis based on the Hamiltonian or the equation of motion has been less developed. We model this rotation with a simple setup of an electrically dipolar or multipolar particle irradiated by circularly polarized laser and comprehensively analyze the Langevin-type equation of motion by using the Floquet theory for dissipative classical systems and the mode separation method. Furthermore, we numerically compute the time evolution of the particle. As a result, we accurately estimate the dependence of the laser-frequency, laser-intensity, particle mass, temperature, and friction (dissipation) on the laser-driven rotation. We determine the ``nonequilibrium phase diagram'' of the laser-driven rotation in a broad parameter regime, which consists of three regimes: the rotation frequency $Ω\proptoω^{-1}$, $Ω\proptoω^{-3}$, or $Ω=ω$ ($ω$ is the laser frequency). Comparing our theoretical result with some experiments, we show that the result of the overdamped Langevin equation is qualitatively consistent with the experiments.

</details>


### [144] [Coulomb blockade-like transport and enhanced memory in organic transistors embedded with sub-nm Pt nanoparticles for neuromorphic computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20245)
*Arash Ghobadi,Thomas B. Kallaos,Abhi Abhijeet,Stephen C. Klue,Joseph C. Mathai,Carsten A. Ullrich,Shubhra Gangopadhyay,Suchismita Guha*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究在有机薄膜晶体管的半导体与铁电介电层之间引入超薄氧化物层，其中均匀分布亚纳米铂纳米粒子（PtNP），形成Al₂O₃/PtNP/Al₂O₃界面堆叠结构。该结构实现局部电荷捕获与释放，使有机晶体管展现出大于20V的存储窗口，并支持电学和光学刺激下的多模态编程，兼具长期可塑性与增强的短期可塑性（STP），为神经形态计算中实现STP提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机晶体管在神经形态应用中日益重要，但仅依赖铁电开关或界面陷阱实现多电导态的器件存在存储窗口受限的问题。因此需要探索能够扩大存储窗口并增强突触可塑性的新型器件结构。

Method: 在聚合物半导体与铁电介电层之间的界面处，引入均匀分布亚纳米铂纳米粒子（PtNP）的超薄氧化物层，构建Al₂O₃/PtNP/Al₂O₃三层界面堆叠结构，利用PtNP实现局部电荷捕获/释放，以有效调控沟道电导。

Result: 有机晶体管在电流-电压特性中展现出大于20V的大存储窗口；亚纳米PtNP引起与室温库仑阻塞（Coulomb blockade）类输运一致的特征，支持存储窗口内离散且良好分离的能级；器件支持电学和光学两种刺激下的多模态编程，同时呈现长期可塑性和增强的短期可塑性（STP）现象。

Conclusion: 该研究为在神经形态计算中实现短期可塑性（STP）开辟了新方向，所提出的界面纳米粒子工程策略可为发展高性能有机神经形态器件提供可行途径。

Abstract: Organic transistors are playing an increasingly important role for neuromorphic applications. However, devices that rely solely on ferroelectric switching or on interface traps for their multi-conductance states exhibit limited memory windows. Here, we introduce an ultrathin oxide layer with a uniform distribution of sub-nm platinum nanoparticles (PtNPs) at the interface of a polymer semiconducting and a ferroelectric dielectric in a thin film transistor architecture. The interfacial stack, Al$_2$O$_3$/PtNP/Al$_2$O$_3$, provides a viable route for localized charge trapping and de-trapping in a region where it can most effectively influence the channel conductance. The organic transistors display a large memory window (> 20 V) in their current-voltage characteristics. The sub-nm PtNPs give rise to features that are consistent with room temperature Coulomb blockade-like transport, supporting discrete and well-separated levels within the memory window. The devices support multimodal programming using electrical and optical stimuli with both long-term plasticity and enhanced short-term plasticity (STP) phenomena. These results open new directions for implementing STP in the development of neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [145] [Signatures of a light-induced exciton condensate exhibiting BEC-BCS crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20310)
*Khanh Duy Nguyen,Gabriele Berruto,Yunhe Bai,Thomas Marchese,Woojoo Lee,Haoran Lin,Jiangang Yang,Chong Liu,Y. Shirley Meng,Shuolong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用时间分辨和角度分辨光电发射谱（time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）在单层MnBi2Te4中观测到光诱导的非平衡激子凝聚体（nonequilibrium exciton condensate）及其BEC-BCS交叉（BEC-BCS crossover）的新颖实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子凝聚体为研究准粒子配对、BEC-BCS交叉和激子拓扑现象提供了平台，实现非平衡（nonequilibrium）激子凝聚体可对这些涌现现象进行终极调控，但此前光诱导的非平衡激子凝聚体及其BEC-BCS交叉的实验证据仍然缺失。

Method: 采用时间分辨与角分辨光电发射谱对单层MnBi2Te4进行光学激发后的观测，分析空穴型色散（hole-like dispersion）的形成、能带尖锐化（energy-domain sharpening）的时间演化及其对泵浦注量（pump fluence）的阈值响应，并结合Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）相变模型和Nelson-Kosterlitz临界密度进行定量比对。

Result: 光学激发后出现并持续超过20 ps的独特空穴型色散；价带（valence band）在时间零点后2 ps出现能量域尖锐化，并在0.84 mJ/cm2的泵浦注量阈值处呈现尖锐起始行为；高注量下激子特征发展为驼峰形（camel-back-shaped）色散，与凝聚体框架中的BEC-BCS交叉相符；估计的阈值激子密度与Nelson-Kosterlitz临界密度定量一致。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了超薄MnBi2Te4作为研究非平衡激子凝聚体的模型体系，并与超导性及激子驱动拓扑相（exciton-driven topological phases）建立了联系。

Abstract: Exciton condensates provide a platform to study quasiparticle pairing, Bose-Einstein condensation-Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BEC-BCS) crossover, and excitonic topological phenomena. Achieving a nonequilibrium exciton condensate allows the ultimate tunability of these emergent phenomena. Yet, evidence of a light-induced, nonequilibrium exciton condensate and its BEC-BCS crossover remains elusive. Here, we use time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy to demonstrate signatures of a non-equilibrium exciton condensate and its BEC-BCS crossover in monolayer MnBi2Te4. Following optical excitation, a distinctive hole-like dispersion representing excitons emerges and persists for >20 ps. Strikingly, energy-domain sharpening in the valence band occurs 2 ps after time zero and exhibits a sharp onset at a threshold pump fluence of 0.84 mJ/cm2. The delayed and strongly nonlinear response is difficult to reconcile with transient field effects or conventional carrier-induced band shifts but is consistent with a model of exciton condensation governed by a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transition. The estimated threshold exciton density agrees quantitatively with the Nelson-Kosterlitz critical density. At higher fluences, the exciton feature develops a camel-back-shaped dispersion, consistent with the BEC-BCS crossover in the condensate framework. Our work establishes ultrathin MnBi2Te4 as a model system for studying nonequilibrium exciton condensates with a connection to superconductivity and exciton-driven topological phases.

</details>

### [146] [Interferometric Signatures of Zero Modes in Fractional Quantum Hall-Superconductor Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19312)
*Junyi Cao,Ramanjit Sohal,Angela Kou,Eduardo Fradkin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于分数量子霍尔-超导体（FQH-SC）异质结的探测构型，用于揭示缺陷束缚寄生费米子零模（PZM）的融合结构，并通过约瑟夫森谱和干涉仪响应验证其非阿贝尔性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔-超导体异质结被预言可承载缺陷束缚的寄生费米子零模（parafermion zero modes, PZMs），但这些零模的融合结构尚未被实验直接探测，需要设计可行的探测方案。

Method: 提出两种关联构型：其一为耦合单量子点接触（quantum point contact, QPC）的约瑟夫森结，通过准粒子隧穿切换缺陷融合通道，在分数量子约瑟夫森谱中产生随机分支跃迁；其二为嵌入双QPC法布里-珀罗（Fabry-Pérot）干涉仪中的约瑟夫森结，弱隧穿时产生融合通道依赖的干涉信号，强隧穿时则探测融合通道的叠加态。

Result: 在强隧穿极限下，干涉仪信号被强烈抑制，且在拓扑极限下精确消失，这一现象揭示了缺陷的非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）本质。

Conclusion: 该工作表明即使在母体分数量子霍尔态本身是阿贝尔（Abelian）的情况下，缺陷束缚的零模仍可展现非阿贝尔统计性质，两种探测构型为实验验证提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall-superconductor (FQH-SC) heterostructures are predicted to host defect-bound parafermion zero modes (PZMs). We propose two related configurations to probe their fusion structure. In a Josephson junction coupled to a single quantum point contact (QPC), quasiparticle tunneling switches the defect fusion channel, producing stochastic transitions between branches of the fractional Josephson spectrum. Embedding the junction in a two-QPC Fabry-Pérot interferometer provides a complementary probe. Weak zero mode tunneling produces fusion-channel-dependent interference while strong tunneling makes the interferometer probe a superposition of fusion channels and strongly suppresses the signal: in the topological limit it vanishes exactly, revealing the defects' non-Abelian nature even when the parent FQH state is Abelian.

</details>


### [147] [Hysteresis without coexistence: disorder-rounded first-order transitions in a van der Waals magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19352)
*Xiaoyu Guo,Abby N. Neill,Christopher M. Pasco,Tyrel M. McQueen,N. P. Armitage*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用时域太赫兹光谱（time-domain terahertz spectroscopy）研究了准二维kagome范德华磁体家族 Nb₃Cl₈₋ₓBrₓ（x=0, 1, 8）中淬火无序（quenched disorder）对一级相变（first-order phase transition）的影响。研究发现Br掺杂引入无序后，相变从宏观相共存加热滞的清洁一级相变，转变为无宏观相共存但热滞保留的展宽相变，与Imry-Wortis和Aizenman-Wehr理论预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火无序可深刻改变相变行为，但在低维系统中，理论预测弱淬火无序能够削平一级相变的热力学不连续性，这一预测缺乏系统的实验验证。本文旨在通过可控的化学掺杂体系，直接观察无序对低维一级相变的影响。

Method: 采用时域太赫兹光谱技术，对Nb₃Cl₈₋ₓBrₓ系列样品进行温度依赖的声子频率测量，通过Br取代Cl引入取代无序（substitutional disorder），比较纯体系与无序体系中声子模式和相变特征的差异。

Result: Br取代导致额外声子支（phonon branch）出现，其谱权重随Br浓度增加而增加，频率则软化。Nb₃Cl₈表现出具有宏观相共存和热滞（thermal hysteresis）的干净一级相变，而Nb₃Cl₇Br的相变虽保留热滞但显著展宽，且无可分辨的宏观相共存。

Conclusion: 无序将相变碎片化为局域有序的畴域而非由稳定相界分隔的体相，与Imry-Wortis和Aizenman-Wehr理论场景一致。热滞在无序化合物中持续存在而宏观相共存消失，表明这两项常被视作一级相变统一标志的特征可被无序区分。Nb₃Cl₈₋ₓBrₓ为研究低维系统中无序对一级相变的影响提供了理想平台。

Abstract: Quenched disorder can profoundly modify phase transitions. In low-dimensional systems, theory predicts that even weak quenched disorder can round the thermodynamic discontinuities associated with a first-order phase transition. Here, we employ time-domain terahertz spectroscopy to investigate the quasi-two-dimensional trimerized kagome van der Waals magnet family Nb$_3$Cl$_{8-x}$Br$_x$ ($x=0$, 1 and 8). We observe the emergence of an additional phonon branch upon Br substitution, whose spectral weight increases and frequency softens with increasing Br concentration. The temperature evolution of the phonon frequencies reveals a clean first-order transition in Nb$_3$Cl$_8$ characterized by macroscopic phase coexistence and thermal hysteresis. In contrast, the transition in the substitutionally disordered compound Nb$_3$Cl$_7$Br retains its hysteresis while exhibiting a substantially broadened transition with no resolvable macroscopic phase coexistence. These observations reveal disorder-induced fragmentation of the transition into locally favored domains instead of well-defined bulk phases separated by stable phase boundaries. The behavior is consistent with the Imry-Wortis and the Aizenman-Wehr scenarios for the effect of quenched disorder in low-dimensional systems, which destabilizes macroscopic phase coexistence and rounds the thermodynamic discontinuities associated with first-order transitions. Thermal hysteresis persists in the disordered compound despite the lack of resolvable coexistence, indicating that the two features often treated as a single hallmark of first-order character arise distinctly and can be separated by disorder. Moreover, our results establish Nb$_3$Cl$_{8-x}$Br$_x$ as a promising platform for investigating the effects of disorder on first-order transitions in low-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [148] [Tunable inter-bilayer magnetic correlations and candidate multipolar physics in the van der Waals oxyhalides DyOCl, DyOBr, and DyOI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19421)
*F. C. Brooks,X. Bai,J. Bacsa,V. O. Garlea,S. Calder,N. Butch,M. B. Stone,M. Mourigal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对稀土氧卤化物DyOX（X = Cl, Br, I）进行了系统比较研究，建立了这类准二维稀土范德华磁体家族的可调性，在三个化合物中均观察到低温反铁磁序（TN ~ 7-10 K）和更高温异常（TQ ~ 27-30 K），并指出TQ下可能存在与低能晶体场态相关的多极物理（multipolar physics）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土范德华磁体为在体相晶体中结合强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）、大磁矩和降低维度提供了一条途径。作者旨在通过系统比较DyOX系列化合物，研究层间范德华间隙的可调性如何影响磁性能，并探索其中可能存在的低能激发物理。

Method: 采用X射线结构精修确定晶体结构；使用磁化强度和比热测量探测低温热力学异常；对DyOCl和DyOBr进行单晶磁化测量并结合DyOCl的晶体场（crystal-field）分析；通过中子衍射和中子非弹性散射分别研究磁结构与磁激发谱。

Result: 增加卤素离子半径显著扩大层间间距而局部双层几何几乎不变；三个化合物均显示TN ~ 7-10 K的反铁磁序和TQ ~ 27-30 K的更宽异常；DyOCl和DyOBr呈现强c轴硬轴各向异性，DyOCl晶体场分析得到XY型基态g张量；DyOCl中观测到长程反铁磁序，而DyOBr和DyOI表现出尖锐磁散射与Warren型弥散特征共存；DyOCl的非弹性中子散射在25-30 meV附近识别出晶体场激发，另有约10 meV磁模式其温度依赖与高温异常相关。

Conclusion: DyOX被确立为一个可调谐的准二维稀土磁体家族，其TQ异常可能与低能晶体场态相关的多极序有关，但需要共振X射线散射或弹性常数测量等直接手段来确定TQ处的序参量（order parameter）。

Abstract: Rare-earth van der Waals magnets provide a route to combining strong spin-orbit coupling, large magnetic moments, and reduced dimensionality in bulk crystals. We report a comparative study of the dysprosium oxyhalides DyOX (X = Cl, Br, I), which realize square-bilayer networks of Dy3+ moments separated by a tunable van der Waals gap. Structural refinements show that increasing the halide ionic radius strongly expands the inter-bilayer spacing while leaving the local bilayer geometry nearly unchanged. Magnetization and heat-capacity measurements reveal two low-temperature anomalies in all three compounds: antiferromagnetic order at TN ~ 7-10 K and a broader anomaly near TQ ~ 27-30 K. Single-crystal magnetization on DyOCl and DyOBr establishes a strong hard-c-axis anisotropy, consistent with crystal-field analysis of DyOCl, which yields an XY-like ground-state g tensor. Neutron diffraction shows long-range antiferromagnetic order in DyOCl, whereas DyOBr and DyOI exhibit sharp magnetic scattering coexisting with Warren-like diffuse features, consistent with robust in-plane correlations and imperfect inter-bilayer registry. Inelastic neutron scattering on DyOCl identifies crystal-field excitations near 25-30 meV and an additional magnetic mode near 10 meV whose temperature dependence is tied to the high-temperature anomaly. Taken together, these results establish DyOX as a tunable family of quasi-two-dimensional rare-earth magnets and point to candidate multipolar physics associated with low-lying crystal-field states. Direct probes of quadrupolar order, such as resonant x-ray scattering or elastic-constant measurements, will be required to determine the order parameter at TQ.

</details>


### [149] [Antiferromagnetic order and lattice response in DyCuAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19465)
*M. G. Kim,J. -W. Kim,P. Ryan,D. Evans,E. D. Mun*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过高分辨同步辐射X射线衍射和X射线共振磁散射（XRMS）确定了DyCuAs₂在低温下的晶体与磁结构，发现其具有磁弹性耦合和Γ₁₀表示的面内反铁磁序（++--堆叠），并探讨了该磁结构与家族中电阻率极小值异常输运行为之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DyCuAs₂属于RECuAs₂家族，该家族在反铁磁转变温度以上表现出电阻率极小值这一反常输运行为，但其低温晶体结构和磁结构尚未被清晰表征。理解其磁结构与异常输运之间的联系是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 采用高分辨同步辐射X射线衍射（synchrotron X-ray diffraction）与X射线共振磁散射（XRMS）技术，在Dy L₃吸收边进行测量，并结合表示分析（representation analysis）与反铁磁布拉格峰强度计算来确定磁结构。

Result: DyCuAs₂在低温下保持四方对称性，但在反铁磁转变温度T_N≈7 K附近晶格参数a和c出现显著异常，表明存在强的磁弹性耦合。XRMS测量确认了T_N以下具有q=(0,0,0.5)的公度反铁磁序，磁结构属于Γ₁₀表示，由沿c轴以++--序列堆叠的面内Dy磁矩构成，与SmCuAs₂此前报道的磁结构相同。

Conclusion: 对DyCuAs₂、SmCuAs₂和GdCuAs₂的比较表明，面内反铁磁序及其在四方晶格上的磁挫败（magnetic frustration）与RECuAs₂家族电阻率极小值的出现密切相关。同时，DyCuAs₂中增强的晶格响应和更强的磁场敏感性表明磁弹性与自旋-轨道相互作用在决定该异常输运行为的鲁棒性方面也起重要作用。

Abstract: We report high-resolution synchrotron X-ray diffraction and X-ray resonant magnetic scattering (XRMS) studies of the low-temperature crystal and magnetic structures of DyCuAs$_2$, a member of the \RE{}CuAs$_2$ family exhibiting a resistivity minimum above the antiferromagnetic transition temperature. Synchrotron diffraction measurements reveal that DyCuAs$_2$ preserves tetragonal symmetry down to low temperature within the experimental resolution, although pronounced anomalies in both lattice parameters $a$ and $c$ are observed near the antiferromagnetic transition temperature, $T_{\mathrm N}\approx7$~K, indicating strong magnetoelastic coupling. XRMS measurements at the Dy $L_3$ edge establish commensurate antiferromagnetic ordering below $T_{\mathrm N}$ with AFM Bragg peaks at \qq{} = (0, 0, 0.5). Representation analysis and calculations of the AFM Bragg peak intensities identify the magnetic structure as the $Γ_{10}$ representation, consisting of in-plane Dy moments stacked along the \cc{} axis in a $++--$ sequence. The magnetic structure is therefore identical to that previously reported for SmCuAs$_2$. Comparison among DyCuAs$_2$, SmCuAs$_2$, and GdCuAs$_2$ suggests that in-plane AFM order and the associated magnetic frustration on the tetragonal lattice are closely connected to the emergence of the resistivity minimum in the \RE{}CuAs$_2$ family. At the same time, the enhanced lattice response and stronger magnetic-field sensitivity observed in DyCuAs$_2$ imply that magnetoelastic and spin-orbit interactions additionally play important roles in determining the robustness of this anomalous transport behavior.

</details>


### [150] [Universality of long-wavelength behavior of composite-fermion Fermi liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19604)
*Aamir A. Makki,Mytraya Gattu,J. K. Jain*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过复合费米子（composite-fermion, CF）对角化方法改进波函数，研究了投影静态结构因子在小波矢极限下的行为，发现其满足 $\bar{S}(\mathbf{q})\sim q^3$ 而非 C-S 场论预测的 $q^3\ln q$ 形式，且该行为对电子间相互作用形式不敏感，暗示其具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前有研究在零阶微观理论框架下发现复合费米子液体的投影静态结构因子在小波矢下表现为 $\sim q^3$，这与C-S场论对库仑相互作用预测的 $\sim q^3\ln q$ 行为不一致。本文旨在检验这种分歧是否源于此前工作中使用的零阶CF费米液体波函数未能正确捕捉长波行为。

Method: 采用复合费米子对角化（CF diagonalization）方法对波函数进行显著改进，在此基础上重新计算投影静态结构因子在小波矢极限下的行为，并与零阶微观理论结果及C-S场论预测进行比较。

Result: 改进波函数后仍未发现 $\bar{S}(\mathbf{q})\sim q^3\ln q$ 行为的任何证据，确认了小波矢下 $\sim q^3$ 的标度行为。此外，小波矢极限下 $\bar{S}(\mathbf{q})$ 的行为对电子间相互作用的改变不敏感，表明该行为具有普适性。

Conclusion: C-S场论的 $q^3\ln q$ 预测与数值结果不一致，且分歧不能归因于此前零阶波函数的不足；$\bar{S}(\mathbf{q})\sim q^3$ 在小波矢极限下的普适性表明该行为是复合费米子液体的内在特征。

Abstract: A recent article evaluated the long-wavelength behavior of the projected static structure factor of the composite-fermion (CF) liquid within the zeroth-order microscopic theory and found $\bar{S}(\mathbf{q})\sim q^3$, in disagreement with the $\bar{S}(\mathbf{q})\sim q^3\ln q$ behavior predicted by the Chern-Simons field theory for the Coulomb interaction. Here we consider the possibility that the discrepancy arises because the zeroth-order CF Fermi-liquid wave function used in that work does not properly capture the long wavelength behavior. We use CF diagonalization to significantly improve the wave function but do not find any evidence for $\bar{S}(\mathbf{q})\sim q^3 \ln q$ behavior. Additionally, we find that the small-$q$ behavior of $\bar{S}(\mathbf{q})$ is also insensitive to the form of the interaction between electrons, suggesting universality.

</details>


### [151] [Torus Berry Data Determine All-Genus Abelian Topological Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20330)
*Daniel Galviz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明了阿贝尔陈-西蒙斯（Abelian Chern-Simons）拓扑序中，环面贝里矩阵（torus Berry matrices）可以完全确定全亏格（all-genus）扩展拓扑场论（extended TQFT）。论文识别了度量形变下的射影贝里完整（projective Berry holonomy）的拓扑部分与阿贝尔陈-西蒙斯TQFT的映射类群表示（mapping-class-group representation）之间的对应关系，并证明了归一化的环面数据能够重构其有限二次模（finite quadratic module）$(G,q)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展拓扑场论的完整分类问题是一个核心理论问题。近期工作表明$(G,q)$可分类扩展理论，因此需要证明只需要亏格一（genus-one）数据即可确定全亏格理论，无需选择$K$-矩阵表示。此外，在存在缺陷或噪声的实际物理场景中，需要验证最近邻行解码（nearest-row decoding）算法的鲁棒性。

Method: 论文采用数学物理框架，建立环面贝里矩阵的射影完整性与映射类群表示之间的严格对应关系。通过分析归一化的环面数据来重构有限二次模$(G,q)$，并利用对称幺半自然同构（symmetric monoidal natural isomorphism）的分类结果将亏格一数据提升到全亏格理论。在解码部分，推导了归一化特征行误差（character row error）的定量容错阈值，分析最近邻行解码恢复阿贝尔融合代数（fusion algebra）的条件。

Result: 论文证明了归一化的环面数据能够重构有限二次模$(G,q)$，从而确定全亏格扩展TQFT。同时证明了当归一化特征行误差满足$\delta<21.96\%$时，最近邻行解码可以独立于任意子（anyon）数量地恢复阿贝尔融合代数。

Conclusion: 该结果适用于由偶格陈-西蒙斯理论（even-lattice Chern-Simons theory）描述的阿贝尔分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum hall）相和自旋液体（spin-liquid）相，为这类物理系统的完整拓扑分类和鲁棒解码提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We show that for Abelian Chern-Simons topological orders, torus Berry matrices determine the all-genus extended TQFT. We identify the topological part of the projective Berry holonomy under metric deformations with the mapping-class-group representation of the Abelian Chern-Simons TQFT and prove that the normalized torus data reconstruct its finite quadratic module $(G,q)$. Recent work showed that $(G,q)$ classifies the extended theory up to symmetric monoidal natural isomorphism, then genus-one data determine the all-genus theory without choosing a $K$-matrix presentation. We also prove that, for normalized character row errors $δ<21.96$%, nearest-row decoding recovers the Abelian fusion algebra independently of the number of anyons. The result applies to Abelian fractional quantum Hall and spin-liquid phases described by even-lattice Chern-Simons theories.

</details>


### [152] [Tilted $p$-wave magnet candidate CeNiAsO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19856)
*Zhuo Wang,Zheng Liu,Shuo Zou,Hua-Xun Li,Jin-Xin Hu,Zhuolun Qiu,Ze Wang,Jiamin Gong,Lucheng Wei,Kangjian Luo,Hai Zeng,Meng Zhang,Chao Dong,Chuanyin Xi,Junfeng Wang,Jiakun Fang,Xiaotao Han,Guang-Han Cao,Liang Li,Yongkang Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过75As核四极/磁共振实验，本文揭示了CeNiAsO中一种具有极小面外磁矩（m_z≈0.05 μB）的共线反铁磁序，并将其定义为一种新的"倾斜p波磁体"范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CeNiAsO作为关联p波磁体的候选材料，其意外极小的有序磁矩给磁结构的精确测定带来了严重挑战，阻碍了对该材料基本性质的理解。

Method: 利用核磁共振对局域内部场的高灵敏度，作者对CeNiAsO进行了75As核四极/磁共振（nuclear quadrupole / magnetic resonance）实验测量。

Result: 实验揭示了一种共度反铁磁序（commensurate antiferromagnetic order），其面外磁矩极小（m_z≈0.05 μB）；这一倾斜的磁构型不仅使自旋极化轴偏离晶体c轴，还增强了非相对论自旋劈裂。

Conclusion: 作者将这种稀有的磁性范式命名为"倾斜p波磁体"（tilted p-wave magnet），为理解相关材料中非相对论自旋劈裂增强机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: The unexpectedly small ordered moments of CeNiAsO, a candidate for correlated $p$-wave magnet, have posed a serious challenge to the precise determination of its magnetic structure, hindering the understanding of its fundamental properties. By leveraging the high sensitivity to local internal fields, our $^{75}$As nuclear quadrupole / magnetic resonance experiments reveal a commensurate antiferromagnetic order with a small out-of-plane moment $m_z\approx0.05$ $μ_{\mathrm{B}}$. This tilted magnetic configuration not only rotates the spin polarization axis away from the crystallographic $\mathbf{c}$-axis, but also enhances the non-relativistic spin splitting. We refer to this rare paradigm as a \textit{tilted $p$-wave magnet}.

</details>


### [153] [Volatile resistive-switched state in a bulk organic conductor with a sharp metal-insulator transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19864)
*Riku Ishii,Ryo Motohashi,Keitaro Tada,Yusuke Suzuki,Takayoshi Kouchi,Hiroshi Oike,Fumitaka Kagawa,Reizo Kato,Tetsuaki Itou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过在块体有机导体($d$7-DMe-DCNQI)$_{2}$Cu中研究电阻开关（resistive switching）态，利用其极锐利的一级金属-绝缘体转变（MIT）和微弱热耗散特性，清晰地揭示了焦耳热（Joule heating）引发的非线性热效应，观察到温度锁定于MIT以及"反欧姆定律"（inverse Ohm's law）现象，为下一代高效电阻开关技术提供了基础物理见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联电子系统中的挥发性电阻开关对下一代电子器件至关重要，但其底层物理机制尚不明确。无机薄膜平台因宽温区金属-绝缘体转变（MIT）和显著热耗散，使焦耳热引发的非线性效应过于复杂而难以解析，亟需更理想的研究平台。

Method: 选取经历极锐利一级MIT且热耗散微弱的块体有机导体($d$7-DMe-DCNQI)$_{2}$Cu作为研究对象，通过电阻测量和$^{1}$H-NMR（质子核磁共振）测量，系统探测其在外加电流下的电阻开关态行为。

Result: 在极端的材料条件下，焦耳热效应得以鲜明显现，观察到两个特殊现象：温度锁定（temperature locking）于金属-绝缘体转变温度，以及电压与电流呈反比关系的"反欧姆定律"行为。

Conclusion: 这些发现为电阻开关中的非线性热效应提供了基础性物理见解，为发展高效电阻开关技术开辟了可行路径。

Abstract: Volatile resistive switching in correlated-electron systems, characterized by an abrupt resistance decrease under applied current, is crucial for developing next-generation electronics. Despite its technological significance, the underlying physics remains elusive. Inorganic thin films on substrates---the widely studied platform for resistive switching---usually exhibit broad temperature-induced metal-insulator transitions (MITs) and substantial heat dissipation. These factors complicate the nonlinear thermal effect induced by Joule heating, a key contributor to resistive switching, rendering it excessively complex and difficult to decipher. Here we investigate a resistive-switched state in the bulk organic conductor ($d$7-DMe-DCNQI)$_{2}$Cu, which undergoes an extremely sharp first-order MIT and exhibits weak heat dissipation, using resistance and $^{1}$H-NMR measurements. These extreme conditions make the Joule heating effect vivid, allowing us to observe peculiar phenomena, including temperature locking to the MIT and `inverse Ohm's law'---an inverse proportionality between voltage and current. These findings provide fundamental insights into the nonlinear thermal effect in resistive switching, offering a pathway to efficient resistive-switching technologies.

</details>


### [154] [Universal meson spectra near $(1+1)$-dimensional Ising criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20221)
*Xiao Wang,Jianda Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究证明了在近(1+1)维伊辛临界点附近，磁扰动诱导的禁闭现象所产生的介子束缚态在独立重新标定模型依赖的微观耦合后，遵循普适的质量标度定律。通过哈密顿截断法和张量网络计算，作者在伊辛场论、近临界混合场伊辛链以及BaCo₂V₂O₈等准一维反铁磁模型中验证了这一普适性，表明量子伊辛临界性的普适标度结构延伸至附近的禁闭区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在(1+1)维伊辛临界点附近，磁扰动会诱导禁闭并产生一系列被称为介子（meson）的束缚态激发，但已有研究缺乏对这些介子光谱普适组织的理解。本文旨在探索介子质量标度是否具有模型无关的普适结构，并为解释准一维伊辛类磁性材料在混合场下的激发提供实用判据（超越E₈可积性框架）。

Method: 采用哈密顿截断法（Hamiltonian truncation method）在伊辛场论和近临界混合场伊辛链（MFIC）中数值获得最轻介子质量轨迹；同时利用张量网络（tensor-network）方法计算四周期自旋-1/2海森堡-伊辛链中BaCo₂V₂O₈对应的最轻介子质量轨迹。所有结果均在独立重新标定微观耦合后与场论结果进行对比。

Result: 独立重新标定后，MFIC的介子质量轨迹和稳定介子计数交叉窗口均与场论结果重合；四周期自旋链家族中BaCo₂V₂O₈的最轻介子质量轨迹同样坍缩到与场论一致的普适曲线上。稳定介子的数量受最轻双介子阈值（two-meson threshold）控制，最轻介子质量由单一标度参数刻画其连续轨迹。

Conclusion: 量子伊辛临界点的普适标度结构延伸至附近的禁闭区域，主导了介子光谱的组织方式。这一结果为在E₈可积性之外解读准一维伊辛类磁性材料在混合场中的激发谱提供了实用判据。

Abstract: Near $(1+1)$-dimensional [$(1+1)$D] Ising criticality, a magnetic perturbation induces confinement and produces a cascade of bound-state excitations known as mesons. Here we show these mesons share a universal mass scaling after independently rescaling the model-dependent microscopic couplings. The number of stable mesons is controlled by the lightest two-meson threshold, while the lightest-meson mass follows a continuous trajectory characterized by a single scaling parameter. Using Hamiltonian truncation method, we obtain the trajectory numerically in both Ising field theory and the near-critical mixed-field Ising chain (MFIC). Under the rescaling, the trajectory and stable-meson-count crossover windows of MFIC both collapse onto the field-theory results. To further demonstrate the above universal organization of the meson spectra, we consider a class of four-periodic spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg-Ising chains under transverse fields, whose parameter space contains a family of quantum Ising critical points. The Hamiltonian family includes effective spin models for the quasi-one-dimensional antiferromagnets Ba(Sr)Co$_2$V$_2$O$_8$. Using tensor-network calculations, we obtain the corresponding lightest-meson mass trajectory for BaCo$_2$V$_2$O$_8$ and find that it also collapse onto the same universal curve given by field-theory result. Our results suggest that the universal scaling structure of quantum Ising criticality extends into the nearby confining regime, governing the organization of the meson spectrum. They thereby provide a practical criterion for interpreting excitations of quasi-1D Ising-like magnets in mixed fields beyond $E_8$ integrability.

</details>


### [155] [Pressure-tuning of electronic structure of CeTe3 probed by femtosecond collective mode spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20057)
*Chandra V. Kotyada,Priyanka Yogi,Amon P. Lanz,Rolf Heid,Jonas Tauch,Manuel Obergfell,Hanjo Schaefer,Paula Giraldo-Gallo,Ian R. Fisher,Alexej Pashkin,Jure Demsar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用飞秒光学光谱（femtosecond optical spectroscopy）在流体静压（hydrostatic pressure）条件下探究了典型电荷密度波（charge-density-wave, CDW）系统CeTe3中有序参数相干动力学的演化。研究表明，随着压力增加，CDW转变温度从环境压力下的约570 K降至约6 GPa时接近室温；压力依赖的恢复动力学表明电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）增强，CDW抑制主要由费米面嵌套（Fermi-surface nesting）减弱驱动。7 GPa以上在低温下未观察到CDW序，同时低温弛豫动力学显著减慢，与压力增强的Ce 4f局域电子与巡游载流子杂化（hybridization）引发的重电子（heavy-electron）行为一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解电荷密度波系统中的压力如何调控有序参数动力学及其潜在机制，特别是区分费米面嵌套减弱与电子-声子耦合变化在CDW抑制中的相对贡献，并探索压力诱导的重电子行为。

Method: 采用飞秒光学光谱（femtosecond optical spectroscopy）在流体静压条件下测量CeTe3单晶的有序参数恢复动力学与弛豫行为，追踪CDW转变温度随压力的变化。

Result: CDW转变温度从环境压力下约570 K降至约6 GPa的接近室温；压力的增加伴随电子-声子耦合增强，而CDW抑制主要由费米面嵌套减弱驱动。7 GPa以上低温下无CDW序，且低温度弛豫显著减慢，呈现重电子行为。

Conclusion: CDW的抑制主要由压力导致的费米面嵌套减弱引起，而非电子-声子耦合削弱；高压下低温弛豫减慢表明Ce 4f与巡游载流子的杂化增强，促使系统向重电子（heavy-electron）行为过渡。

Abstract: We use femtosecond optical spectroscopy to study the evolution of coherent order-parameter dynamics in the prototypical charge-density-wave (CDW) system CeTe3 under hydrostatic pressure. The CDW transition temperature decreases from approximately 570 K at ambient pressure to near room temperature at approximately 6 GPa. The pressure dependence of the order-parameter recovery dynamics indicates enhanced electron-phonon coupling with increasing pressure, implying that CDW suppression is driven predominantly by the reduction in Fermi-surface nesting. Above 7 GPa, no evidence of CDW order is observed down to cryogenic temperatures. Concurrently, the relaxation dynamics exhibit significant slowing down at low-temperatures, consistent with emerging heavy-electron behaviour due to pressure-enhanced hybridization between localized Ce 4f levels and itinerant carriers.

</details>


### [156] [Nonlinear Drude weight of the one-dimensional Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20269)
*Tetsuya Iwasaki,Hosho Katsura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过精确Bethe-ansatz计算与低能有效场论相结合的方法，系统研究了一维排斥性Hubbard模型中的非线性Drude权重（NLDW）。研究揭示了半填充时NLDWs在Mott金属-绝缘体相变附近的临界标度行为，并指出了传统低能有效描述在高阶输运研究中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性输运系数（如非线性Drude权重）是理解强关联体系中高阶输运性质的关键，但基于Tomonaga-Luttinger液体（TLL）的低能有效场论对其预测存在未经验证的不确定性，亟需与精确方法进行系统对比。

Method: 将精确Bethe-ansatz计算与低能有效场论相结合。在四分之一填充下推导了NLDWs的强耦合展开并数值验证；在TLL描述中加入无关微扰（irrelevant perturbations）进行比较。在半填充下，通过有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析Mott金属-绝缘体相变附近NLDWs的渐近行为，并提出超标度假说（hyperscaling ansatz）。

Result: 带曲率修正对高阶NLDWs产生有限贡献，而Umklapp相互作用在Drude权重的阶数n超过由TLL参数决定的阈值时预测发散行为；然而精确Bethe-ansatz的有限尺寸标度显示所有计算的NLDWs在热力学极限下保持有限，与低能有效场论预测存在分歧。半填充下，绝缘相中NLDWs的渐近行为被导出，所提出的超标的假说在临界点附近得到数值验证。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了一维Hubbard模型中非线性输运系数的相互作用依赖性和临界标度行为，同时揭示了传统低能有效描述对高阶输运性质的局限性，为修正和拓展相关场论方法提供了精确的基准。

Abstract: We investigate nonlinear Drude weights (NLDWs) in the one-dimensional repulsive Hubbard model by combining exact Bethe-ansatz calculations with low-energy effective field theory. At quarter filling, we first derive the strong-coupling expansion of the NLDWs and confirm it numerically over a wide range of interaction strength. We then compare the numerical results with the prediction of the Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid (TLL) description including irrelevant perturbations. While band-curvature corrections yield finite contributions to higher-order NLDWs, the Umklapp interaction predicts divergent NLDWs when the order $n$ of the Drude weight exceeds a threshold determined by the TLL parameter. In contrast, finite-size scaling of the exact Bethe-ansatz results indicates that all calculated NLDWs remain finite in the thermodynamic limit, revealing a discrepancy between the exact results and the predictions of the low-energy effective field theory. At half filling, we analyze the finite-size scaling of the NLDWs across the Mott metal-insulator transition. We derive their asymptotic behavior in the insulating phase and propose a hyperscaling ansatz for NLDWs near the critical point, which is verified numerically. Our results clarify the interaction dependence and critical scaling of nonlinear transport coefficients in the one-dimensional Hubbard model and highlight limitations of the conventional low-energy effective description for higher-order transport.

</details>

### [157] [Growth phases of an active tissue: determinate, indeterminate, and proportionate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20091)
*Jigyasa Watwani,K. Vijay Kumar,Vishal Vasan*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个沿单一轴线生长的活性黏弹性连续介质模型（active viscoelastic continuum model），其中细胞分裂与死亡产生主动应力（active stress）。模型发现两种渐近状态——组织达到由材料参数决定的有限相对尺寸（确定型生长）或无限线性伸长（不确定型生长），其临界条件由主动应力与弹性模量之比决定。由不同材料属性部件组成的组织会形成由机械阻抗（mechanical impedance）而非遗传决定的比例尺寸，从而将确定型、不确定型与比例型生长统一在一个连续介质力学框架内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生长可能在达到目标尺寸后停止（确定型生长）或终生持续（不确定型生长），但此前缺乏统一的物理机制来解释这两种截然不同的生长表型如何产生，以及不同组织部分之间尺寸比例的形成机制。

Method: 作者构建了一个沿单一轴线生长的活性黏弹性连续介质模型，将细胞分裂与死亡建模为产生主动应力的活性过程，通过材料参数和应力平衡分析研究组织的渐近生长行为。

Result: 模型揭示了两种渐近状态：组织达到由材料参数决定的有限相对尺寸，或线性无界伸长。状态选择由主动应力与弹性模量之比决定，其转变源于组织可承受的弹性应力存在上限——当活性足够大时应力无法被平衡。在异质材料组成的组织中，生长相会趋于由各部件机械阻抗决定而非遗传决定的固定长度比例；若阻抗与初始长度匹配，则保留组织初始的尺寸比例。

Conclusion: 确定型、不确定型与比例型生长可被统一视为同一连续介质力学框架下的不同涌现机制，为理解组织生长模式提供了统一的机械物理基础。

Abstract: Growth may cease at a target size or continue throughout life: the determinate and indeterminate phenotypes. We develop an active viscoelastic continuum model of a tissue growing along one axis, in which cell division and death generate active stresses. We find two asymptotic states: one in which the tissue reaches a relative size fixed by its material parameters, and one in which it elongates linearly without bound. Which state is realised is set by the ratio of active stress to elastic modulus. The transition originates in a bound on the elastic stress the tissue can support: a sufficiently large activity can never be balanced. In a tissue made of parts with different material properties, the growing phase settles into fixed length proportions, set by the mechanical impedances of the parts rather than inherited; matching impedances to initial lengths preserves the proportions the tissue began with. Determinate, indeterminate and proportionate growth thus appear as regimes of one continuum mechanical framework.

</details>

### [158] [Hamiltonian Floer Theory for Quantum Electrodynamics up to First Order in $\hbar$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19996)
*Oliver Fabert,Jesse Straat*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文运用哈密顿Floer理论（Hamiltonian Floer theory）证明了一个关于带有高斯随机场（Gaussian random field）的粒子-场系统周期解存在性的cuplength结果，并以随机电动力学（stochastic electrodynamics）作为具体模型进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子-场系统在随机场背景下是否存在周期解是一个基础但重要的问题，而现有方法缺乏系统的几何框架来证明此类解的存在性。作者希望通过建立这类问题与Floer理论之间的联系，为随机背景下动力系统的周期解研究提供新的工具。

Method: 作者采用哈密顿Floer理论作为核心分析工具，将其应用于描述带有高斯随机场的粒子-场系统，并以此证明了关于周期解存在性的cuplength结果。同时引入随机电动力学作为具体物理模型加以验证，该模型在低阶哈密顿量或经典粒子处理条件下精确近似量子电动力学。

Result: 证明了带有高斯随机场的粒子-场系统存在周期解的cuplength结果，并确认该结果在随机电动力学这一具体模型下成立。

Conclusion: 哈密顿Floer理论为随机场背景下的粒子-场系统周期解存在性提供了有效的几何证明框架，而随机电动力学作为该理论的验证模型，在低阶近似下与量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics）一致。

Abstract: We use Hamiltonian Floer theory to prove a cuplength result about the existence of periodic solutions of particle-field systems with a Gaussian random field. As a concrete model we study stochastic electrodynamics, which approximates quantum electrodynamics up to first order in $\hbar$, and is even exact in the case of low-order Hamiltonians or when the particles are treated classically.

</details>

### [159] [Green BOA: Determining the environmental break-even point for ML-based data compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19994)
*Caterina Doglioni,Akshat Gupta,Thomas Elliott,Hanzila Hussain,Sanjiban Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文总结了曼彻斯特大学两个暑期实习项目的成果，以基于机器学习（ML）的无损压缩算法为例，比较了ML训练与推理所需基础设施的碳当量排放与因减少磁盘存储需求而带来的碳当量节省，并讨论了二者的盈亏平衡点（break-even point）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于评估基于机器学习的压缩算法在环境可持续性方面的实际净收益。虽然ML压缩算法能减少存储需求，但其训练和推理过程中消耗的计算资源同样产生碳排放，需要系统性比较两种效应的盈亏平衡点。

Method: 采用基于ML的无损压缩算法作为具体案例，将ML训练与推理所需基础设施的碳当量排放估算值与因缩减磁盘存储需求所产生的碳当量节省进行对比分析，从而评估其环境可持续性平衡点。

Result: 研究得出了ML压缩算法在环境可持续性上的盈亏平衡点估算结果，明确了ML训练与推理碳排放和存储节省碳排放之间的比较关系。

Conclusion: 研究为评估ML驱动的数据压缩算法是否在环境可持续性意义上值得实施提供了定量依据，揭示了其碳收支平衡的关键条件。

Abstract: We summarise the outcome of two summer internship projects based at the University of Manchester, focused on the break-even point in terms of environmental sustainability for ML-based data compression algorithms. Using the example of a ML-based lossless compression algorithm, we compare estimates for the carbon-equivalent of the infrastructure needed for ML training and inference with the carbon-equivalent savings from reduced disk storage requirements, and discuss their break-even point.

</details>

### [160] [Layered matter that maintains spacing but loses stacking order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19365)
*Osvaldo Trigueiro Neto,Paulo Henrique Michels-Brito,Bruno Ceccato Telli,Natalie Jasmin Eichstaedt,Leide P. Cavalcanti,Tomás S. Plivelic,Josef Breu,Jon Otto Fossum*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 在层状材料中，层间距与堆叠有序度的锁定关系通常在溶胀悬浮体系中成立，但本文在带电刚性纳米片的溶胀悬浮体中实现了两者的解耦，形成一种区别于晶体溶胀和维格纳溶胀的独特状态，并通过近完美模型材料验证了其内在本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状材料中层间距与堆叠有序度通常紧密锁定，本文旨在探究在刚性带电纳米片的溶胀悬浮体系中，这种锁定关系能否被打破，从而揭示一种此前未被识别的物理状态。

Method: 作者采用X射线散射（X-ray scattering）和中子散射（neutron scattering）、沉降实验（sedimentation）以及Donnan分析（Donnan analysis）对近完美模型材料进行表征，测定层间距、堆叠层数以及渗透恢复斜率（osmotic restoring slope）等关键参数。

Result: 研究发现平均层间距保持尖锐且可通过盐浓度调节，而散射加权堆叠（scattering-weighted stacking）仅跨越两到三层。层间距由无参数渗透恢复斜率（低于1帕斯卡每纳米）维持，该斜率极弱，使得层间距处于平衡态而缺陷缓慢弛豫，形成一种淬火亚稳态的层间配准（quenched metastable registry）。

Conclusion: 该解耦行为预期普遍存在于从黏土、氧化物纳米片到氧化石墨烯（graphene oxide）的刚性溶胀纳米片体系中，标志着低维周期序中类老化弛豫（ageing-like relaxation）这一此前未被实现的物理现象。

Abstract: In layered materials, spacing and stacking-order extent are usually locked. Here we show that in swollen suspensions of stiff, charged nanosheets they decouple, and that this defines a distinct regime, apart from the crystalline- and Wigner-swelling regimes such systems usually occupy. The mean spacing stays sharp and salinity-tunable while scattering-weighted stacking spans only two to three layers. We demonstrate this in a near-perfect model material, so the behaviour is intrinsic, not defect-driven. X-ray and neutron scattering, sedimentation and a Donnan analysis show the spacing is held by a parameter-free osmotic restoring slope below one pascal per nanometre. Because the slope is so weak, the spacing sits at equilibrium while faults relax slowly, a quenched metastable registry whose ageing-like relaxation of low-dimensional periodic order has not, to our knowledge, been realised before. The same decoupling is expected across stiff, swollen nanosheets, from clays to oxide nanosheets and graphene oxide.

</details>


### [161] [Role of topology in scaling laws for studying mechanics in open-porous solids: Moving beyond classical Gibson-Ashby scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20003)
*Ameya Rege*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑（topology）的框架，用于解析无序多孔材料（disordered porous materials）中弹性模量与相对密度之间的幂律标度关系，通过分离固有力学贡献与网络结构效应，解释了表观标度指数（apparent scaling exponent）偏离固有指数的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔材料的弹性模量通常用与相对密度的幂律标度关系描述，其标度指数常被归因于底层变形机制。然而在高度无序的多孔网络中，密度变化往往伴随网络拓扑变化，这会显著改变表观标度行为，因此需要将拓扑效应与固有力学贡献分离以准确理解标度指数。

Method: 提出一个拓扑信息知情框架（topology-informed framework），分别考虑三个代表性拓扑描述符：平均配位数（mean coordination number）、承载骨干网络分数（fraction of the load-bearing backbone）和载荷路径曲折度（tortuosity of the load paths）。对每种情况，推导并分析其对表观模量标度指数的密度依赖贡献。

Result: 结果表明，连通性变化、固相力学参与度的变化以及载荷路径效率的变化均可导致表观标度指数超过与局部变形机制相关的固有指数，且这些效应在低相对密度区域尤为显著，因为该区域内网络拓扑演化最为剧烈。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解无序多孔材料中异常的模量-密度标度行为提供了物理解释基础，并强调在考虑相对密度的同时必须显式地纳入拓扑因素。

Abstract: The elastic modulus of porous materials is commonly described using power-law scaling relations with relative density, where the scaling exponent is often interpreted in terms of the underlying deformation mechanism. However, in highly disordered porous networks, changes in density are generally accompanied by changes in network topology, which can substantially modify the apparent scaling behavior. In this paper, we propose a topology-informed framework that separates the intrinsic mechanical contribution from the effects of network structure. Three representative topological descriptors are considered: the mean coordination number, the fraction of the load-bearing backbone, and the tortuosity of the load paths. For each case, the corresponding density-dependent contribution to the apparent modulus-scaling exponent is derived and analyzed. The results show that variations in connectivity, mechanical participation of the solid phase, and load-path efficiency can all lead to apparent scaling exponents exceeding the intrinsic exponent associated with the local deformation mechanism. These effects are particularly pronounced at low relative densities, where network topology evolves most strongly. The framework provides a physically interpretable basis for understanding anomalous modulus-density scaling in disordered porous materials and highlights the need to consider topology explicitly alongside relative density.

</details>


### [162] [Cooperative effects of membrane confinement and gelation on PEG crystallization pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19724)
*Masaki Yoshida,Naoya Yanagisawa,Fumiya Kanie,Hidemasa Teraoka,Ikki Morichika,Satoshi Ashihara,Tetsuya Hama,Miho Yanagisawa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过偏振光显微镜和红外显微光谱等手段，发现包裹在脂质包被琼脂糖微凝胶中的聚乙二醇（PEG）在体相中不结晶的条件下仍能形成晶体，并且这一结晶路径经由亚稳无定形前驱体（metastable amorphous precursor）实现，膜润湿（membrane wetting）与凝胶化（gelation）的协同作用改变了PEG的局部水合环境，进而调控其结晶行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脂质包被的微米级水凝胶为聚合物的相行为提供了与体相显著不同的受限、水合环境，但此前对其中聚合物结晶路径的机制理解仍不充分。作者旨在探究PEG在微凝胶受限环境中的结晶通路及其调控因素。

Method: 采用偏振光显微镜（polarized-light microscopy）检测微凝胶中的双折射现象；通过加热与冷却及局部机械刺激实验考察双折射的可逆性变化；利用红外显微光谱（infrared microspectroscopy）区分微凝胶中PEG的结晶态与无定形态；通过降温实验观察PEG富集域的空间分布。

Result: 在体相中PEG不结晶的条件下，微凝胶内PEG出现双折射，指示结晶发生；双折射随加热消失并在冷却或机械刺激后重现。光谱结果显示双折射微凝胶含结晶态PEG，而非双折射微凝胶含无定形态PEG，证实存在亚稳前驱体。降温至相分离温度以下时，PEG富集域优先出现在膜附近，表明膜润湿主导结晶前的空间分布。

Conclusion: 膜限制与琼脂糖凝胶化通过协同改变PEG的局部水合环境，稳定了无定形前驱体并改变后续结晶路径，膜润湿与凝胶化的耦合是受限软材料中PEG结晶的关键调控因素。

Abstract: Lipid-coated microscale hydrogels provide confined, hydrated environments in which polymer phase behavior can differ markedly from that in bulk. Here, we investigate the crystallization pathway of poly(ethylene glycol) (PEG) encapsulated in lipid-coated agarose microgels. Surprisingly, polarized-light microscopy reveals birefringence in the microgels under conditions where PEG remains non-crystalline in the corresponding bulk solution. The birefringence disappears upon heating and spontaneously reappears after further cooling or upon local mechanical stimulation. Infrared microspectroscopy demonstrates that the birefringent microgels contain crystalline PEG, whereas non-birefringent microgels contain PEG in an amorphous-like state, indicating the existence of a metastable precursor prior to crystallization. Furthermore, cooling below the phase-separation temperature produces PEG-rich domains preferentially near the membrane, suggesting that membrane wetting governs the spatial distribution of PEG before crystallization. Together, these results indicate that membrane confinement and agarose gelation cooperatively alter the local hydration environment of PEG, thereby stabilizing an amorphous-like precursor and redirecting the subsequent crystallization pathway. Our findings identify the coupling of membrane wetting and gelation as a key factor governing PEG crystallization in confined soft materials.

</details>


### [163] [A Central Disulfide Junction Drives Transient Network Formation in Elastin-Like Polypeptides, Enabling Low-Concentration Hydrogels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19810)
*Tingting Zhang,Jean-François Le Meins,Jean-Paul Chapel,Saron Catak,Guillaume Goudounet,Olivier Sandre,Nadia Mahmoudi,Christophe Schatz,Bertrand Garbay*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究了由亲水性类弹性蛋白多肽（ELP）嵌段和短脂肪酸端基（C16）组成的缔合三嵌段共聚物在水溶液中的自组装行为。研究发现，分子量相近但结构不同的两种聚合物表现出截然不同的自组装性质：C16-80-C16形成动力学受限的大疏水聚集体，而C16-(40)2-C16则通过中心二硫键促进小缔合节点的形成，进而在低至2.5 wt%浓度下形成透明水凝胶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过对比两种分子量和组成几乎相同但结构略有差异的缔合三嵌段共聚物，揭示中心连接方式（线性连接 vs. 二硫键桥接）对聚合物自组装行为和网络形成能力的决定性影响。

Method: 采用振荡流变学（oscillatory rheology）表征水凝胶的线性黏弹性和松弛过程；结合小角X射线散射（SAXS）、光散射、冷冻透射电子显微镜（cryo-TEM）以及分子模拟，对自组装结构进行多尺度表征。

Result: C16-80-C16形成大的疏水聚集体，呈动力学捕获状态且无法形成动态连接的网络；而C16-(40)2-C16形成聚集数仅约3条链的极小缔合节点，与较大聚集体共存，并在高浓度下动态互连形成透明水凝胶（最低浓度2.5 wt%）。流变学结果表明该凝胶呈现由单一松弛过程控制的瞬态Maxwell网络，该松弛过程与C16端基的可逆缔合相关。

Conclusion: 中心二硫键连接是促进瞬态网络形成的关键结构因素：SAXS、光散射、cryo-TEM和分子模拟一致支持中心二硫键节点促进瞬态网络形成的模型，表明聚合物的拓扑结构而非分子量或组成本身决定了其缔合行为和凝胶化能力。

Abstract: The self-assembly of associative triblock copolymers composed of a central hydrophilic elastin-like polypeptide (ELP) block and short fatty acid end groups (C16) was investigated in aqueous solution. In one system, the ELP contains 80 pentapeptide units (C16-80-C16), whereas in the other two C16-ELP40 chains were oxidatively coupled through their terminal cysteine residues to form a central disulfide bond, yielding C16-(40)2-C16. Despite their nearly identical molecular weights and compositions, the two polymers exhibit markedly different self-assembly behaviors. C16-80-C16 forms large hydrophobic aggregates that remain kinetically trapped and do not develop a dynamically connected network. In contrast, C16-(40)2-C16 forms very small associative nodes with an aggregation number of only $\sim$3 chains. These nodes coexist with larger clusters and become dynamically interconnected at higher concentrations, leading to transparent hydrogels at concentrations as low as 2.5 wt %. Oscillatory rheology reveals a transient Maxwell network governed by a single relaxation process associated with the reversible association of the C16 end groups. SAXS, light scattering, cryo-TEM, and molecular modeling consistently support a model in which the central disulfide junction promotes transient network formation.

</details>


### [164] [A solvent-flux theory for nonequilibrium swelling dynamics of thermoresponsive microgels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19884)
*Arturo Moncho-Jordá,Alessandro Patti,Fabián A. García-Daza,Alejandro Cuetos*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对球形微凝胶（microgel）动态行为的溶剂通量（solvent-flux）理论，该理论通过渗透压失衡驱动半径变化率、并耦合聚合物网络中的水输运，导出了非线性溶胀动力学方程及状态与温度依赖的溶胀扩散系数。在非线性区间内，模型可预测非对称的溶胀/退溶胀路径、有限热化引起的弛豫—热化转变以及基于Smoluchowski方程的随机扩展所揭示的尺寸涨落展宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度响应型微凝胶在大体积变化过程中，聚合物体积分数、力学响应和溶剂输运同步演化，导致其非平衡溶胀与退溶胀动力学的预测极具挑战。现有理论缺乏能够统一描述这些耦合效应的框架。

Method: 发展了一个溶剂通量理论，将微凝胶半径变化率视为由粒子边界处的渗透压失衡驱动、并被聚合物网络中的水输运所抵抗的过程。该理论产生非线性全局溶胀坐标方程和状态与温度相关的溶胀扩散系数D_SW(φ,T)，并在线性响应极限还原因子Tanaka-Fillmore指数弛豫；在此基础上构建了基于Smoluchowski方程的随机扩展以描述尺寸涨落。

Result: 对于pNIPAM微凝胶，模型预测非对称动力学路径：相同温度区间内退溶胀快于溶胀。有限热化导致从微凝胶控制区间到热化控制区间的交叉，表观弛豫时间随外部热化时间线性增长，并在半径—温度平面出现类似迟滞（hysteresis）的环。随机扩展预测坍塌过程中半径分布的瞬态展宽，体积转变区域涨落最大。

Conclusion: 该理论将溶剂输运、非线性溶胀动力学、热化效应和响应性微凝胶的非平衡尺寸涨落统一起来，为理解和预测温度响应微凝胶的完整动态行为提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Thermoresponsive microgels undergo large reversible size changes as temperature alters solvent quality. Predicting their nonequilibrium swelling and deswelling kinetics is challenging because polymer volume fraction, mechanical response, and solvent transport evolve during large volume changes. Here we develop a solvent-flux theory for the dynamics of a spherical microgel. The radius-change rate is driven by the osmotic-pressure imbalance across the particle boundary and resisted by water transport through the polymer network, yielding a nonlinear equation for the global swelling coordinate and a state- and temperature-dependent swelling diffusion coefficient, $D_{\mathrm{SW}}(φ,T)$. In the linear-response regime, the theory recovers Tanaka--Fillmore exponential relaxation and the scaling $τ_\mathrm{SW}\sim R_{\mathrm{eq}}^2γ/K_{\mathrm{eq}}$, while providing a microscopic interpretation of the polymer--solvent friction coefficient $γ$. Beyond this limit, the model retains quadratic size scaling while accounting for state-dependent transport and mechanics. For pNIPAM microgels, it predicts asymmetric pathways, with deswelling faster than swelling over the same temperature interval. Finite thermalization produces a crossover from a microgel-controlled to a thermalization-controlled regime, in which the apparent relaxation time grows linearly with the external thermalization time and hysteresis-like loops emerge in the radius--temperature plane. A stochastic extension based on the Smoluchowski equation predicts transient broadening of the radius distribution during collapse, with maximal fluctuations in the volume-transition region. The theory links solvent transport, nonlinear swelling dynamics, thermalization effects, and nonequilibrium size fluctuations in responsive microgels.

</details>


### [165] [Unmasking the internal structure of casein micelles through enzymatic hydrolysis: A SAXS study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19969)
*Julien Bauland,Ghazi Ben Messaoud,François Boué,Pascaline Hamon,Florence Rousseau,Thomas Gibaud,Thomas Croguennec*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究利用时间分辨小角X射线散射（time-resolved SAXS）监测凝乳酶诱导酪蛋白胶束酶促胶凝过程中3 nm至3 μm尺度的结构变化，发现酶驱动聚集可揭示胶束内部组织结构，且κ-酪蛋白裂解导致中间尺度的结构特征逐渐消失，为胶凝过程为探测生物胶体内部结构提供了独特途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 酪蛋白胶束（casein micelles）是最为研究的天然缔合胶体之一，但其内部结构仍存在争议，多种结构模型共存。小角散射是原位探测生物胶体的首选方法，然而由于酪蛋白胶束的层级结构特性，散射数据的解释仍具有挑战性。

Method: 采用凝乳酶（chymosin）诱导的酶促胶凝过程，该酶可裂解κ-酪蛋白并触发聚集。利用时间分辨SAXS在从3 nm到3 μm的尺度范围内探测溶胶-凝胶转变过程中的结构变化，并分析结构特征随时间的消失动力学。

Result: 首先发现酶驱动聚集出乎意料地揭示了酪蛋白胶束的内部组织信息：胶凝过程中比表面积（specific surface area）减少，揭露出之前仅在对比度匹配条件下观察到的高q结构峰。其次，κ-酪蛋白裂解导致中间尺度结构特征逐渐消失，动力学分析表明其诱导了胶体多孔子结构的渐进式松弛，为该结构贡献于胶束组织提供了直接证据。

Conclusion: 胶凝过程为探测生物胶体的内部结构提供了独特途径，并为解释复杂软物质系统中的散射数据提供了新视角。

Abstract: Casein micelles, one of the most studied natural association colloids, are supramolecular assemblies of caseins and colloidal calcium phosphate that constitute the fundamental building blocks of dairy matrices. Despite extensive investigation, the internal structure of casein micelles remains debated. While $κ$-casein is known to ensure colloidal stability of casein micelle suspension, the spatial organization of casein fractions and salts is still unresolved, and several structural models coexist. Small-angle scattering is a method of choice to probe biological colloids \emph{in situ}, yet interpretation of scattering data remains challenging due to the hierarchical nature of casein micelles.
  Here we address this issue by probing micelle structure during enzymatic gelation induced by chymosin, which cleaves $κ$-casein and triggers aggregation. Using time-resolved SAXS, we probe structural changes throughout the sol-gel transition over length scales from 3 nm to 3 $μ$m. First, we show that enzyme-driven aggregation, counterintuitively, reveals information about the internal organization of casein micelles: the reduction of specific surface area during gelation unmasks a high-q structural peak previously observed under contrast-matching conditions. Second, we report that $κ$-casein cleavage leads to a gradual disappearance of the structural feature at intermediate scales. Analysis of the disappearance kinetics and comparison to structural models reveal that $κ$-casein cleavage induces a progressive relaxation of the colloidal porous substructure, providing direct evidence for its contribution to micellar organization. More broadly, these results demonstrate that the gelation process provides unique access to the internal structure of biological colloids and offers new perspectives for interpreting scattering data in complex soft-matter systems.

</details>


### [166] [Effect of molecular constraints on vibrational and quasilocalized excitations in glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20049)
*Keane Ramdin,Edan Lerner*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文直接比较了分子玻璃（molecular glass）与具有相同分子间相互作用势、且采用相同形成协议的原子玻璃（atomistic glass）的力学与振动性质。研究发现分子玻璃具有更高的力学无序度（mechanical disorder）和更大的介观关联长度（mesoscopic correlation length），但每原子软准局域振动（soft quasilocalized vibrations）数量更低；这些差异可通过刚性分子约束减少有效自由度来合理化，同时非线性塑性模与体积应变在分子玻璃中耦合更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然结构玻璃（structural glasses）振动性质的统计物理研究近年取得显著进展，但实际科学与技术相关的大多数玻璃为分子玻璃，而多数计算研究采用简单的原子玻璃形成模型。因此，刚性分子约束对玻璃力学与振动物理的影响在很大程度上尚未被探索，有必要直接比较分子玻璃与原子玻璃的性质差异。

Method: 作者直接构建并比较一对玻璃体系：一个分子计算机玻璃和一个具有相同分子间相互作用势、以相同形成协议（formation protocol）生成的原子玻璃模型。通过对比两者的力学无序度、介观关联长度以及每原子软准局域振动数量等性质，并利用刚性分子约束导致有效自由度减少的框架对差异进行合理化解释。

Result: 研究发现分子玻璃具有更高的力学无序度和更大的介观关联长度，但每原子软准局域振动数量更低。此外，分子玻璃中承载塑性变形的非线性塑性模（nonlinear plastic modes）与体积应变（volumetric strains）的耦合显著更强。

Conclusion: 分子玻璃与原子玻璃在力学和振动性质上存在系统性差异，这些差异可由刚性分子约束引起的有效自由度减少予以解释。未来研究方向已讨论，但摘要未给出具体细节。

Abstract: Recent years have seen substantial progress in elucidating the statistical physics of the vibrational properties of structural glasses. Although many real-world glasses relevant for science and technology are molecular, the majority of computational studies concerning the mechanical and vibrational properties of structural glasses employ simple atomistic glass-forming models. Thus, the effects of stiff molecular constraints on mechanical and vibrational glass physics remain largely unexplored. In this work, we directly compare the properties of a molecular computer glass with those of an atomistic model featuring the same inter-molecular interaction potential, and created using the same formation protocol. We find that the molecular glass features a higher degree of mechanical disorder, with larger mesoscopic correlation lengths, while at the same time it hosts a lower number of soft, quasilocalized vibrations per atom -- compared to the atomistic glass. We rationalize these differences by accounting for the reduction in the effective number of degrees of freedom induced by the stiff molecular constraints. We additionally find that nonlinear plastic modes --- that carry plastic deformation in driven glassy solids --- couple much more strongly to volumetric strains in the molecular glass. Future research directions are discussed.

</details>

### [167] [Modeling Relativistic Tidal Disruptions of MESA Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19402)
*Liam M. Wang,Giovanni Maria Tomaselli,Zihan Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在基于MESA恒星的牛顿引力潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）模型中引入相对论修正，包括赤道Kerr测地线、相对论潮汐场和相对论回流时间，发现恒星更早瓦解、回流时间更长，但黑洞自旋影响甚微，且计算代价仍然低廉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的潮汐瓦解事件（tidal disruption event, TDE）建模依赖计算密集的流体动力学（hydrodynamical）模拟；此前提出的牛顿引力模型虽计算高效，但缺乏相对论效应的准确性，因此需要引入相对论修正以提高模型的物理精度。

Method: 在已有牛顿引力TDE模型（基于MESA恒星的恒星微扰理论）基础上，加入三类相对论修正：赤道Kerr测地线（equatorial Kerr geodesics）、相对论潮汐场（relativistic tidal fields）以及相对论回流时间（relativistic fallback times），并与牛顿情形进行对比分析。

Result: 相较牛顿情形，恒星在轨道上更早被瓦解，因此累积物理形变的时间更短；同时由于瓦解时距离黑洞更远，回流时间（fallback time）更长。黑洞自旋对回流时间的影响可忽略，仅在近心点极近的轨道上才显现。

Conclusion: 该模型相比牛顿模型能够更精确地计算回流速率，同时保持计算成本低廉，代码已在GitHub上开源发布。

Abstract: Tidal disruption events (TDEs) occur when a star passes so close to a black hole that its self-gravity is overcome by the external tidal field. As the star passes, it initially deforms, then is ripped apart, and some of its material eventually falls back on bound orbits, forming an accretion disk around the black hole. A Newtonian model of TDEs, based on stellar perturbation theory of MESA stars, was recently introduced as an alternative to computationally intensive hydrodynamical simulations. In this work, we add relativistic corrections to the model, incorporating equatorial Kerr geodesics, relativistic tidal fields, and relativistic fallback times. Compared to the Newtonian case, we find that stars are disrupted earlier in their orbit, which gives them less time to accumulate physical deformations. Additionally, we find that the increased distance from the black hole at the time of disruption makes the fallback time longer. However, the black hole spin has a negligible impact on fallback time, except for orbits with exceptionally close pericenter. Our results allow for a more accurate calculation of fallback rates than the Newtonian model, while also remaining computationally cheap. The code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [168] [Probabilistic kilonova prediction from gravitational wave inferred binary neutron star parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20262)
*Xiao-Fei Dong,Ik Siong Heng,Gavin P. Lamb,Chris Messenger,Nikhil Sarin,Benjamin Rayson,Nial R. Tanvir,Jessica Irwin,Thomas Wallace,Skye Rosetti,Bo Milvang-Jensen,Andrew J. Levan,Jens Hjorth*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出Genova，一个基于条件归一化流（conditional normalising flow）的概率框架，直接从双中子星并合（binary neutron star mergers）的引力波后验样本预测千新星（kilonova）光谱和光变曲线。该框架在自洽测试中表现出色，并成功应用于GW170817/AT2017gfo的多波段观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千新星是理解双中子星并合系统属性和宇宙r过程核合成（r-process nucleosynthesis）的关键电磁窗口，但迄今仅探测到少数候选体，且只有AT2017gfo与引力波事件GW170817相关联。现有模型预测千新星光谱和光变曲线时，通常需要手动设定许多模型参数，未能充分利用引力波后验信息来系统传播不确定性。

Method: 采用条件归一化流学习静止系光谱在源框架分量质量、潮汐形变率（tidal deformabilities）、观测角度和并合后时间条件下的分布。训练过程中边缘化（marginalise）其他千新星模型参数（如抛射物不透明度），其效应作为预测不确定性传播到预测光谱中。

Result: 自洽测试中，模型再现中位光变曲线残差通常低于约0.1星等，预测的中心68%区间宽度比值在0.4至8.0天范围内主要集中在0.8到1.4之间。与物理上不同的千新星模型比较显示，概率预测在训练模型之外仍具信息量。应用于GW170817/AT2017gfo的多波段观测（包括新给出的Y、J、Ks波段光度数据）时，预测区间总体上覆盖观测结果。

Conclusion: Genova能够同时捕捉引力波后验不确定性和被边缘化的千新星模型参数引起的变异性，为从天体物理探测数据预测千新星电磁信号提供了端到端的概率框架。

Abstract: Kilonovae provide a key electromagnetic window into binary neutron star mergers, revealing the properties of the merging system while probing r-process nucleosynthesis of the Universe. However, only a few kilonova candidates have been detected to date, with AT2017gfo being the only one associated with a gravitational wave event, GW170817. In this work, we present \textsc{Genova}, a probabilistic framework for predicting kilonova spectra and light curves directly from gravitational wave posterior samples of binary neutron star mergers. This method uses a conditional normalising flow to learn the distribution of rest-frame spectra conditioned on the source-frame component masses, tidal deformabilities, viewing angle and time since merger. Other kilonova model parameters, such as ejecta opacities, are marginalised over during training, so that their effects are propagated into the predicted spectra as predictive uncertainty. In the self-consistency test, the flow model reproduces the median light curves with residuals typically below $\sim 0.1$ mag, and the ratio of the predicted central 68\% interval widths remains predominantly between $0.8$ and $1.4$ over $\sim 0.4$--$8.0$ days. Comparisons with a physically distinct kilonova model show that the probabilistic prediction can remain informative beyond the model used for training. We apply \textsc{Genova} to GW170817/AT2017gfo using multi-band observations, including newly re-reduced $Y$-, $J$-, $K_s$-band photometry from the Visible and Infrared Survey Telescope for Astronomy, which we present in this work. The resulting predictive intervals broadly encompass the observations while capturing both gravitational wave posterior uncertainty and the variation induced by marginalised kilonova model parameters.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Emergence of cooperation: A reputation-modulated reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20016)
*Chenyang Zhao,Jiqiang Zhang,Li Chen,Yong Zou*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习（reinforcement learning）范式的空间囚徒困境博弈模型，其中智能体通过Q-learning整合个体与社会信息，并利用局部声誉（reputation）指标指导决策。研究发现声誉调制的学习机制显著促进合作行为涌现，且随诱惑因子增大系统出现从全合作到全背叛的不连续相变（discontinuous phase transition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有博弈论模型大多将声誉视为外部因素，仅用于调节收益、交互结构或策略更新规则。然而在许多社会情境中，声誉实际作为信息发挥作用——它塑造了个体如何解释自身经验并评估他人行为，现有模型未能捕捉这一功能。

Method: 构建一个基于强化学习范式的空间囚徒困境（prisoner's dilemma）博弈模型，智能体配备Q-learning算法，通过局部定义的声誉指标同时整合个体信息与社会信息以指导决策。

Result: 声誉调制的学习机制显著促进了合作行为的涌现；随着诱惑因子（temptation）增大，系统表现出从全合作到全背叛的不连续相变。合作通过合作簇的成核（nucleation）过程扩散，而这些簇的瓦解则驱动系统进入完全背叛的吸收态（absorbing state）。

Conclusion: 声誉促进合作不仅通过提供直接激励实现，还通过重塑智能体赖以学习和适应的社会信息景观（social information landscape）发挥作用。

Abstract: Reputation is widely recognized as a key mechanism for sustaining cooperation. However, most existing game-theoretic models treat reputation primarily as an external factor that modulates payoffs, interaction structures, or strategy update rules. In many social contexts, though, reputation operates primarily as information -- it shapes how individuals interpret their own experiences and assess the behavior of others. To bridge this gap, we propose a spatial prisoner's dilemma game grounded in the reinforcement learning paradigm, in which agents equipped with Q-learning integrate both individual and social information via a locally defined reputation metric to guide their decisions. Our results reveal that reputation-modulated learning significantly promotes the emergence of cooperative behavior, and we observe a discontinuous phase transition from full cooperation to full defection as the temptation increases. Cooperation spreads through the nucleation of cooperative clusters, whereas the disintegration of these clusters drives the system into an absorbing state of complete defection. Overall, this study demonstrates that reputation facilitates cooperation not only by providing direct incentives but also by reshaping the social information landscape that agents rely on for learning and adaptation.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Antipodal constraints on transient Hawking radiation in a conformal channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19244)
*M. Baran Ökten,Aslı S. Turan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在射线追踪（ray tracing）层面研究偏离平稳时空的霍金辐射（Hawking radiation），通过平衡均匀化（balanced uniformisation）在对偶映射中定义诱导反极对合（antipodal involution），证明中心反极奇结构（antipodal odd structure）的创生与擦除对共形通道缺陷（conformal channel defect）的时空积分范数施加了严格下界，其强制性尺度仅由最大中间失配和反极的内在雅可比畸变决定，其余传播修正项需独立控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解非平稳霍金辐射中由零映射的时间演化所控制的对偶通道结构，并量化反极奇模式在辐射过程中的动力学约束。

Method: 采用平衡均匀化方法在固定参考视界切片上定义诱导反极对合；通过标签剥离场（peeling field）分析通道结构，并利用圆球奇标量谐波（odd scalar harmonics）作为解析标定工具。

Result: 证明了反极奇结构的创生与擦除对共形通道缺陷的时空积分范数施加下界；紧凑偶极脉冲（compact dipole pulse）饱和了几何与时间估计；时间平均形式标识出正比于反极剥离振幅除以事件持续时间的瞬态共形通道尺度。

Conclusion: 霍金辐射的反极奇结构动力学受到严格的不等式约束，且其强制性尺度仅依赖于中间失配与反极Jacobian畸变，而灰体滤波、角频率混合、质量与自旋效应等修正需在积分范数意义上独立控制。

Abstract: Hawking radiation away from stationarity is governed, at the ray tracing level, by the retarded time evolution of the null mapping. We consider an extracted massless conformal channel whose outgoing directions are labelled on a fixed reference horizon slice. Balanced uniformisation, after fixing a nondegenerate balanced branch, defines an induced antipodal involution on this slice. We prove that creation and erasure of centred antipodal odd structure in the channel labelled peeling field enforce a lower bound on the time integrated spatial norm of the conformal channel defect. The forcing scale depends only on the largest intermediate mismatch and the intrinsic Jacobian distortion of the induced antipode, while greybody filtering, angular and frequency mixing, mass effects, spin dependence, and residual propagation or extraction corrections enter through a remainder whose integrated norm must be controlled independently. Round sphere odd scalar harmonics provide analytic calibrations, with the compact dipole pulse saturating the geometric and temporal estimates. A time-averaged form identifies a transient conformal channel scale proportional to the antipodal peeling amplitude divided by the episode duration.

</details>


### [171] [On the physical origin of the radial acceleration relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19245)
*Gary Nash*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文提出修改广义相对论（Modified General Relativity, MGR）作为唯一能与度量相容的引力理论，通过对称张量$\varPhi_{\alpha\beta}$完整描述爱因斯坦方程的总能量-动量张量，将暗能量归结为能量分量$\varPhi_{00}$，暗物质归结为应力分量$\varPhi_{ij}$，并由此自然推导出星系中观测到的径向加速度关系（radial acceleration relation, RAR），同时囊括MOND理论、解释宽双星系中的引力异常，并在大尺度上恢复牛顿引力行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 径向加速度关系（RAR）在星系中表现出紧密的相关性，即使是在暗物质主导的系统中也成立，这表明星系中存在一个普适的加速度尺度，既挑战了$Λ$CDM标准宇宙学模型，又与MOND理论存在偏差。因此需要一种从几何上统一解释引力与暗区、并将暗物质视为自旋1粒子的理论框架。

Method: 通过构造修改广义相对论（MGR），利用与联络无关的对称张量$\varPhi_{\alpha\beta}$来描述局域引力能量-动量，将其整合进爱因斯坦方程的总能量-动量张量。暗能量对应新张量的能量分量$\varPhi_{00}$，暗物质对应应力分量$\varPhi_{ij}$，并从星系或星系团旋转曲线外部区域暗能量与牛顿力之间的平衡以及内部区域暗区势力之间的平衡出发，直接推导RAR的物理起源。

Result: 研究表明MGR囊括了MOND理论，能够解释宽双星系统中观测到的引力异常，同时在宇宙学大尺度上重现牛顿引力行为，并且从暗能量与暗区势力之间的平衡直接导出了RAR，从而为星系动力学中的这一普适关系提供了统一的理论基础。

Conclusion: MGR作为唯一能通过几何方式统一描述引力与暗区的度量相容引力理论，为RAR提供了自然的物理起源解释，并因暗物质本质上与时空的洛伦兹度量相关联而极为难以观测，从而为理解星系动力学与暗区的本质提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: The radial acceleration relation (RAR) is a tight correlation between the distribution of baryonic matter in galaxies and their observed dynamics, even in systems where dark matter dominates. This suggests a universal acceleration scale exists in galaxies, which appears to challenge the $Λ$CDM standard cosmological model and favor Modified Newtonian dynamics (MOND). However, MOND also deviates from the RAR, so these deviations are not fully explained by the $Λ$CDM model or MOND. A theory that explains gravity and the dark sector geometrically, and treats dark matter as a spin-1 particle, is required, such as Modified General Relativity (MGR). It is the only metric-compatible theory of gravity that describes local gravitational energy-momentum by the connection-independent symmetric tensor $\varPhi_{αβ}$, which completes the total energy-momentum tensor of the Einstein equation. Dark energy is explained by the energy-related component $\varPhi_{00}$, and dark matter by the stress components $\varPhi_{ij}$. Dark matter is extremely difficult to observe because it is fundamentally related to the Lorentzian metric of spacetime itself. The physical origin of the RAR follows directly from the equilibrium between the dark energy and Newtonian forces in the outer part of the rotation curve of galaxies or galaxy clusters, and that between the dark sector forces in the inner part of the rotation curve. It is shown that MGR subsumes MOND, accounts for the gravitational anomaly observed in wide binaries, and exhibits the observed Newtonian behavior of gravity at large scales.

</details>


### [172] [Reconstruction of f(Q,T) Gravity from Logarithmically Corrected Ricci-Gauss-Bonnet Holographic Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19252)
*Preeti Joshi,Ertan Gudekli,Antonio Pasqua,Surajit Chattopadhyay*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个f(Q,T)修正引力框架的宇宙学与热力学行为，构建了基于Nojiri-Odintsov方案的广义Ricci-Gauss-Bonnet全息暗能量模型，并通过观测数据约束背景演化，最终在表观视界上验证了背景描述与重构有效流体描述下熵演化的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索f(Q,T)修正引力框架下暗能量（dark energy）的有效动力学与经典稳定性，并建立广义全息暗能量（holographic dark energy）模型与该引力框架的对应关系，以提供一种统一的宇宙学与热力学描述框架。

Method: 将引力拉格朗日量写为f(Q,T)=f(Q)+λT的形式，在有效暗能量扇区中构建修正Friedmann方程；采用唯象多项式形式的f(Q)导出态方程与声速平方；利用32个宇宙计时器（Cosmic Chronometer）观测数据约束幂律背景；基于Nojiri-Odintsov方案构造含对数熵修正的广义Ricci-Gauss-Bonnet全息暗能量模型，并与f(Q,T)框架的有效暗能量密度建立对应关系，最后在表观视界（apparent horizon）处结合Gibbs关系研究热力学行为。

Result: 重构得到的f(Q,T)模型在考虑的红移范围内有效暗能量表现出经典稳定性；从紧凑背景描述获得的熵演化与从重构有效流体表述得到的熵演化高度一致，仅存在较小的数值残差。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个一致框架，用于考察广义全息暗能量、重构f(Q,T)引力与宇宙热力学之间的联系，验证了不同描述途径下熵演化的自洽性。

Abstract: The current paper reports an investigation on the cosmological and thermodynamic behaviour of an $f(Q,T)$ modified-gravity framework in which the gravitational Lagrangian is written as $f(Q,T)=f(Q)+λT$, with $Q$ denoting the non-metricity scalar and $T$ the trace of the energy-momentum tensor. The modified Friedmann equations are formulated in terms of an effective Dark Energy sector, and the corresponding equation of state and squared speed of sound are examined for a phenomenological polynomial form of $f(Q)$. A power-law background is constrained using 32 Cosmic Chronometer measurements. The resulting background is then used to study the effective Dark Energy dynamics and its classical stability in the redshift range considered. In this study, we further construct a generalized Ricci-Gauss-Bonnet holographic Dark Energy model with logarithmic entropy corrections based on the Nojiri-Odintsov prescription and establish a correspondence between its energy density and the effective Dark Energy density of the $f(Q,T)$ framework. The full function $f(Q,T)$ is then given by adding the trace contribution $λT$. Finally, the thermodynamic behaviour of the reconstructed model is studied at the apparent horizon with the Nojiri-Odintsov entropy motivated by \textit{Phys. Rev. D} \textbf{105}, 044042 (2022) and the Gibbs relation. The entropy evolution obtained from the compact background description is also found to be consistent with that obtained from the reconstructed effective-fluid formulation, with only small numerical residuals. These results provide a consistent framework for examining the connection between generalized holographic dark energy, reconstructed $f(Q,T)$ gravity, and cosmic thermodynamics.

</details>


### [173] [Bayesian constraints on quadratic $f(Q)$ gravity from the ADM mass of white dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19257)
*Edson Otoniel,Lucas A. Sousa,Jonathan A. Rebouças,Juan M. Z. Prete*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在二次对称远距平行引力（quadratic symmetric teleparallel gravity）f(Q)=Q+αQ²框架下计算白矮星（white dwarf）静态平衡序列，并利用质量-半径观测数据对负耦合区间、广义相对论（GR）与正耦合区间进行贝叶斯比较。结果显示当前数据仅能提供对耦合常数α的条件约束，并无直接证据支持修正引力，负耦合区间可产生高达14.34倍太阳质量的白矮星配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用白矮星观测检验修正引力理论，特别是对称远距平行引力的f(Q)模型。作者希望明确在不同耦合符号下白矮星的静态平衡序列特征，并通过贝叶斯统计比较来判断现有天文数据能否为修正引力提供证据。

Method: 通过施加仿射场方程（affine field equation）并选择解析分支C=r exp(B/2)获得规则内部解，与渐近平坦外部时空匹配以确定ADM质量。物质部分采用冷碳-12状态方程，包含相对论电子简并、核静止能和库仑晶格修正。在固定α下以对数均匀先验对中心密度进行边际化，序列覆盖10¹⁴≤|α|/cm²≤10²¹。

Result: 正耦合将致密分支移向更大半径和更低质量，而负耦合产生高质量延伸，其中包含一个ADM质量为14.34倍太阳质量的可允许平衡配置。利用5颗白矮星（Sirius B、QS Vir、V471 Tau B、ZTF J1901+1458、LHS 4033）的观测数据，得到条件后验中位数α₋=-1.12×10¹⁶ cm²和α₊=1.85×10¹⁷ cm²。在等先验权重下，负耦合、GR和正耦合扇区的后验概率分别为0.228、0.378和0.395，ln(B₊/GR)=0.043。

Conclusion: 当前观测数据对耦合常数α提供了条件约束，但后验概率表明GR仍是最受支持的模型之一（后验概率0.378，与正耦合扇区0.395相当），不存在直接证据支持修正引力。作者强调在利用白矮星观测约束该理论时，必须一致地定义ADM质量处方（ADM mass prescription）和可允许恒星域。

Abstract: We calculate static equilibrium sequences for white dwarfs (WDs) in quadratic symmetric teleparallel gravity, f(Q)=Q+alpha Q^2, and perform a Bayesian comparison between the negative-coupling sector, general relativity (GR), and the positive-coupling sector using mass-radius measurements. Regular interior solutions are obtained by imposing the affine field equation and selecting the analytic branch C=r exp(B/2), and are matched to asymptotically flat exterior spacetimes to determine the ADM mass. The matter sector is described by a cold carbon-12 equation of state including relativistic electron degeneracy, nuclear rest energy, and Coulomb lattice corrections. Central density is marginalized at fixed alpha with a log-uniform prior. The sequences span 10^14 <= |alpha|/cm^2 <= 10^21. Positive couplings shift the compact branch toward larger radii and lower masses, whereas negative couplings produce high-mass extensions, including an admissible equilibrium configuration with M_ADM=14.34 solar masses. Using Sirius B, QS Vir, V471 Tau B, ZTF J1901+1458, and LHS 4033, we obtain conditional posterior medians alpha_-= -1.12x10^16 cm^2 and alpha_+=1.85x10^17 cm^2. With equal prior weights, the posterior probabilities for the negative, GR, and positive sectors are 0.228, 0.378, and 0.395, respectively, with ln(B_+/GR)=0.043. Current data therefore provide conditional constraints on the coupling but no direct evidence for modified gravity, emphasizing that the ADM mass prescription and admissible stellar domain must be consistently defined when using WD observations to constrain the theory.

</details>


### [174] [On the Tidal and Frame-Drag Fields in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19260)
*Rahulkumar Solanki*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用（1+3）协变时空分解，在存在物质的情况下，将潮汐场和框架拖曳场定义为带有惯性陀螺仪的类时观察者族的约束方程，并分析了广义相对论中观察者相对运动、陀螺进动与牛顿引力之间的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知在真空中，自由下落的两个无限小分离的类时观察者会因潮汐场（Weyl张量的“电”部分）产生相对加速度，因框架拖曳场（“磁”部分）产生陀螺相对进动。本文旨在将这些场推广到存在物质的时空情形下，形式化地定义并分析其约束方程及其观测效应。

Method: 采用（1+3）协变分裂（(1+3) covariant splitting）方法，对携带惯性陀螺仪并形成一致族（congruence）的类时观察者，在存在物质的情况下将潮汐场和框架拖曳场定义为约束方程，并沿闭合空间轮廓计算观察者相对速度与陀螺自旋相对取向的累积变化。

Result: 研究发现：在闭合轮廓上，观察者相对速度因框架拖曳场和流体的动量密度而不为零；陀螺自旋的相对取向因潮汐场、流体变量（各向异性压强和能量密度）以及分离向量的空间叉积而不完全抵消；此外，即使不存在框架拖曳场，当相对速度和加速度非平行时，观察者间也会因微分Thomas进动（Thomas precession）观测到陀螺相对进动。

Conclusion: 框架拖曳场在牛顿引力中没有对应物，其效应连同流体动力学变量共同构成了广义相对论中区别于牛顿引力的可观测特征；即使无框架拖曳场，微分Thomas进动也能独立导致相邻观察者间的陀螺相对进动。

Abstract: It is known that in vacuum, two infinitesimally separated timelike observers under free fall notice a relative acceleration due to the tidal field (or the `electric' part of the Weyl tensor), and if they are carrying inertial guidance gyroscopes, they notice relative precession between these gyroscopes due to the frame-drag field (or the `magnetic' part of the Weyl tensor). The frame-drag field thus has no Newtonian analog. In this paper, for a family of timelike observers carrying inertial gyroscopes and forming a congruence, we define these fields as constraint equations in the presence of matter using (1+3) covariant splitting of spacetime. Unlike Newtonian gravity, the relative velocities of the observers around a closed contour formed by their spatial separations (and a temporal vector that closes this contour if the congruence is not hypersurface orthogonal) do not add up to zero due to the frame-drag field and momentum density of the fluid. Moreover, the relative orientations of the gyroscope spins around the same contour do not generally cancel out due to the tidal field, the fluid variables (anisotropic pressure and energy density), and the spatial cross-product of relative velocities associated with the separation vectors that form the contour. Additionally, it is shown that even in the absence of the frame-drag field, neighboring observers see relative precession between their gyroscopes when their relative velocity and acceleration are non-parallel due to differential Thomas precession.

</details>


### [175] [Relational Wigner-Smith Duration in Wheeler-DeWitt Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19261)
*Daulet Berkimbayev*

Main category: gr-qc
